印度尼西亚分散乙型肝炎病毒载量检测的开放系统实时定量 PCR 测定的开发和性能评估

Development and Performance Evaluation of an Open-System Real-Time Quantitative PCR Assay for Decentralized Hepatitis B Viral Load Testing in Indonesia
作者： Rino Alvani Gani, Kemal Fariz Kalista, Siti Fatimah Selasih, Wisnu Aji Kuncoro, Dini Budhiarko, Muhammad Yunus, Anugrah Dwi Handayu, Dela Intan Permatasari, Akterono Dwi Budiyati
摘要中文翻译摘要 背景 印度尼西亚仍然是东南亚 慢性乙型肝炎 (CHB) 负担最高的国家之一。尽管疾病负担巨大,但在获得定量 乙型肝炎病毒 (HBV) DNA 检测方面存在重大差距,可能会延迟 CHB 的 诊断 和 治疗。本研究旨在开发和验证开放系统定量实时 PCR (qRT-PCR) 测定法,即 INDIGEN HBV DNA qRT-PCR,用于检测和定量 HBV DNA 病毒载量。方法 该检测旨在针对 HBV S 区,并根据 HBV 世界卫生组织 (WHO) 国际标准进行校准。评估了分析性能的线性和检测限 (LoD)。与 Xpert® HBV 病毒载量 (Xpert) 相比,总共使用了 122 份来自 CHB 患者的 HBsAg 阳性样本来评估我们检测的临床性能。结果 该测定在 1.0–9.0 log10 IU/mL (R2 = 0.9964) 范围内表现出出色的线性,LoD 为 14.8 IU/mL。与 Xpert 相比,所开发的检测方法显示出 100% 的特异性和 99.05% 的灵敏度,并且基于戴明回归分析具有很强的相关性。结论 INDIGEN HBV DNA qRT-PCR 检测显示出与 Xpert 相当的稳健评估性能,支持其作为可靠的开放系统检测在印度尼西亚实施分散式 HBV 病毒载量检测。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
印度尼西亚仍然是东南亚 慢性乙型肝炎 (CHB) 负担最高的国家之一。尽管疾病负担巨大,但在获得定量 乙型肝炎病毒 (HBV) DNA 检测方面存在重大差距,可能会延迟 CHB 的 诊断 和 治疗。
设计与对象
本研究旨在开发和验证开放系统定量实时 PCR (qRT-PCR) 测定法,即 INDIGEN HBV DNA qRT-PCR,用于检测和定量 HBV DNA 病毒载量。与 Xpert® HBV 病毒载量 (Xpert) 相比,总共使用了 122 份来自 CHB 患者的 HBsAg 阳性样本来评估我们检测的临床性能。
核心方法
该检测旨在针对 HBV 的 S 区,并根据 HBV 世界卫生组织 (WHO) 国际标准进行校准。评估了分析性能的线性和检测限 (LoD)。
主要结果
该测定在 1.0–9.0 log10 IU/mL (R2 = 0.9964) 范围内表现出出色的线性,LoD 为 14.8 IU/mL。与 Xpert 相比,所开发的检测方法显示出 100% 的特异性和 99.05% 的灵敏度,并且基于戴明回归分析具有很强的相关性。
解释与创新
INDIGEN HBV DNA qRT-PCR 检测显示出与 Xpert 相当的强大评估性能,支持其作为可靠的开放系统检测在印度尼西亚实施,用于分散式 HBV 病毒载量检测。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果支持实施开放系统 qRT-PCR 检测来进行分散的 HBV 病毒载量检测,这可以改善资源有限环境中获得诊断服务的机会。
局限与证据强度
该研究仅限于摘要评估,证据基于单一分析性能评估。由于报告的强相关性以及高敏感性和特异性,研究结果的可信度很高。

Development and Performance Evaluation of an Open-System Real-Time Quantitative PCR Assay for Decentralized Hepatitis B Viral Load Testing in Indonesia

Authors: Rino Alvani Gani, Kemal Fariz Kalista, Siti Fatimah Selasih, Wisnu Aji Kuncoro, Dini Budhiarko, Muhammad Yunus, Anugrah Dwi Handayu, Dela Intan Permatasari, Akterono Dwi Budiyati
Original abstract (English)
Abstract Background Indonesia remains one of the countries with the highest burdens of chronic hepatitis B (CHB) in Southeast Asia. Despite this substantial disease burden, a major gap in access to quantitative hepatitis B virus (HBV) DNA testing potentially delays the diagnosis and treatment of CHB. This study aimed to develop and validate an open-system quantitative real-time PCR (qRT-PCR) assay, the INDIGEN HBV DNA qRT-PCR for detecting and quantifying HBV DNA viral load. Methods The assay was designed to target the S region of HBV and calibrated against the HBV World Health Organization (WHO) International Standard. Analytical performance was evaluated for linearity and limit of detection (LoD). A total of 122 HBsAg-positive samples from CHB patients were used to assess the clinical performance of our assay compared to the Xpert® HBV Viral Load (Xpert). Results The assay demonstrated excellent linearity across 1.0–9.0 log10 IU/mL (R2 = 0.9964) with a LoD of 14.8 IU/mL. Compared to Xpert, the developed assay showed 100% specificity and 99.05% sensitivity with strong correlation based on Deming regression analysis. Conclusions The INDIGEN HBV DNA qRT-PCR assay demonstrated robust evaluation performance comparable to the Xpert, supporting its implementation as a reliable open-system assay for decentralized HBV viral load testing in Indonesia.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Indonesia remains one of the countries with the highest burdens of chronic hepatitis B (CHB) in Southeast Asia. Despite this substantial disease burden, a major gap in access to quantitative hepatitis B virus (HBV) DNA testing potentially delays the diagnosis and treatment of CHB.
Design and population
This study aimed to develop and validate an open-system quantitative real-time PCR (qRT-PCR) assay, the INDIGEN HBV DNA qRT-PCR for detecting and quantifying HBV DNA viral load. A total of 122 HBsAg-positive samples from CHB patients were used to assess the clinical performance of our assay compared to the Xpert® HBV Viral Load (Xpert).
Core methods
The assay was designed to target the S region of HBV and calibrated against the HBV World Health Organization (WHO) International Standard. Analytical performance was evaluated for linearity and limit of detection (LoD).
Main results
The assay demonstrated excellent linearity across 1.0–9.0 log10 IU/mL (R2 = 0.9964) with a LoD of 14.8 IU/mL. Compared to Xpert, the developed assay showed 100% specificity and 99.05% sensitivity with strong correlation based on Deming regression analysis.
Interpretation and novelty
The INDIGEN HBV DNA qRT-PCR assay demonstrated robust evaluation performance comparable to the Xpert, supporting its implementation as a reliable open-system assay for decentralized HBV viral load testing in Indonesia.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings support the implementation of an open-system qRT-PCR assay for decentralized HBV viral load testing, which can improve access to diagnostic services in resource-limited settings.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited to an abstract-only evaluation, and the evidence is based on a single assay performance assessment. The confidence in the findings is high due to the strong correlation and high sensitivity and specificity reported.

在公开的 乙型肝炎病毒 序列中,乙型肝炎病毒 突变 的频率与 功能性治愈 现有和新兴药物的耐药性相关

Frequency of hepatitis B virus mutations associated with resistance to current and emerging drugs for functional cure in publicly available hepatitis B virus sequences
作者： Gnanesvari Anandasaravanan, Sarah Sepehriamin, Johan Ringlander, Navaneethan Palanisamy, Johan Lennerstrand
摘要中文翻译背景 乙型肝炎病毒 (HBV) 仍然是一个巨大的全球健康挑战。尽管有 抗病毒药物 疗法,但尚不存在治愈性的 治疗。只有一小部分患有 慢性乙型肝炎 (CHB) 的个体达到了 功能性治愈。耐药性和 疫苗 逃逸 突变 (EM) 可能会损害当前的核苷(酸)类似物、疫苗 和新兴疗法,导致 治疗 失败。本研究旨在评估超过 17,000 个公开的患者来源的 HBV 序列中这些 HBV 突变 的频率。方法从文献检索中检索倍数变化(FC)数据和与抗性/逃逸相关的突变。HBV 序列取自 NCBI 核苷酸和 NCBI 病毒数据库。使用 HBVdb 和斯坦福大学 HBVseq 工具以及 GenBank 数据对人类 HBV 分离株进行基因分型。使用 MAFFT v7 生成基因型特异性比对,并使用内部 Python 脚本跨逆转录酶 (RT)、核心 (C)、表面 (S) 和 X 区域分析 突变。结果 从检索到的 19,192 个序列标识符中,分析了 17,012 个基因分型序列。基因型 C、B、D 和 A 约占数据集的 85%。RT-突变、L180M + M204V 和 M204I 分别在 A、G、H、AG 和 G 等某些基因型中常见。C-突变、Y38F、I105T 和 T109M 在常见基因型中被鉴定。在 S 蛋白中,G145R、E164G 和 E164D 突变 是最常见的,并且已知这些 突变 会改变单克隆抗体 (mAb) 的有效性。一般来说,针对所研究的单克隆抗体的 S-突变 发生频率为低至中等。先前认为与 疫苗 逃逸相关的 突变 的一些替换非常普遍,然而,这些替换可能是特定于某些基因型的多态性,需要进一步验证。总体而言,与基于反义寡核苷酸 (ASO) 的 治疗 相关的 X 区 突变 并不常见。结论 具有耐药性的 HBV 分离株和 疫苗 逃逸相关的 突变 在全球范围内存在,并且可能具有基因型依赖性。因此,在开始治疗以获得良好结果之前,基因型和耐药性评估至关重要,特别是在引入新药以实现 功能性治愈 时。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
乙型肝炎病毒 (HBV) 仍然是一个巨大的全球健康挑战。尽管有 抗病毒药物 疗法,但尚不存在治愈性的 治疗。
设计与对象
HBV 序列取自 NCBI 核苷酸和 NCBI 病毒数据库。从检索到的 19,192 个序列标识符中,分析了 17,012 个基因分型序列。基因型 C、B、D 和 A 约占数据集的 85%。
核心方法
使用 HBVdb 和斯坦福大学 HBVseq 工具以及 GenBank 数据对 HBV 分离株进行基因分型。使用 MAFFT v7 生成基因型特异性比对,并使用内部 Python 脚本跨逆转录酶 (RT)、核心 (C)、表面 (S) 和 X 区域分析 突变。
主要结果
RT-突变、L180M + M204V 和 M204I 分别在 A、G、H、AG 和 G 等某些基因型中常见。C-突变、Y38F、I105T 和 T109M 在常见基因型中被鉴定。在 S 蛋白中,G145R、E164G 和 E164D 突变 是最常见的,并且已知这些 突变 会改变单克隆抗体 (mAb) 的有效性。先前认为与 疫苗 逃逸相关的 突变 的一些替换非常普遍,然而,这些替换可能是特定于某些基因型的多态性,需要进一步验证。总体而言,与基于反义寡核苷酸 (ASO) 的 治疗 相关的 X 区 突变 并不常见。
解释与创新
具有耐药性的 HBV 分离株和与 疫苗 逃逸相关的 突变 在全球范围内存在,并且可能具有基因型依赖性。
科研与公卫意义
在开始治疗以获得良好的患者预后之前,基因型和耐药性评估至关重要,特别是在引入新药以实现 功能性治愈 时。
局限与证据强度
所提供的证据并未报告研究设计的具体局限性。

Frequency of hepatitis B virus mutations associated with resistance to current and emerging drugs for functional cure in publicly available hepatitis B virus sequences

Authors: Gnanesvari Anandasaravanan, Sarah Sepehriamin, Johan Ringlander, Navaneethan Palanisamy, Johan Lennerstrand
Original abstract (English)
Background Hepatitis B virus (HBV) remains an immense global health challenge. Despite the availability of antiviral therapies, no curative treatment exists. Only a small proportion of individuals with chronic hepatitis B (CHB) achieve a functional cure. Resistance and vaccine escape mutations (EMs) may compromise current nucleos(t)ide analogs, vaccines and emerging therapies, leading to treatment failure. The present study aimed to assess the frequency of these HBV mutations in >17,000 publicly available patient-derived HBV sequences. Methods Fold-change (FC) data and resistance/escape-associated mutations were retrieved from literature searches. HBV sequences were retrieved from the NCBI Nucleotide and NCBI Virus databases. Human HBV isolates were genotyped using the HBVdb and Stanford HBVseq tools and GenBank data. Genotype-specific alignments were generated using MAFFT v7 and mutations were analyzed across reverse transcriptase (RT), core (C), surface (S) and X regions using in-house Python scripts. Results From 19,192 sequence identifiers retrieved, 17,012 genotyped sequences were analyzed. Genotypes C, B, D and A comprised approximately 85% of the dataset. RT-mutations, L180M + M204V and M204I, were frequent in certain genotypes like A, G, H, AG, and G, respectively. C-mutations, Y38F, I105T, and T109M, were identified in common genotypes. In the S-protein, G145R, E164G, and E164D mutations were the most common and these mutations are known to alter the effectiveness of monoclonal antibodies (mAbs). In general, S-mutations toward the studied mAbs occurred at low-to-moderate frequency. Some substitutions previously suggested to be vaccine escape-associated mutations were highly prevalent, however, these substitutions may be polymorphisms specific to certain genotypes requiring further validation. X-region mutations relevant for treatment based on antisense oligonucleotides (ASOs) were uncommon overall. Conclusion HBV isolates with resistance and vaccine escape-associated mutations are present globally and might be genotype-dependent. Therefore, genotype and resistance assessment are crucial before initiating therapy for a favorable outcome in patients, especially when new drugs are introduced to achieve functional cure.
View research seven-element analysis

Hepatitis B virus (HBV) remains an immense global health challenge. Despite the availability of antiviral therapies, no curative treatment exists.

RT-mutations, L180M + M204V and M204I, were frequent in certain genotypes like A, G, H, AG, and G, respectively. C-mutations, Y38F, I105T, and T109M, were identified in common genotypes. In the S-protein, G145R, E164G, and E164D mutations were the most common and these mutations are known to alter the effectiveness of monoclonal antibodies (mAbs). Some substitutions previously suggested to be vaccine escape-associated mutations were highly prevalent, however, these substitutions may be polymorphisms specific to certain genotypes requiring further validation. X-region mutations relevant for treatment based on antisense oligonucleotides (ASOs) were uncommon overall.
Interpretation and novelty

Scientific and public-health significance
Genotype and resistance assessment are crucial before initiating therapy for a favorable outcome in patients, especially when new drugs are introduced to achieve functional cure.
Limitations and evidence strength
The supplied evidence does not report specific limitations of the study design.

乙型肝炎剪接生成的蛋白质可防止肿瘤发生,但在已形成的肝癌中形成促肿瘤免疫微环境。

Hepatitis B splicing-generated protein prevents tumor initiation but shapes a pro-tumor immune microenvironment in established HCC.
作者： Bouchra Lekbaby, Jules Sotty, Hoan Nguyen Dang, Jesus Emanuel Brunetti, Pierre Bablon, Céline Goy, Martin Larsen, Anne-Marie Cassard, Gabriel Perlemuter, Gwennhael Autret
摘要中文翻译背景和目的:乙型肝炎病毒 (HBV) 通过直接致癌和间接免疫介导机制在肝细胞癌 (HCC) 中发挥关键作用。在 HCC 发病前,慢性携带者中主要 HBV 剪接转录物 sp1RNA 的表达逐渐增加。然而,sp1RNA 和相关乙型肝炎剪接生成蛋白 (HBSP) 对肝癌发生和肿瘤微环境的贡献尚未得到研究。方法:根据 HBV 表达 (RT)-qPCR 对 57 名患者的 HBV 相关 HCC 肿瘤进行分类,并使用 Nanostring 免疫肿瘤学面板进行分析。在遭受二乙基亚硝胺 (DEN) 诱导的 HCC 的转基因雄性小鼠 (TgHBSP) 中评估了 HBSP 表达的功能影响。开发了使用 HBSP 表达细胞的原位同种异体移植的第二种模型。DEN 注射后 48 小时、6 个月和 10 个月对肝组织和免疫浸润进行了表征。结果:在 HBV 相关 HCC 患者中,sp1RNA 表达与较差的总体生存率和肿瘤内单核细胞/巨噬细胞基因特征减少相关。在 TgHBSP 小鼠中,DEN 给药后 6 个月和 10 个月时,肿瘤 发病率 均减少,这与癌症起始阶段 p38-MAPK 的持续激活有关。尽管 发病率 肿瘤较小,但 TgHBSP 小鼠中产生的肿瘤在终点时是两倍大。炎症信号减弱和巨噬细胞向促肿瘤表型极化与 HBSP 在早期 HCC 中的促肿瘤作用有关。结论:本研究揭示了 HBSP 在肝癌发生中的双重、阶段依赖性作用,首先在肝癌起始阶段发挥与 p38-MAPK 激活相关的抗肿瘤作用,然后对肿瘤生长和已建立的 HCC 免疫微环境的改变产生促肿瘤作用。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
乙型肝炎病毒 (HBV) 通过直接致癌和间接免疫介导机制在肝细胞癌 (HCC) 中发挥关键作用。
设计与对象
在 HCC 发病前,慢性携带者中主要 HBV 剪接转录物 sp1RNA 的表达逐渐增加。然而,sp1RNA 和相关乙型肝炎剪接生成蛋白 (HBSP) 对肝癌发生和肿瘤微环境的贡献尚未得到研究。
核心方法
根据 HBV 表达 (RT)-qPCR 对 57 名患者的 HBV 相关 HCC 肿瘤进行分类,并使用 Nanostring 免疫肿瘤学面板进行分析。在遭受二乙基亚硝胺 (DEN) 诱导的 HCC 的转基因雄性小鼠 (TgHBSP) 中评估了 HBSP 表达的功能影响。开发了使用 HBSP 表达细胞的原位同种异体移植的第二种模型。
主要结果
在 HBV 相关 HCC 患者中,sp1RNA 表达与肿瘤内总生存率较差和单核细胞/巨噬细胞基因特征减少相关。在 TgHBSP 小鼠中,DEN 给药后 6 个月和 10 个月时,肿瘤 发病率 均减少,这与癌症起始阶段 p38-MAPK 的持续激活有关。尽管 发病率 肿瘤较小,但 TgHBSP 小鼠中产生的肿瘤在终点时是两倍大。
解释与创新
这项研究揭示了 HBSP 在肝癌发生中的双重、阶段依赖性作用,首先在肝癌起始阶段发挥与 p38-MAPK 激活相关的抗肿瘤作用,然后对肿瘤生长和已建立的 HCC 免疫微环境的改变产生促肿瘤作用。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果表明,HBSP 可能对了解 HBV 相关 HCC 的进展具有重要意义,并可为针对已形成肿瘤中的免疫微环境的策略提供信息。
局限与证据强度
该研究因其依赖患者样本和动物模型而受到限制,可能无法完全捕捉人类疾病的复杂性。由于使用观察和实验模型而没有长期随访,证据是中等的。

Hepatitis B splicing-generated protein prevents tumor initiation but shapes a pro-tumor immune microenvironment in established HCC.

Authors: Bouchra Lekbaby, Jules Sotty, Hoan Nguyen Dang, Jesus Emanuel Brunetti, Pierre Bablon, Céline Goy, Martin Larsen, Anne-Marie Cassard, Gabriel Perlemuter, Gwennhael Autret
Original abstract (English)
BACKGROUND AND AIMS: Hepatitis B virus (HBV) plays a key role in hepatocellular carcinoma (HCC) through both direct oncogenic and indirect immune-mediated mechanisms. Expression of the main HBV spliced transcript, sp1RNA, gradually increases in chronic carriers prior to HCC onset. However, sp1RNA and related Hepatitis B Splicing-generated Protein (HBSP) contributions to hepatic carcinogenesis and tumor microenvironment have not been investigated. METHODS: HBV-related HCC tumors from 57 patients were classified according to HBV expression (RT)-qPCR and profiled using Nanostring immuno-oncology panel. The functional impact of HBSP expression was evaluated in transgenic male mice (TgHBSP) subjected to diethylnitrosamine (DEN)-induced HCC. A second model using orthotopic allografts of HBSP-expressing cells was developed. Liver tissues and immune infiltrates were characterized at 48 h, 6- and 10-month post-DEN injection. RESULTS: In HBV-related HCC patients, sp1RNA expression was associated with worse overall survival and reduced monocyte/macrophage gene signature within tumors. In TgHBSP mice, tumor incidence was reduced at both 6- and 10-month post-DEN administration, associated with sustained p38-MAPK activation at the initiation stage of cancer. Despite lower tumor incidence, tumors arising in TgHBSP mice were twice as large at endpoint. Attenuated inflammatory signaling and macrophage polarization towards a pro-tumor phenotype were implicated in the pro-tumor effect of HBSP at early HCC. CONCLUSION: This study reveals a dual, stage-dependent, role for HBSP in liver carcinogenesis, exerting first anti-tumor effects associated with p38-MAPK activation at initiation stage of hepatic cancer, then pro-tumor effect on tumor growth and alterations in the immune microenvironment of established HCC.

Question and background
Hepatitis B virus (HBV) plays a key role in hepatocellular carcinoma (HCC) through both direct oncogenic and indirect immune-mediated mechanisms.
Design and population
Expression of the main HBV spliced transcript, sp1RNA, gradually increases in chronic carriers prior to HCC onset. However, sp1RNA and related Hepatitis B Splicing-generated Protein (HBSP) contributions to hepatic carcinogenesis and tumor microenvironment have not been investigated.
Core methods
HBV-related HCC tumors from 57 patients were classified according to HBV expression (RT)-qPCR and profiled using Nanostring immuno-oncology panel. The functional impact of HBSP expression was evaluated in transgenic male mice (TgHBSP) subjected to diethylnitrosamine (DEN)-induced HCC. A second model using orthotopic allografts of HBSP-expressing cells was developed.
Main results
In HBV-related HCC patients, sp1RNA expression was associated with worse overall survival and reduced monocyte/macrophage gene signature within tumors. In TgHBSP mice, tumor incidence was reduced at both 6- and 10-month post-DEN administration, associated with sustained p38-MAPK activation at the initiation stage of cancer. Despite lower tumor incidence, tumors arising in TgHBSP mice were twice as large at endpoint.
Interpretation and novelty
This study reveals a dual, stage-dependent, role for HBSP in liver carcinogenesis, exerting first anti-tumor effects associated with p38-MAPK activation at initiation stage of hepatic cancer, then pro-tumor effect on tumor growth and alterations in the immune microenvironment of established HCC.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings suggest that HBSP may have implications for understanding the progression of HBV-related HCC and could inform strategies for targeting the immune microenvironment in established tumors.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited by its reliance on patient samples and animal models, which may not fully capture human disease complexity. The evidence is moderate due to the use of observational and experimental models without long-term follow-up.

血清乙型肝炎表面抗原的轨迹预测 慢性乙型肝炎 治疗儿童的 HBeAg 清除率

Trajectories of serum hepatitis B surface antigen predict HBeAg clearance in treated children with chronic hepatitis B
作者： Wen-Xian Ouyang, Zhe Ji, Zhen-Zhen Yao, Song-Xu Peng
摘要中文翻译
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究旨在调查接受 抗病毒药物 治疗 治疗的 慢性乙型肝炎 儿童患者的 HBsAg 纵向轨迹模式及其与乙型肝炎 e 抗原 (HBeAg) 清除的关系。在 慢性乙型肝炎 (CHB) 患者中观察到乙型肝炎表面抗原 (HBsAg) 的早期动态与其随后的清除之间的关联。
设计与对象
对 402 名 HBeAg 阳性 慢性乙型肝炎 儿童患者进行了一项大型回顾性 队列研究 研究。在中位随访 19.03 个月期间,216 名参与者 (53.73%) 获得了 HBeAg 清除。
核心方法
应用潜在类别轨迹模型 (LCTM) 检查开始 抗病毒药物 治疗的前六个月内的 HBsAg 轨迹。Cox 比例风险模型用于评估 HBsAg 轨迹与 HBeAg 清除率之间的关联。
主要结果
LCTM revealed three distinct HBsAg trajectory patterns: ‘trajectory 1’ (sustained stability, n=43, 10.7%), ‘trajectory 2’ (slow decline, n=195, 48.5%), and ‘trajectory 3’ (rapid decline, n=164, 40.8%). Patients in trajectories 2 and 3 had significantly higher probabilities of HBeAg clearance compared to those in trajectory 1 (adjusted hazard ratio [aHR] = 3.99, 95% CI, 2.20-7.22 for trajectory 2; aHR = 7.21, 95% CI, 3.65-14.24 for trajectory 3).
解释与创新
该研究发现 抗病毒药物 治疗 儿童 CHB 患者 HBsAg 水平的纵向变化存在显着异质性。大约一半的患者表现出 HBsAg 大幅下降,这与 HBeAg 清除的可能性较高相关。
科研与公卫意义
这些发现可能通过识别与 HBeAg 清除相关的不同 HBsAg 轨迹模式,为 慢性乙型肝炎 儿科患者制定更个性化的 治疗 策略提供信息。
局限与证据强度
该研究基于回顾性设计,可能会引入偏差并限制建立因果关系的能力。由于研究的观察性质和潜在的混杂因素,证据是中等的。

Trajectories of serum hepatitis B surface antigen predict HBeAg clearance in treated children with chronic hepatitis B

Authors: Wen-Xian Ouyang, Zhe Ji, Zhen-Zhen Yao, Song-Xu Peng
Original abstract (English)
Background The association between the early dynamics of hepatitis B surface antigen (HBsAg) and its subsequent clearance has been observed in patients with chronic hepatitis B (CHB). However, its relationship with other clinical outcomes remains poorly understood. Objective This study aimed to investigate the longitudinal trajectories patterns of HBsAg and their associations with hepatitis B e antigen (HBeAg) clearance in pediatric patients with CHB undergoing antiviral treatment. Methods A large retrospective cohort study was conducted on 402 pediatric patients with HBeAg-positive CHB. Latent class trajectory models (LCTM) were applied to examine HBsAg trajectories within the first six months of initiating antiviral therapy. Cox proportional hazard models were used to assess the associations between HBsAg trajectories and HBeAg clearance. Results Among the 402 participants, the median age was 5 years, with 249 (61.94%) being male. During a median follow-up of 19.03 months, 216 participants (53.73%) achieved HBeAg clearance. LCTM revealed three distinct HBsAg trajectory patterns: ‘trajectory 1’ (sustained stability, n=43, 10.7%), ‘trajectory 2’ (slow decline, n=195, 48.5%), and ‘trajectory 3’ (rapid decline, n=164, 40.8%). Patients in trajectories 2 and 3 had significantly higher probabilities of HBeAg clearance compared to those in trajectory 1 (adjusted hazard ratio [aHR] = 3.99, 95% CI, 2.20-7.22 for trajectory 2; aHR = 7.21, 95% CI, 3.65-14.24 for trajectory 3). Conclusions There is significant heterogeneity in the longitudinal changes of HBsAg levels among pediatric CHB patients with antiviral treatment. Approximately half of the patients exhibited a substantial HBsAg decline, which was associated with a higher likelihood of HBeAg clearance.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to investigate the longitudinal trajectories patterns of HBsAg and their associations with hepatitis B e antigen (HBeAg) clearance in pediatric patients with chronic hepatitis B undergoing antiviral treatment. The association between the early dynamics of hepatitis B surface antigen (HBsAg) and its subsequent clearance has been observed in patients with chronic hepatitis B (CHB).
Design and population
A large retrospective cohort study was conducted on 402 pediatric patients with HBeAg-positive chronic hepatitis B. During a median follow-up of 19.03 months, 216 participants (53.73%) achieved HBeAg clearance.
Core methods
Latent class trajectory models (LCTM) were applied to examine HBsAg trajectories within the first six months of initiating antiviral therapy. Cox proportional hazard models were used to assess the associations between HBsAg trajectories and HBeAg clearance.
Main results
LCTM revealed three distinct HBsAg trajectory patterns: ‘trajectory 1’ (sustained stability, n=43, 10.7%), ‘trajectory 2’ (slow decline, n=195, 48.5%), and ‘trajectory 3’ (rapid decline, n=164, 40.8%). Patients in trajectories 2 and 3 had significantly higher probabilities of HBeAg clearance compared to those in trajectory 1 (adjusted hazard ratio [aHR] = 3.99, 95% CI, 2.20-7.22 for trajectory 2; aHR = 7.21, 95% CI, 3.65-14.24 for trajectory 3).
Interpretation and novelty
The study identified significant heterogeneity in the longitudinal changes of HBsAg levels among pediatric CHB patients with antiviral treatment. Approximately half of the patients exhibited a substantial HBsAg decline, which was associated with a higher likelihood of HBeAg clearance.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings may inform the development of more personalized treatment strategies for pediatric patients with chronic hepatitis B by identifying distinct HBsAg trajectory patterns linked to HBeAg clearance.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is based on a retrospective design, which may introduce bias and limit the ability to establish causality. The evidence is moderate due to the observational nature of the study and potential confounding factors.

新生儿 乙型肝炎病毒 (HBV) 疫苗接种 时代的出生和 HIV-1 和 HBV 合并感染者接受含替诺福韦抗逆转录病毒治疗的乙型肝炎表面抗原血清清除。

Birth in the Neonatal Hepatitis B Virus (HBV) Vaccination Era and Hepatitis B Surface Antigen Seroclearance in People With HIV-1 and HBV Coinfection on Tenofovir-containing Antiretroviral Therapy.
作者： Yu-Shan Huang, Hsin-Yun Sun, Kuan-Yin Lin, Sung-Hsi Huang, Wen-Chun Liu, Guan-Jhou Chen, Kai-Hsiang Chen, Li-Hsin Su, Yi-Ching Su, Sui-Yuan Chang
摘要中文翻译
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究旨在调查长期含替诺福韦的抗逆转录病毒治疗 (ART) 对 HIV-1 和 HBV 合并感染者乙型肝炎表面抗原 (HBsAg) 血清清除的影响,特别是在具有通用新生儿 疫苗接种 计划的 HBV 高流行地区。
设计与对象
对 2011 年至 2023 年间接受含替诺福韦 ART 的 HBsAg 阳性 HIV-1 患者 (PWH) 进行随访,直至 2025 年 12 月。该研究包括 415 名 HBV 合并感染的 PWH。
核心方法
收集人口统计学、HBV 血清学标志物、血浆 HBV DNA 和肝纤维化阶段。Cox 比例风险模型用于识别与 HBsAg 血清清除相关的因素。
主要结果
经过中位随访 9.4 年,415 名感染者中的 35 名(8.4%)在总共 3600 人年的含替诺福韦 ART 治疗期间实现了 HBsAg 血清清除,相当于每 100 人年 0.97 的 发病率 率。其中,37.1%(13/35)在血清清除时抗-HBs检测呈阳性。
解释与创新
作者解释说,新生儿 HBV 疫苗接种 时代的出生和较低的基线 HBsAg 水平是 HBsAg 血清清除的两个独立预测因素。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果表明,新生儿 HBV 疫苗接种 时代的出生和基线 HBsAg 水平可能为未来监测和管理 HIV-1 感染者 HBV 合并感染的策略提供信息。
局限与证据强度
该研究受到观察设计和潜在混杂因素的限制,但由于样本量大和随访期长,证据有力。

Birth in the Neonatal Hepatitis B Virus (HBV) Vaccination Era and Hepatitis B Surface Antigen Seroclearance in People With HIV-1 and HBV Coinfection on Tenofovir-containing Antiretroviral Therapy.

Authors: Yu-Shan Huang, Hsin-Yun Sun, Kuan-Yin Lin, Sung-Hsi Huang, Wen-Chun Liu, Guan-Jhou Chen, Kai-Hsiang Chen, Li-Hsin Su, Yi-Ching Su, Sui-Yuan Chang
Original abstract (English)
BACKGROUND: The impact of long-term tenofovir-containing antiretroviral therapy (ART) on hepatitis B surface antigen (HBsAg) seroclearance in people with HIV-1 (PWH) and HBV coinfection remains under-defined, particularly in HBV-hyperendemic regions with universal neonatal vaccination programs. METHODS: HBsAg-positive PWH receiving tenofovir-containing ART between 2011 and 2023 were followed until December 2025. Demographics, HBV serologic markers, plasma HBV DNA, and liver fibrosis stage were collected. Cox proportional hazards models were used to identify factors associated with HBsAg seroclearance. Clinical and hepatic outcomes were compared with seroclearance status. RESULTS: After a median follow-up of 9.4 years, 35 (8.4%) of 415 PWH achieved HBsAg seroclearance during a total of 3600 person-years of tenofovir-containing ART, corresponding to an incidence rate of 0.97 per 100 person-years. Among them, 37.1% (13/35) tested positive for anti-HBs at the time of seroclearance. In multivariable analysis, birth in universal neonatal HBV vaccination era (after July 1986) (adjusted hazard ratio [aHR] 13.56, 95% confidence interval [CI] 6.08-30.28, P < .001), baseline HBsAg ≤250 IU/mL (aHR 7.36, 95% CI 3.29-16.50, P < .001), and higher baseline ALT level (aHR 1.00 per 1-U/L increase, 95% CI 1.00-1.01, P < .001) were independently associated with HBsAg seroclearance. None of PWH who achieved HBsAg seroclearance developed seroreversion, cirrhosis, or hepatocellular carcinoma (HCC), whereas 6.6% and 2.1% of PWH without seroclearance developed cirrhosis and HCC, respectively. CONCLUSIONS: Despite long-term tenofovir-containing ART, HBsAg seroclearance was uncommon (0.97% annually) among PWH with HBV coinfection. Birth in the neonatal HBV vaccination era and lower baseline HBsAg level were 2 independent predictors of HBsAg seroclearance.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to investigate the impact of long-term tenofovir-containing antiretroviral therapy (ART) on hepatitis B surface antigen (HBsAg) seroclearance in people with HIV-1 and HBV coinfection, particularly in HBV-hyperendemic regions with universal neonatal vaccination programs.
Design and population
HBsAg-positive people with HIV-1 (PWH) receiving tenofovir-containing ART between 2011 and 2023 were followed until December 2025. The study included 415 PWH with HBV coinfection.
Core methods
Demographics, HBV serologic markers, plasma HBV DNA, and liver fibrosis stage were collected. Cox proportional hazards models were used to identify factors associated with HBsAg seroclearance.
Main results
After a median follow-up of 9.4 years, 35 (8.4%) of 415 PWH achieved HBsAg seroclearance during a total of 3600 person-years of tenofovir-containing ART, corresponding to an incidence rate of 0.97 per 100 person-years. Among them, 37.1% (13/35) tested positive for anti-HBs at the time of seroclearance.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpreted that birth in the neonatal HBV vaccination era and lower baseline HBsAg level were two independent predictors of HBsAg seroclearance.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings suggest that birth in the neonatal HBV vaccination era and baseline HBsAg levels may inform future strategies for monitoring and managing HBV coinfection in people with HIV-1.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited by its observational design and potential confounding factors, but the evidence is strong due to the large sample size and long follow-up period.

转化多重拉曼病理成像用于 HBV 相关肝细胞癌的精确 诊断。

Translational Multiplexed Raman Pathological Imaging for Precise Diagnosis of HBV-Associated Hepatocellular Carcinoma.
作者： Wei Zhu, Wen-Jing Xu, Xin-Ping Zhan, Jia-Cheng Chen, Yao Wang, Kai Dai, Ji-Ming Hu, Ai-Guo Shen, Jürgen Popp
摘要中文翻译多重振动成像在组织分子分析方面具有巨大潜力,但适合常规临床组织病理学的完全标准化工作流程仍然很少。在此,我们报告了一种使用单分散三键拉曼聚合物纳米颗粒(∼50 nm)的临床适用方法。该方法集成了一步标记和单次扫描采集,可实现肝细胞癌 (HCC) 组织中 5 种蛋白质标记物的高性能多重拉曼成像。针对 乙型肝炎病毒 (HBV) 相关 HCC,我们的生物标志物组合将病毒蛋白 HBx 与 4 个临床使用的指标相结合:AFP、GS、GPC3 和 CD34。我们进一步揭示了临床上关键的分离:几个高级别 HBV-HCC 标本表现出显着升高的致癌标志物,而 HBx 表达相对较低,这与 抗病毒药物 疗法抑制病毒蛋白产生而不逆转肿瘤侵袭性一致。利用生物沉默区域(1800-2800 cm-1)内三键标签产生的超窄、高分辨率信号,我们实现了高保真空间映射和定量分析。与依赖形态学观察、临床表现或单标志物检测的传统病理方法不同,该技术同时评估病毒病原学、肿瘤进展和转移潜力,有效降低病毒和致癌谱不一致患者的错误分类风险,并在约24小时内完成整个检测。这项研究建立了一个快速、可靠的组织病理学平台,整合了病原体识别和全面的癌症表征,展示了对癌症研究和临床诊断的巨大价值。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究旨在通过先进的分子成像技术解决 HBV 相关肝细胞癌精确 诊断 缺乏标准化工作流程的问题。
设计与对象
该研究引入了一种临床适用的方法,使用单分散三键拉曼聚合物纳米颗粒来分析 HBV 相关的肝细胞癌组织。
核心方法
该方法集成了一步标记和单次扫描采集,可对 HCC 组织中的 5 种蛋白质标记物进行高性能多重拉曼成像,重点关注 HBV 相关疾病。
主要结果
该研究表明,一些高级别 HBV-HCC 标本显示致癌标志物升高,而 HBx 表达相对较低,表明 抗病毒药物 疗法可以抑制病毒蛋白的产生,而不会逆转肿瘤的侵袭性。
解释与创新
作者将这些发现解释为揭示了病毒病因学和肿瘤进展之间临床上关键的分离,强调了他们的技术在更准确的 诊断 方面的潜力。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果表明,该技术可以提高 诊断 对 HBV 相关肝细胞癌的精确度,有助于更好的患者管理和 治疗 策略。
局限与证据强度
该研究仅限于摘要报告,证据本质上是一般性的,表明所得出的结论具有中等可信度。

Translational Multiplexed Raman Pathological Imaging for Precise Diagnosis of HBV-Associated Hepatocellular Carcinoma.

Authors: Wei Zhu, Wen-Jing Xu, Xin-Ping Zhan, Jia-Cheng Chen, Yao Wang, Kai Dai, Ji-Ming Hu, Ai-Guo Shen, Jürgen Popp
Original abstract (English)
Multiplexed vibrational imaging holds great potential for tissue molecular analysis, yet fully standardized workflows suitable for routine clinical histopathology remain scarce. Herein, we report a clinically applicable methodology using monodisperse triple-bond Raman polymer nanoparticles (∼50 nm). This approach integrates one-step labeling and single-scan acquisition to achieve high-performance multiplexed Raman imaging of 5 protein markers in hepatocellular carcinoma (HCC) tissues. Targeting hepatitis B virus (HBV)-associated HCC, our biomarker panel combines the viral protein HBx with 4 clinically used indicators: AFP, GS, GPC3, and CD34. We further reveal a clinically critical dissociation: several high-grade HBV-HCC specimens exhibit markedly elevated oncogenic markers with comparatively low HBx expression, consistent with antiviral therapy suppressing viral protein production without reversing tumor aggressiveness. Taking advantage of the ultranarrow, well-resolved signals generated by triple-bond tags within the biological silent region (1800-2800 cm-1), we achieve high-fidelity spatial mapping and quantitative analysis. Unlike conventional pathological methods relying on morphological observation, clinical manifestations, or single-marker detection, this technique simultaneously evaluates viral etiology, tumor progression, and metastatic potential, effectively reducing misclassification risk in patients with discordant viral and oncogenic profiles, and completes the entire assay within approximately 24 h. This study establishes a rapid and reliable histopathological platform that integrates pathogen identification and comprehensive cancer characterization, demonstrating substantial value for both cancer research and clinical diagnostics.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to address the lack of standardized workflows for precise diagnosis of HBV-associated hepatocellular carcinoma through advanced molecular imaging techniques.
Design and population
The research introduced a clinically applicable methodology using monodisperse triple-bond Raman polymer nanoparticles to analyze HBV-associated hepatocellular carcinoma tissues.
Core methods
The method integrates one-step labeling and single-scan acquisition to enable high-performance multiplexed Raman imaging of 5 protein markers in HCC tissues, with a focus on HBV-related disease.
Main results
The study demonstrated that several high-grade HBV-HCC specimens showed elevated oncogenic markers with comparatively low HBx expression, indicating antiviral therapy may suppress viral protein production without reversing tumor aggressiveness.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpreted these findings as revealing a clinically critical dissociation between viral etiology and tumor progression, highlighting the potential of their technique for more accurate diagnosis.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings suggest that this technique could improve the precision of diagnosis for HBV-associated hepatocellular carcinoma, aiding in better patient management and treatment strategies.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited to an abstract-only report, and the evidence is general in nature, suggesting a moderate confidence level in the conclusions drawn.

接受化疗的恶性肿瘤患者的 乙型肝炎病毒 再激活和 抗病毒药物 预防实践:真实世界回顾性研究

Hepatitis B Virus Reactivation and Antiviral Prophylaxis Practices in Patients with Malignancies Receiving Chemotherapy: A Real-World Retrospective Study
作者： Fatma Tuğba Çatan Erdekli, Hayrunnisa Albayrak, Pınar Tarkun, Umut Kefeli, Kazım Uygun, Devrim Çabuk
摘要中文翻译背景和目的:乙型肝炎病毒 (HBV) 再激活是接受化疗和免疫抑制治疗的患者潜在的危及生命的并发症。尽管进行 抗病毒药物 预防,有关重新激活的真实数据仍然有限。材料和方法:我们回顾性评估了 2013 年至 2017 年在科贾埃利大学医学院医院接受化疗的恶性肿瘤患者。分析了人口学特征、恶性肿瘤类型、病毒学标志物、实验室检查结果、化疗方案、抗病毒药物 预防策略和 HBV 再激活率。结果:本研究共纳入 294 名患者,其中 18 名(6.1%)经历了 HBV 再激活。血液系统恶性肿瘤患者(11.1%)的再激活明显高于实体瘤患者(3.2%)。骨髓移植与观察到的乙型肝炎病毒再激活频率较高相关。在接受 抗病毒药物 预防的患者亚组中观察到 HBV 再激活。在一些发生 HBV 再激活的患者中记录了 抗病毒药物 的延迟启动和 抗病毒药物 的不规则使用。结论:尽管采取了预防策略,但恶性肿瘤和强化免疫抑制治疗仍与 HBV 再激活风险增加相关。我们的研究结果还强调了化疗前 HBV 筛查、及时启动有效的 抗病毒药物 预防以及在高危人群中延长 治疗 后监测的重要性。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
乙型肝炎病毒 (HBV) 再激活是接受化疗和免疫抑制治疗的患者潜在的危及生命的并发症。该研究旨在评估接受化疗的恶性肿瘤患者的 患病率 和 HBV 再激活的危险因素。
设计与对象
这项回顾性研究评估了 2013 年至 2017 年间在科贾埃利大学医学院医院接受化疗的恶性肿瘤患者。该研究总共包括 294 名患者,重点关注那些经历过 HBV 再激活的患者。
核心方法
该研究回顾性分析了人口特征、恶性肿瘤类型、病毒学标志物、实验室检查结果、化疗方案、抗病毒药物 预防策略和 HBV 再激活率。从医疗记录中收集数据并审查相关临床和病毒学参数。
主要结果
这项研究总共纳入了 294 名患者,其中 18 名(6.1%)经历了 HBV 再激活。血液系统恶性肿瘤患者(11.1%)的再激活明显高于实体瘤患者(3.2%)。骨髓移植与观察到的乙型肝炎病毒再激活频率较高相关。在接受 抗病毒药物 预防的患者亚组中观察到 HBV 再激活。
解释与创新
作者解释说,尽管采取了预防策略,但恶性肿瘤和强化免疫抑制治疗仍与 HBV 再激活风险增加相关。这项研究强调了在高危人群中进行针对性 抗病毒药物 预防的必要性。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果强调了化疗前 HBV 筛查、及时启动有效的 抗病毒药物 预防以及在高危人群中延长 治疗 后监测的重要性。这些见解可以为临床实践提供参考,以降低恶性肿瘤患者乙型肝炎病毒再激活的风险。
局限与证据强度
该研究因其回顾性设计和潜在的数据缺失而受到限制。然而,证据是强有力的,因为它基于来自明确人群的真实数据,并包括特定的比率和风险因素。

Hepatitis B Virus Reactivation and Antiviral Prophylaxis Practices in Patients with Malignancies Receiving Chemotherapy: A Real-World Retrospective Study

Authors: Fatma Tuğba Çatan Erdekli, Hayrunnisa Albayrak, Pınar Tarkun, Umut Kefeli, Kazım Uygun, Devrim Çabuk
Original abstract (English)
Background and Objectives: Hepatitis B virus (HBV) reactivation is a potentially life-threatening complication in patients receiving chemotherapy and immunosuppressive therapy. Real-world data regarding reactivation despite antiviral prophylaxis remain limited. Materials and Methods: We retrospectively evaluated patients with malignancies who received chemotherapy at Kocaeli University Faculty of Medicine Hospital between 2013 and 2017. Demographic characteristics, malignancy type, virological markers, laboratory findings, chemotherapy regimens, antiviral prophylaxis strategies, and HBV reactivation rates were analyzed. Results: A total of 294 patients were included in this study, of whom 18 (6.1%) experienced HBV reactivation. Reactivation was significantly more common in patients with hematologic malignancies (11.1%) than in patients with solid tumors (3.2%). Bone marrow transplantation was associated with a higher observed frequency of HBV reactivation. HBV reactivation was observed in a subset of patients receiving antiviral prophylaxis. Delayed initiation of antiviral treatment and irregular antiviral use were documented in some patients who developed HBV reactivation. Conclusions: Malignancies and intensive immunosuppressive therapies were associated with an increased risk of HBV reactivation despite prophylactic strategies. The results of our study also highlight the importance of HBV screening before chemotherapy, timely initiation of potent antiviral prophylaxis, and extended post-treatment monitoring in high-risk populations.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Hepatitis B virus (HBV) reactivation is a potentially life-threatening complication in patients receiving chemotherapy and immunosuppressive therapy. The study aimed to evaluate the prevalence and risk factors of HBV reactivation in patients with malignancies undergoing chemotherapy.
Design and population
This retrospective study evaluated patients with malignancies who received chemotherapy at Kocaeli University Faculty of Medicine Hospital between 2013 and 2017. The study included a total of 294 patients, with a focus on those who experienced HBV reactivation.
Core methods
The study retrospectively analyzed demographic characteristics, malignancy type, virological markers, laboratory findings, chemotherapy regimens, antiviral prophylaxis strategies, and HBV reactivation rates. Data were collected from medical records and reviewed for relevant clinical and virological parameters.
Main results
A total of 294 patients were included in this study, of whom 18 (6.1%) experienced HBV reactivation. Reactivation was significantly more common in patients with hematologic malignancies (11.1%) than in patients with solid tumors (3.2%). Bone marrow transplantation was associated with a higher observed frequency of HBV reactivation. HBV reactivation was observed in a subset of patients receiving antiviral prophylaxis.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpreted that malignancies and intensive immunosuppressive therapies were associated with an increased risk of HBV reactivation despite prophylactic strategies. This study highlights the need for targeted antiviral prophylaxis in high-risk populations.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings emphasize the importance of HBV screening before chemotherapy, timely initiation of potent antiviral prophylaxis, and extended post-treatment monitoring in high-risk populations. These insights can inform clinical practices to reduce the risk of HBV reactivation in patients with malignancies.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited by its retrospective design and potential for missing data. However, the evidence is strong as it is based on real-world data from a well-defined population and includes specific rates and risk factors.

治疗 慢性乙型肝炎 病毒感染的患者的灾难性健康支出:来自尼日利亚拉菲亚联邦大学教学医院的证据

Catastrophic Health Expenditure among Patients Managing Chronic Hepatitis B Virus Infection: Evidence from Federal University Teaching Hospital, Lafia, Nigeria
作者： Authors unavailable
摘要中文翻译
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
本研究调查了尼日利亚患者 慢性乙型肝炎 病毒 (CHBV) 感染的经济负担及其对灾难性医疗支出 (CHE) 的影响。该研究解决了人们日益关注的与管理 CHBV 相关的财务压力,特别是在低收入环境中。
设计与对象
该研究是一项以医院为基础的横断面调查,在尼日利亚纳萨拉瓦州拉菲亚联邦大学教学医院进行,回顾期为 12 个月,截至 2026 年。研究对象包括 108 名在胃肠病科就诊的慢性乙型肝炎成人患者。
核心方法
该研究使用基于 患病率 的疾病成本框架,通过比较年度患者总支出与家庭收入来评估经济负担。它应用世界卫生组织的灾难性医疗支出阈值,并使用卡方分析来测试收入、保险参保和 治疗 中断之间的关联。
主要结果
The study found that the average annual economic burden of CHBV management is ₦738,049.26 per patient, exceeding the WHO catastrophic threshold at all observed income levels. Additionally, 83 percent of patients reported financial stress, and 41 percent delayed or discontinued treatment due to financial reasons.
解释与创新
作者解释说,CHBV 管理的高经济负担导致了巨大的财务压力和 治疗 中断,凸显了改进财务保护机制的必要性。这项研究为尼日利亚 CHBV 的财务影响提供了新颖的见解。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果强调了扩大尼日利亚 CHBV 患者健康保险覆盖范围和改善获得 抗病毒药物 药物的重要性。他们还强调需要对灾难性卫生支出进行常规监测,作为实现全民健康覆盖的更广泛努力的一部分。
局限与证据强度
该研究仅限于单一医院环境,并依赖于自我报告的数据,这可能会引入偏差。尽管存在这些限制,但由于使用了世卫组织既定阈值和统计分析,证据强度中等。

Catastrophic Health Expenditure among Patients Managing Chronic Hepatitis B Virus Infection: Evidence from Federal University Teaching Hospital, Lafia, Nigeria

Authors: Authors unavailable
Original abstract (English)
This study examines catastrophic health expenditure (CHE) among patients managing chronic Hepatitis B virus (CHBV) infection in Federal University Teaching Hospital, Lafia, Nasarawa State, Nigeria.The study covers a 12-month recall period ending in 2026, drawing on hospital-based cross-sectional survey data from 108 adult CHBV patients attending the Gastroenterology Unit of the teaching hospital.The study adopts a prevalence-based Cost-of-Illness framework, benchmarking total annual patient expenditure against household income using the World Health Organization's catastrophic health expenditure thresholds (outof-pocket payments exceeding 10 percent of household income or 40 percent of capacity to pay), and tests bivariate associations between income, insurance enrolment, and treatment interruption using chi-square analysis.The findings show that CHBV management imposes an average annual economic burden of ₦738,049.26per patient, an amount that exceeds the WHO catastrophic threshold at every observed household income level, from below 30.75 percent of annual income among the highest earners to several times annual income among the lowest earners.Consistent with this, 83 percent of patients reported financial stress and 41 percent had delayed or discontinued treatment for financial reasons, with household income significantly associated with treatment interruption.while health insurance enrolment was low, reflecting the shallow depth of coverage even among the insured.Based on the findings, the study recommends that government and health insurance providers expand comprehensive, adequately resourced coverage for HBV diagnostics and antiviral medication, decentralise chronic HBV care to primary and secondary facilities, and prioritise catastrophic-expenditure monitoring as a routine indicator of financial risk protection in Nigeria's path toward universal health coverage.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
This study investigates the economic burden of chronic Hepatitis B virus (CHBV) infection and its impact on catastrophic health expenditure (CHE) among patients in Nigeria. The research addresses the growing concern of financial strain associated with managing CHBV, particularly in low-income settings.
Design and population
The study is a hospital-based cross-sectional survey conducted at Federal University Teaching Hospital, Lafia, Nasarawa State, Nigeria, over a 12-month recall period ending in 2026. It includes 108 adult patients with chronic HBV infection attending the Gastroenterology Unit.
Core methods
The study uses a prevalence-based Cost-of-Illness framework to assess the economic burden by comparing total annual patient expenditure with household income. It applies the World Health Organization's catastrophic health expenditure thresholds and uses chi-square analysis to test associations between income, insurance enrolment, and treatment interruption.
Main results
The study found that the average annual economic burden of CHBV management is ₦738,049.26 per patient, exceeding the WHO catastrophic threshold at all observed income levels. Additionally, 83 percent of patients reported financial stress, and 41 percent delayed or discontinued treatment due to financial reasons.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpret that the high economic burden of CHBV management contributes to significant financial stress and treatment interruptions, highlighting the need for improved financial protection mechanisms. This study provides novel insights into the financial implications of CHBV in a Nigerian context.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings underscore the importance of expanding health insurance coverage and improving access to antiviral medications for CHBV patients in Nigeria. They also emphasize the need for routine monitoring of catastrophic health expenditure as part of broader efforts toward universal health coverage.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited to a single hospital setting and relies on self-reported data, which may introduce bias. Despite these limitations, the evidence is moderate in strength due to the use of established WHO thresholds and statistical analysis.

改善双阴性人群乙型肝炎 疫苗接种 摄取的多成分干预措施

Multicomponent Interventions to Improve Hepatitis B Vaccination Uptake Among Double-Negative Population
作者： Yunkai Lin, Shuaijing Zhang, Jianhang Li, Rong Cao, Jianpeng Xiao, 何剑峰, 邓世洲, Jun Liu, Guiyuan Ji, Xingfen Yang
摘要中文翻译通过乙型肝炎筛查发现的很大一部分个体是双阴性(HBsAg 阴性和抗 HBs 阴性),并且尽管存在有效的 疫苗,但仍然完全容易受到 乙型肝炎病毒 (HBV) 感染。检索了 PubMed、Web of Science 和 CNKI 2000 年 1 月至 2025 年 12 月期间发表的有关双阴性人群中 HBV 疫苗接种 障碍及其干预措施的文献。障碍存在于个人(知识匮乏、疫苗 犹豫)、服务提供(地理不便、财务负担、筛选到 疫苗接种 路径的断开)和政策(提供者激励薄弱、跨部门协调不足)层面。报告的干预措施包括以患者为导向的策略(提醒和回忆、基于健康信念模型和计划行为理论的教育、社区参与)、以提供者为导向的方法(临床决策支持、疫苗接种激励、计划-实施-研究-行动周期)和政策层面的措施(控制温度链、向前支付、服务分散化)。多组件捆绑可能是互补的,并且比单一组件产生更大的效果;然而,双阴性人群的直接直接证据有限。双阴性状态是三种免疫轨迹(从未接种疫苗、疫苗 无应答者和免疫力减弱)的趋同终点表型,因此应相应地对干预措施进行分层。提出了“筛选-教育-疫苗接种-随访”框架,并应优先考虑具有嵌套成本效益分析的实用整群随机试验。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景

检索了 PubMed、Web of Science 和 CNKI 2000 年 1 月至 2025 年 12 月期间发表的有关双阴性人群中 HBV 疫苗接种 障碍及其干预措施的文献。

障碍存在于个人(知识匮乏、疫苗 犹豫)、服务提供(地理不便、财务负担、筛选到 疫苗接种 路径的断开)和政策(提供者激励薄弱、跨部门协调不足)层面。

科研与公卫意义
双阴性状态是三种免疫轨迹(从未接种疫苗、疫苗 无应答者和免疫力减弱)的趋同终点表型,因此应相应地对干预措施进行分层。

Multicomponent Interventions to Improve Hepatitis B Vaccination Uptake Among Double-Negative Population

Authors: Yunkai Lin, Shuaijing Zhang, Jianhang Li, Rong Cao, Jianpeng Xiao, 何剑峰, 邓世洲, Jun Liu, Guiyuan Ji, Xingfen Yang
Original abstract (English)
A substantial proportion of individuals identified through hepatitis B screening are double-negative (HBsAg-negative and anti-HBs-negative) and remain fully susceptible to hepatitis B virus (HBV) infection despite the availability of an effective vaccine. PubMed, Web of Science, and CNKI were searched for literature published between January 2000 and December 2025 on barriers to HBV vaccination in double-negative populations and the interventions addressing them. Barriers operate at individual (knowledge deficits, vaccine hesitancy), service delivery (geographic inaccessibility, financial burden, disconnected screening-to-vaccination pathways), and policy (weak provider incentives, insufficient cross-sector coordination) levels. Reported interventions comprise patient-oriented strategies (reminder and recall, Health Belief Model- and Theory of Planned Behavior-based education, community engagement), provider-oriented approaches (clinical decision support, vaccination incentives, Plan-Do-Study-Act cycles), and policy-level measures (controlled temperature chain, pay-it-forward, service decentralization). Multicomponent bundles may be complementary and produce greater effects than single components; however, direct head-to-head evidence in the double-negative population is limited. Double-negative status is a convergent end-point phenotype of three immunological trajectories (never-vaccinated, vaccine non-responders, and waned immunity), and interventions should therefore be stratified accordingly. A “Screening-Education-Vaccination-Follow-up” framework is proposed, and pragmatic cluster-randomized trials with nested cost-effectiveness analyses should be prioritized.
View research seven-element analysis

A “Screening-Education-Vaccination-Follow-up” framework is proposed, and pragmatic cluster-randomized trials with nested cost-effectiveness analyses should be prioritized.

Seroprevalence of Hepatitis B and C Viruses and Association with Biochemical Parameters among Patients Tested for Viral Hepatitis in Cumilla, Bangladesh: A Cross-sectional Study

Background: Viral hepatitis, caused mainly by hepatitis B virus (HBV) and hepatitis C virus (HCV), is a leading cause of liver-related morbidity and mortality worldwide, contributing to an estimated 1.3 million deaths annually. View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Viral hepatitis, primarily caused by HBV and HCV, is a leading cause of liver‐related morbidity and mortality worldwide, and Bangladesh is considered intermediate‐endemic for HBV but lacks local data from downtown Cumilla. The study aimed to determine the proportion of HBV and HCV infections among patients tested in Cumilla and to examine their association with age, sex, and biochemical parameters (SGPT/ALT and serum bilirubin).
Design and population
A cross‐sectional, laboratory‐based study was conducted among 100 patients who attended selected diagnostic centres and healthcare facilities in downtown Cumilla for viral hepatitis testing between January and August 2026, recruited consecutively using convenience sampling.
Core methods
Age, sex, HBsAg, anti‐HCV, serum SGPT/ALT and serum bilirubin were extracted from laboratory reports and patient records. Associations were analysed using chi‐square tests (with continuity correction for 2×2 tables) and independent‐samples t‐tests, with p‐values reported as appropriate.

巴勒斯坦西岸吸毒者中丙型肝炎 血清流行率 及其与注射吸毒的关系:一项回顾性横断面研究。

Hepatitis C seroprevalence and its association with injecting drug use among people who use drugs in the West Bank, Palestine: a retrospective cross-sectional study.
作者： Abeer Habash, Asad Ramlawi, Razan AbuGhoush, Ghada Issa, Maria Kokali, Radwan Qasrawi, Razan Abu Ghoush
摘要中文翻译
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
吸毒者 (PWUD) 发生血源性病毒感染的风险增加。关于巴勒斯坦残疾人士中的丙型肝炎病毒 (HCV)、乙型肝炎病毒 (HBV) 和人类免疫缺陷病毒 (HIV) 的证据有限。
设计与对象
这项回顾性横断面研究纳入了 2019 年至 2023 年期间西岸物质使用障碍 (SUD) 治疗 中心的 397 名残疾人。
核心方法
抗 HCV、HBsAg 和抗 HIV 的血清学结果从常规临床记录中获得。使用改良泊松回归估计注射吸毒与抗 HCV 血清阳性之间关联的粗略和调整后的 患病率 比率 (PR)。
主要结果
HCV 血清流行率 为 23.0%,HBsAg 患病率 为 2.0%,未检测到 HIV。注射者的 HCV 血清流行率 为 55.4%,而非注射者的 HCV 血清流行率 为 6.7%。
解释与创新
注射吸毒与 HCV 血清阳性密切相关,并且 患病率 随着吸毒持续时间的延长而增加。
科研与公卫意义
在西岸接受 SUD 治疗 的残疾人中,HCV 是主要感染病例,而 HBV 患病率 感染率较低,未检测到 HIV。将 HCV 筛查、RNA 检测、抗病毒药物 治疗 和减少危害政策纳入 SUD 服务对于实现 WHO 2030 年肝炎消除目标至关重要。
局限与证据强度
所提供的证据并未报告该研究的具体局限性。

Hepatitis C seroprevalence and its association with injecting drug use among people who use drugs in the West Bank, Palestine: a retrospective cross-sectional study.

Authors: Abeer Habash, Asad Ramlawi, Razan AbuGhoush, Ghada Issa, Maria Kokali, Radwan Qasrawi, Razan Abu Ghoush
Original abstract (English)
BACKGROUND: People who use drugs (PWUD) are at increased risk of blood-borne viral infections. Evidence on hepatitis C virus (HCV), hepatitis B virus (HBV), and human immunodeficiency virus (HIV) among PWUD in Palestine is limited. This study determined the seroprevalence of HCV, HBV, and HIV among PWUD attending governmental substance use disorder (SUD) treatment centers in the West Bank. METHODS: This retrospective cross-sectional study included 397 PWUD in SUD treatment centers in the West Bank during 2019-2023. Serological results for anti-HCV, HBsAg, and anti-HIV were obtained from routine clinical records. Crude and adjusted prevalence ratios (PRs) for the association between injecting drug use and anti-HCV seropositivity were estimated using modified Poisson regression. RESULTS: HCV seroprevalence was 23.0%, HBsAg prevalence was 2.0%, and no HIV was detected. HCV seroprevalence was 55.4% among injecting versus 6.7% among non-injecting users. After adjustment for age, education, employment status, and previous treatment attempts, injecting drug use remained strongly associated with HCV seropositivity (adjusted PR = 6.43, 95% CI 3.82-10.82). HCV prevalence increased with longer duration of drug use (p for trend <0.001), and varied significantly across governorates. CONCLUSIONS: HCV is the predominant infection among PWUD receiving SUD treatment in the West Bank, whereas HBV prevalence was low and HIV was not detected. Injecting drug use was strongly associated with HCV seropositivity, and prevalence increased with longer duration of drug use. Integrating HCV screening, RNA testing, antiviral treatment, and harm-reduction policies into SUD services is essential to achieve the WHO 2030 hepatitis elimination targets.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
People who use drugs (PWUD) are at increased risk of blood-borne viral infections. Evidence on hepatitis C virus (HCV), hepatitis B virus (HBV), and human immunodeficiency virus (HIV) among PWUD in Palestine is limited.
Design and population

Core methods

Main results
HCV seroprevalence was 23.0%, HBsAg prevalence was 2.0%, and no HIV was detected. HCV seroprevalence was 55.4% among injecting versus 6.7% among non-injecting users.
Interpretation and novelty
Injecting drug use was strongly associated with HCV seropositivity, and prevalence increased with longer duration of drug use.
Scientific and public-health significance
HCV is the predominant infection among PWUD receiving SUD treatment in the West Bank, whereas HBV prevalence was low and HIV was not detected. Integrating HCV screening, RNA testing, antiviral treatment, and harm-reduction policies into SUD services is essential to achieve the WHO 2030 hepatitis elimination targets.
Limitations and evidence strength
The supplied evidence does not report specific limitations of the study.

聚乙二醇化干扰素 α-2b 添加到核苷(酸)类似物可减少肝细胞癌和晚期纤维化的失代偿 慢性乙型肝炎

Pegylated interferon α-2b add-on to nucleos(t)ide analogues reduces hepatocellular carcinoma and decompensation in advanced fibrotic chronic hepatitis B
作者： Xiang-Yong Li, Jing-Tao Huang, Ting Liu, Ying-Yi Li, Long Zhe, Hong Cao, Bing-Liang Lin
摘要中文翻译背景目前在核苷(酸)类似物(NA)中添加聚乙二醇化干扰素(Peg-IFN)α-2b的治疗策略主要旨在实现功能性治愈。然而,其在改善 乙型肝炎病毒 (HBV) 相关晚期纤维化或代偿性肝硬化患者预后方面的价值以及该联合策略的合适候选者仍有待进一步探索。目的 评估附加 Peg-IFNα-2b 治疗对 HBV 相关晚期纤维化或代偿性肝硬化患者的长期预后益处。方法 这项回顾性 队列研究 研究纳入了诊断为 HBV 相关晚期纤维化或代偿性肝硬化的患者。参与者被分为两组:(1)接受联合治疗(Peg-IFNα-2b 附加到正在进行的 NA 治疗);(2) 接受 NA 单一疗法的患者。采用 1:1 倾向评分匹配方法来平衡各组之间的基线特征。在生存分析中,肝细胞癌 (HCC) 和失代偿性肝硬化的结果根据基线临床特征和既定的 HCC 风险评分进行分层。结果 该分析包括 302 对配对,中位随访时间为 220 周。与 NA 单一疗法相比,联合治疗与发生 HCC(风险比 = 0.22;95% CI:0.11-0.48)和失代偿性肝硬化(风险比 = 0.39;95% CI:0.20-0.77)的风险显着降低相关。这些益处在经验证的 HCC 预测模型分类为中高风险的患者以及持续生化异常持续 > 12 个月的患者中尤其明显。结论 Peg-IFNα-2b 与 NA 的附加治疗与患有 HBV 相关晚期纤维化或肝硬化的高危患者的 HCC 减少和失代偿显着相关,支持其在该人群中的使用。

问题与背景
该研究旨在评估附加 Peg-IFNα-2b 治疗对 HBV 相关晚期纤维化或代偿性肝硬化患者的长期预后益处。当前在核苷(酸)类似物(NA)中添加聚乙二醇化干扰素(Peg-IFN)α-2b的治疗策略主要旨在实现功能性治愈。

核心方法

科研与公卫意义
研究结果表明,Peg-IFNα-2b 联合治疗可能为减少高危 HBV 患者的 HCC 和失代偿带来重要益处。这可以为未来 治疗 治疗 慢性乙型肝炎 晚期纤维化和肝硬化的策略提供信息。
局限与证据强度
该研究是一项回顾性队列研究研究,可能会引入偏倚和混杂因素。证据是中等的,因为它依赖于倾向评分匹配和分层生存分析。

Pegylated interferon α-2b add-on to nucleos(t)ide analogues reduces hepatocellular carcinoma and decompensation in advanced fibrotic chronic hepatitis B

Authors: Xiang-Yong Li, Jing-Tao Huang, Ting Liu, Ying-Yi Li, Long Zhe, Hong Cao, Bing-Liang Lin
Original abstract (English)
BACKGROUND The current therapeutic strategy of adding pegylated interferon (Peg-IFN) α-2b to nucleos(t)ide analogues (NAs) is primarily aimed at achieving functional cure. However, its value in improving prognosis and the suitable candidates for this combination strategy in patients with hepatitis B virus (HBV)-related advanced fibrosis or compensated cirrhosis remain to be further explored. AIM To evaluate the long-term prognosis benefit of add-on Peg-IFNα-2b therapy in patients with HBV-related advanced fibrosis or compensated cirrhosis. METHODS This retrospective cohort study enrolled patients diagnosed with HBV-related advanced fibrosis or compensated cirrhosis. Participants were divided into two groups: (1) Those receiving combination therapy (Peg-IFNα-2b add-on to ongoing NA treatment); and (2) Those receiving NA monotherapy. A 1:1 propensity score matching approach was applied to balance baseline characteristics between groups. In survival analysis, the outcomes of hepatocellular carcinoma (HCC) and decompensated cirrhosis were stratified by baseline clinical features and established HCC risk scores. RESULTS The analysis included 302 matched pairs, with a median follow-up duration of 220 weeks. Combination therapy was associated with a significantly lower risk of developing HCC (hazard ratio = 0.22; 95%CI: 0.11-0.48) and decompensated cirrhosis (hazard ratio = 0.39; 95%CI: 0.20-0.77) than NA monotherapy. These benefits were particularly pronounced among patients classified as intermediate-to-high risk by validated HCC prediction models and in those with persistent biochemical abnormalities lasting > 12 months. CONCLUSION Add-on therapy with Peg-IFNα-2b to NAs is significantly associated with reduced HCC and decompensation in high-risk patients with HBV-related advanced fibrosis or cirrhosis, supporting its use in this population.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to evaluate the long-term prognosis benefit of add-on Peg-IFNα-2b therapy in patients with HBV-related advanced fibrosis or compensated cirrhosis. The current therapeutic strategy of adding pegylated interferon (Peg-IFN) α-2b to nucleos(t)ide analogues (NAs) is primarily aimed at achieving functional cure.
Design and population
This retrospective cohort study enrolled patients diagnosed with HBV-related advanced fibrosis or compensated cirrhosis. Participants were divided into two groups: (1) Those receiving combination therapy (Peg-IFNα-2b add-on to ongoing NA treatment); and (2) Those receiving NA monotherapy.
Core methods
A 1:1 propensity score matching approach was applied to balance baseline characteristics between groups. In survival analysis, the outcomes of hepatocellular carcinoma (HCC) and decompensated cirrhosis were stratified by baseline clinical features and established HCC risk scores.
Main results
The analysis included 302 matched pairs, with a median follow-up duration of 220 weeks. Combination therapy was associated with a significantly lower risk of developing HCC (hazard ratio = 0.22; 95%CI: 0.11-0.48) and decompensated cirrhosis (hazard ratio = 0.39; 95%CI: 0.20-0.77) than NA monotherapy.
Interpretation and novelty
These benefits were particularly pronounced among patients classified as intermediate-to-high risk by validated HCC prediction models and in those with persistent biochemical abnormalities lasting > 12 months. Add-on therapy with Peg-IFNα-2b to NAs is significantly associated with reduced HCC and decompensation in high-risk patients with HBV-related advanced fibrosis or cirrhosis, supporting its use in this population.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings suggest that combination therapy with Peg-IFNα-2b may offer important benefits for reducing HCC and decompensation in high-risk HBV patients. This could inform future treatment strategies for managing advanced fibrosis and cirrhosis in chronic hepatitis B.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is a retrospective cohort study, which may introduce bias and confounding factors. The evidence is moderate as it relies on propensity score matching and stratified survival analysis.

卡纳塔克邦班加罗尔三级护理医院医护人员关于乙型肝炎感染和 疫苗接种 的知识、态度和实践

Knowledge, Attitude and Practices regarding Hepatitis B Infection and Vaccination among Healthcare Workers in a Tertiary Care Hospital in Bangalore, Karnataka
作者： Smriti Ashok, Shreyas R Kumar, Siri Shivyani PS, Sneha S, Chaitali Ashutosh Gore
摘要中文翻译背景:乙型肝炎虽然是 疫苗 可预防的疾病,但却是全球 疫苗 的主要原因之一。医护人员 (HCW) 的 患病率 比例高出四倍。因此,本研究的目的是评估和比较三级医院不同群体的医护人员对乙型肝炎疫苗接种的KAP。方法:通过问卷进行横断面调查,收集卡纳塔克邦班加罗尔一家三级医院不同群体医护人员的信息,包括医生、护士、牙医和实验室技术人员。使用 SPSS 软件版本 23 对收集的数据进行编码和分析。然后使用方差分析来查找组之间的平均差异。结果:这项横断面研究包括 N = 121 名医疗保健专业人员(医生 n = 31、牙科医生 n = 30、护士 n = 30、实验室技术人员 n = 30)。人口统计变量也按专业群体进行分层,以尽量减少混淆。卡方检验显示各组的性别或年龄分布没有显着差异(p > 0.05)。此外,单因素方差分析显示不同专业群体在知识、态度、实践和总 KAP 分数方面存在统计显着差异 (p < 0.05)。此外,效应量分析显示出中等至较大的影响,其中专业类别解释了知识差异的 16%、态度差异的 21%、实践差异的 9% 和总 KAP 分数的差异 24%。结论:研究表明,医护人员在乙型肝炎方面存在 KAP 差距,而辅助医务人员的 KAP 差距更大。这一差距需要通过创建中央数据库、定期提醒 疫苗接种、加强 HICC 以及对医护人员的废物处理指导来填补。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
乙型肝炎虽然是 疫苗 可预防的疾病,但却是全球 疫苗 的主要原因之一。医护人员 (HCW) 的 患病率 比例高出四倍。
设计与对象
通过问卷进行横断面调查,收集来自卡纳塔克邦班加罗尔一家三级医院的不同医护人员群体的信息,其中包括医生、护士、牙医和实验室技术人员。
核心方法
使用 SPSS 软件版本 23 对收集的数据进行编码和分析。然后使用方差分析来查找组之间的平均差异。
主要结果
这项横断面研究包括 N = 121 名医疗保健专业人员(医生 n = 31、牙科医生 n = 30、护士 n = 30、实验室技术人员 n = 30)。卡方检验显示各组的性别或年龄分布没有显着差异(p > 0.05)。此外,单因素方差分析显示不同专业群体在知识、态度、实践和总 KAP 分数方面存在统计显着差异 (p < 0.05)。此外,效应量分析显示出中等至较大的影响,其中专业类别解释了知识差异的 16%、态度差异的 21%、实践差异的 9% 和总 KAP 分数的差异 24%。

科研与公卫意义
这一差距需要通过创建中央数据库、定期提醒 疫苗接种、加强 HICC 以及对医护人员的废物处理指导来填补。
局限与证据强度
人口统计变量也按专业群体进行分层,以尽量减少混淆。

Knowledge, Attitude and Practices regarding Hepatitis B Infection and Vaccination among Healthcare Workers in a Tertiary Care Hospital in Bangalore, Karnataka

Authors: Smriti Ashok, Shreyas R Kumar, Siri Shivyani PS, Sneha S, Chaitali Ashutosh Gore
Original abstract (English)
Background: Hepatitis B, although a vaccine preventable disease, is one of the leading causes of mortality worldwide. The prevalence is four folds higher among healthcare workers (HCW). Therefore, the objective of this study was to assess and compare the KAP towards Hepatitis B vaccination among different groups of healthcare workers in a tertiary hospital. Methods: Cross-sectional survey was conducted with a questionnaire to gather information from different groups of healthcare workers which included doctors, nurses, dentists and lab technicians in a tertiary hospital in Bangalore, Karnataka. The data collected was coded and analyzed using SPSS software version 23. ANOVA was then used for finding the mean difference between the groups. Results: This cross-sectional study included N = 121 healthcare professionals (doctors n = 31, dental surgeons n = 30, nurses n = 30, lab technicians n = 30). Demographic variables were also stratified by professional groups to minimize confounding. Chi-square test showed no significant difference in gender or age distribution across groups (p > 0.05). Moreover, one-way ANOVA demonstrated statistically significant differences in knowledge, attitude, practice, and total KAP scores across professional groups (p 0.05). In addition, effect size analysis showed moderate-to-large effects, with professional category explaining 16% variance in knowledge, 21% in attitude, 9% in practice, and 24% in total KAP scores. Conclusion: The study showed KAP gap in HCW’s regarding hepatitis B, and more so in paramedical workers. This gap needs to be filled with the creation of a central database, regular reminders for vaccination, strengthening of the HICC, and waste disposal guidance to HCWs.

Histological differences between patients with porto-sinusoidal vascular disorder and fibrosis regression of chronic hepatitis B: an analysis based on machine learning algorithms

Authors: Zhengxin Li, Jinzhao Wang, Meng Li, Yayun Ren, Dean C. S. Tai, Qingchun Fu, Chenghai Liu
Original abstract (English)
Abstract Background Porto-sinusoidal vascular disorder (PSVD) alters portal hemodynamics, leading to portal hypertension without obvious fibrosis. Incomplete septal fibrosis (ISF) is recognized as one of the pathological hallmarks of PSVD; however, the differences in ISF between patients with PSVD and those with fibrosis regression remain controversial. This study aimed to analyze the vascular and fibrotic characteristics of liver biopsies between two cohorts using machine learning algorithms. Methods 41 patients with PSVD and 40 with chronic hepatitis B who achieved fibrosis regression after 48 weeks of entecavir treatment were enrolled. The fibrosis stage was assessed using the Ishak system. Images of unstained liver biopsy slides were obtained using second-harmonic generation/two-photon excited fluorescence. Machine learning algorithms were adopted for the analysis: arterial density (AD), portal vein density (PVD), central vein density (CVD), collagen proportionate area (CPA), and different areas of CPA on the portal area, zones 1/2/3, and ISF. Results In contrast to fibrosis regression, patients with PSVD had significantly higher AD (17.98 ± 10.38/mm2 vs 8.59 ± 8.28/mm2, P

Question and background
This study aimed to analyze the vascular and fibrotic characteristics of liver biopsies between two cohorts using machine learning algorithms. Abstract Background Porto-sinusoidal vascular disorder (PSVD) alters portal hemodynamics, leading to portal hypertension without obvious fibrosis.
Design and population
Methods 41 patients with PSVD and 40 with chronic hepatitis B who achieved fibrosis regression after 48 weeks of entecavir treatment were enrolled. The fibrosis stage was assessed using the Ishak system.
Core methods
Machine learning algorithms were adopted for the analysis: arterial density (AD), portal vein density (PVD), central vein density (CVD), collagen proportionate area (CPA), and different areas of CPA on the portal area, zones 1/2/3, and ISF. Images of unstained liver biopsy slides were obtained using second-harmonic generation/two-photon excited fluorescence.
Main results
Results In contrast to fibrosis regression, patients with PSVD had significantly higher AD (17.98 ± 10.38/mm2 vs 8.59 ± 8.28/mm2, P.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpreted the findings as highlighting distinct vascular and fibrotic patterns in PSVD compared to fibrosis regression, which may inform more precise diagnostic approaches.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings may inform the development of more precise diagnostic tools for distinguishing PSVD from fibrosis regression in patients with chronic hepatitis B.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited by its abstract-only nature and potential biases in the selection of liver biopsy samples, which may affect the generalizability of the results. The evidence is moderate in strength due to the lack of detailed statistical analysis and outcome reporting.

尼日利亚女性乙型肝炎意识和近期检测:2024 年人口和健康调查分析

Hepatitis B Awareness and Recent Testing Among Women in Nigeria: Analysis of the 2024 Demographic and Health Survey
作者： Obinna Emmanuel Ezugwu, Abdul‐Aziz Seidu, Abdul‐Wahab Inusah, Theophilus I. Emeto
摘要中文翻译
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
乙型肝炎病毒 (HBV) 在尼日利亚属于高流行病,但缺乏关于育龄妇女 HBV 认知和检测的国家数据。
设计与对象
我们分析了 2024 年尼日利亚人口和健康调查中 38,647 名 15-49 岁女性的数据。
核心方法
我们估计了听说过 HBV(知晓率)的调查加权 患病率、知晓女性中近期(12 个月)检测以及所有女性近期检测,并拟合了调查加权逻辑回归和多项回归模型。
主要结果
知晓率为 53.3% (95% CI 52.1–54.6)。在有意识的女性中,12.9% (95% CI 12.1–13.7) 最近接受过检测,相当于所有女性的 6.9% (95% CI 6.4–7.4)。
解释与创新
乙肝病毒检测远远落后于人们的认识,并且主要集中在更有优势的女性身上。
科研与公卫意义
这些发现强调需要采取有针对性的干预措施,以改善乙型肝炎检测的可及性和负担能力,特别是在产前护理方面。
局限与证据强度
由于数据是横截面数据和自我报告的,因此这些关联并不具有因果关系。

Hepatitis B Awareness and Recent Testing Among Women in Nigeria: Analysis of the 2024 Demographic and Health Survey

Authors: Obinna Emmanuel Ezugwu, Abdul‐Aziz Seidu, Abdul‐Wahab Inusah, Theophilus I. Emeto
Original abstract (English)
Introduction: Hepatitis B virus (HBV) is hyperendemic in Nigeria, but national data on HBV awareness and testing among women of reproductive age are lacking. Methods: We analysed data from 38,647 women aged 15–49 years in the 2024 Nigeria Demographic and Health Survey. We estimated survey-weighted prevalence of having heard of HBV (awareness), recent (12-month) testing among aware women, and recent testing among all women, and fitted survey-weighted logistic and multinomial regression models. Results: Awareness was 53.3% (95% CI 52.1–54.6). Among aware women, 12.9% (95% CI 12.1–13.7) had been recently tested, equivalent to 6.9% (95% CI 6.4–7.4) of all women. Higher education, greater wealth and health insurance were associated with both outcomes; women in the richest quintile had higher odds of recent testing than those in the poorest (adjusted odds ratio 3.29, 95% CI 2.31–4.68), and the wealth gradient was steeper for testing than for awareness on the relative scale. Recent testing among all women was lowest in the South South (5.0%) and North West (5.4%). The North West reported the highest awareness (66.5%), but almost all interviews there were in Hausa, and this estimate may partly reflect recognition of a translated term rather than of HBV itself. Socioeconomic gradients in both outcomes differed significantly by zone. Conclusions: HBV testing lags far behind awareness and is concentrated among more advantaged women. Zonal awareness estimates, particularly for the North West, should be interpreted with caution. Because the data are cross-sectional and self-reported, these associations are not causal. Approaches that make HBV testing routinely available and affordable, particularly within antenatal care, warrant evaluation.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Hepatitis B virus (HBV) is hyperendemic in Nigeria, but national data on HBV awareness and testing among women of reproductive age are lacking.
Design and population
We analysed data from 38,647 women aged 15–49 years in the 2024 Nigeria Demographic and Health Survey.
Core methods
We estimated survey-weighted prevalence of having heard of HBV (awareness), recent (12-month) testing among aware women, and recent testing among all women, and fitted survey-weighted logistic and multinomial regression models.
Main results
Awareness was 53.3% (95% CI 52.1–54.6). Among aware women, 12.9% (95% CI 12.1–13.7) had been recently tested, equivalent to 6.9% (95% CI 6.4–7.4) of all women.
Interpretation and novelty
HBV testing lags far behind awareness and is concentrated among more advantaged women.
Scientific and public-health significance
These findings highlight the need for targeted interventions to improve HBV testing access and affordability, particularly within antenatal care.
Limitations and evidence strength
Because the data are cross-sectional and self-reported, these associations are not causal.

Predicting hepatitis B virus DNA elevation during pregnancy: Toward risk-stratified monitoring beyond the traditional viral-load threshold
作者： Gemechu Dereje Feyissa

查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究旨在解决传统病毒载量阈值在预测妊娠期间 乙型肝炎病毒 (HBV) DNA 升高方面的局限性,强调需要改进风险分层监测,以更好地管理母婴健康。
设计与对象
该研究是在埃塞俄比亚进行的一项回顾性分析,重点关注母亲 HBV DNA 水平及其与妊娠中晚期升高的关系。它涉及中等大小的数据集,不包括外部验证。
核心方法
研究人员开发了一个模型,使用母亲年龄和基线 HBV DNA 水平≥100国际单位/毫升来预测怀孕期间 DNA 升高。该模型基于现有数据,不涉及前瞻性测试或外部验证。
主要结果
研究发现,母亲年龄和基线 HBV DNA ≥ 100 国际单位/毫升可以预测中晚期妊娠升高。然而,该模型无法预测 传播、产妇突发或 治疗 需求。
解释与创新
作者将他们的发现解释为迈向风险分层监测的一步,表明动态 监测 可以改善及时的 传播 预防并减少母婴 传播。这种方法的新颖之处在于它专注于超越传统病毒载量阈值的预测模型。
科研与公卫意义
这些发现可能为感染 HBV 的孕妇提供更有针对性的产前 监测 策略,通过早期干预和降低母婴 传播 风险可能改善结局。
局限与证据强度
该研究的回顾性设计、缺乏外部验证以及中等的性能限制了其在临床决策中的适用性。由于这些限制,证据的强度中等。

Predicting hepatitis B virus DNA elevation during pregnancy: Toward risk-stratified monitoring beyond the traditional viral-load threshold

Authors: Gemechu Dereje Feyissa
Original abstract (English)
11/17/2026 10:50:12 AM | Browse: 40 | Download: 0 | Publication Name | World Journal of Translational Medicine | |---|---| | Manuscript ID | 129356 | | Country | Ethiopia | | Category | Gastroenterology & Hepatology | |---|---| | Manuscript Type | Editorial | | Article Title | Predicting hepatitis B virus DNA elevation during pregnancy: Toward risk-stratified monitoring beyond the traditional viral-load threshold | | Manuscript Source | Invited Manuscript | | All Author List | Gemechu Dereje Feyissa | | Funding Agency and Grant Number | | | Corresponding Author | Gemechu Dereje Feyissa, Assistant Professor, Department of Public Health, Faculty of Health Sciences, Rift Valley University, Adama Campus, Adama 1715, Oromia Regional State, Ethiopia. gemechudereje80@gmail.com | | Key Words | Hepatitis B virus; Pregnancy; Mother-to-child transmission; Hepatitis B virus deoxyribonucleic acid; Viral-load elevation; Risk prediction; Antenatal surveillance; Antiviral prophylaxis | | Core Tip | Maternal hepatitis B virus (HBV) deoxyribonucleic acid may rise later in pregnancy, but testing often uses a single prophylaxis threshold. found maternal age and baseline HBV deoxyribonucleic acid ≥ 100 international units per milliliter predicted mid-to-late pregnancy elevation. The model may guide repeat testing but cannot predict transmission, maternal hepatitis flares, or treatment need; retrospective design, moderate performance, and absent external validation preclude treatment decisions. Prospective studies should define retesting intervals and assess whether dynamic surveillance improves timely prophylaxis and reduces mother-to-child transmission. | | Citation | Feyissa GD. Predicting hepatitis B virus DNA elevation during pregnancy: Toward risk-stratified monitoring beyond the trad...
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to address the limitations of traditional viral-load thresholds in predicting hepatitis B virus (HBV) DNA elevation during pregnancy, highlighting the need for improved risk-stratified monitoring to better manage maternal and fetal health.
Design and population
The study was a retrospective analysis conducted in Ethiopia, focusing on maternal HBV DNA levels and their association with elevation during mid-to-late pregnancy. It involved a moderate-sized dataset and did not include external validation.
Core methods
The researchers developed a model using maternal age and baseline HBV DNA levels ≥ 100 international units per milliliter to predict DNA elevation during pregnancy. The model was based on existing data and did not involve prospective testing or external validation.
Main results
Maternal age and baseline HBV DNA ≥ 100 international units per milliliter were found to predict mid-to-late pregnancy elevation. However, the model could not predict transmission, maternal flares, or treatment needs.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpreted their findings as a step toward risk-stratified monitoring, suggesting that dynamic surveillance could improve timely antiviral prophylaxis and reduce mother-to-child transmission. This approach is novel in its focus on predictive modeling beyond traditional viral-load thresholds.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings may inform more targeted antenatal surveillance strategies for HBV-infected pregnant women, potentially improving outcomes by enabling earlier interventions and reducing the risk of mother-to-child transmission.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study's retrospective design, lack of external validation, and moderate performance limit its applicability for clinical decision-making. The evidence is moderate in strength due to these constraints.

乙型肝炎病毒 相关肝细胞癌中 CD161 凝集素样转录物 1 轴超出程序性细胞死亡蛋白 1

CD161-lectin-like transcript 1 axis beyond programmed cell death protein 1 in hepatitis B virus-associated hepatocellular carcinoma
作者： Hai Nguyen Ngoc Dang, Hau Phuc Tran, Hung Minh Vu, Trung Tran Nguyen, Bich Ngoc Hoang Thi, Thanh Van Ho, Adegbesan Abiodun Christopher, Hung Tran Xuan, Thang Viet Luong
摘要中文翻译乙型肝炎病毒 (HBV) 相关肝细胞癌在慢性炎症、耐受性肝脏微环境中发生。在这种情况下,T 细胞功能障碍限制了程序性死亡 1 (PD-1) 阻断的功效。Liu 等人发表在《世界胃肠肿瘤学杂志》上的新证据表明,CD161 可能代表耗尽的 CD8+ T 细胞内的另一个抑制轴,该轴未被 PD-1/程序性死亡配体-1 通路完全捕获。在他们的研究中,CD161+CD8+T细胞在肿瘤组织中富集,即使PD-1+区室在PD-1抑制后收缩,CD161表达仍然持续,这表明CD161介导的抑制可能在治疗压力下保持活跃。从机制上讲,CD161-凝集素样转录物 1 相互作用的持续参与可能会损害 T 细胞效应器功能,并有助于 HBV 感染肝脏中的适应性免疫抵抗。这些发现强调了 CD161 蛋白质水平评估和 PD-1 平行评估对于准确表征免疫抑制的重要性。关键知识差距包括肿瘤微环境中凝集素样转录物 1 的细胞来源、持续的 HBV 病毒学活性对 CD161+ T 细胞亚群的影响,以及临床有意义的进展终点的识别。在这篇社论中,我们将这些发现置于机制和转化背景中,强调了 CD161 凝集素样转录物 1 轴作为 PD-1 阻断之外的免疫抵抗的补充途径的潜在作用。总的来说,当前的证据支持谨慎的、假设驱动的转化策略,以评估精心挑选的 HBV 相关肝细胞癌患者中 CD161 和 PD-1 轴的联合调节。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究旨在了解 CD161 在 HBV 相关肝细胞癌 T 细胞功能障碍中的作用,解决当前免疫疗法(例如 PD-1 阻断)的局限性。
设计与对象
该研究旨在调查 HBV 相关肝细胞癌患者肿瘤组织中的 CD161 凝集素样转录物 1 轴。
核心方法
该研究涉及分析 HBV 相关肝细胞癌患者的肿瘤组织样本,重点关注 CD161 表达及其与 PD-1 的相互作用。
主要结果
CD161+CD8+T细胞在肿瘤组织中富集,即使在PD-1抑制后CD161表达仍然持续,表明持续的免疫抵抗。
解释与创新
研究结果表明,CD161 介导的抑制可能在治疗压力下保持活跃,凸显了在 HBV 相关肝细胞癌中评估 CD161 与 PD-1 的重要性。
科研与公卫意义
这些发现可以为针对 HBV 相关肝细胞癌患者的 CD161 和 PD-1 轴的联合调节策略的开发提供信息。
局限与证据强度
主要局限性包括需要进一步研究凝集素样转录物 1 的细胞来源以及持续的 HBV 病毒学活性对 CD161+ T 细胞亚群的影响。

CD161-lectin-like transcript 1 axis beyond programmed cell death protein 1 in hepatitis B virus-associated hepatocellular carcinoma

Authors: Hai Nguyen Ngoc Dang, Hau Phuc Tran, Hung Minh Vu, Trung Tran Nguyen, Bich Ngoc Hoang Thi, Thanh Van Ho, Adegbesan Abiodun Christopher, Hung Tran Xuan, Thang Viet Luong
Original abstract (English)
Hepatitis B virus (HBV)-associated hepatocellular carcinoma develops in a chronically inflamed, tolerogenic liver microenvironment. In this setting, T-cell dysfunction limits the efficacy of programmed death-1 (PD-1) blockade. Emerging evidence from Liu et al published in the World Journal of Gastrointestinal Oncology suggests that CD161 may represent an additional inhibitory axis within exhausted CD8+ T cells that is not fully captured by the PD-1/ programmed death ligand-1 pathway. In their study, CD161+CD8+ T cells were enriched in tumor tissue, and CD161 expression persisted even as PD-1+ compartments contracted after PD-1 inhibition, suggesting that CD161-mediated restraint may remain active under therapeutic pressure. Mechanistically, sustained engagement of the CD161-lectin-like transcript 1 interaction may impair T-cell effector function and contribute to adaptive immune resistance in the HBV-infected liver. These findings highlight the importance of protein-level assessment of CD161 and parallel evaluation with PD-1 to accurately characterize immune inhibition. Key knowledge gaps include the cellular source of lectin-like transcript 1 within the tumor microenvironment, the influence of ongoing HBV virological activity on CD161+ T-cell subsets, and the identification of clinically meaningful progression endpoints. In this editorial, we place these findings in mechanistic and translational context, highlighting the potential role of the CD161-lectin-like transcript 1 axis as a complementary pathway of immune resistance beyond PD-1 blockade. Collectively, the current evidence supports a cautious, hypothesis-driven translational strategy to evaluate combined modulation of the CD161 and PD-1 axes in carefully selected patients with HBV-associated hepatocellular carcinoma.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to understand the role of CD161 in T-cell dysfunction in HBV-associated hepatocellular carcinoma, addressing the limitations of current immunotherapies such as PD-1 blockade.
Design and population
The study was conducted to investigate the CD161-lectin-like transcript 1 axis in tumor tissue from patients with HBV-associated hepatocellular carcinoma.
Core methods
The study involved analyzing tumor tissue samples from patients with HBV-associated hepatocellular carcinoma, focusing on CD161 expression and its interaction with PD-1.
Main results
CD161+CD8+ T cells were enriched in tumor tissue, and CD161 expression persisted even after PD-1 inhibition, suggesting ongoing immune resistance.
Interpretation and novelty
The findings suggest that CD161-mediated restraint may remain active under therapeutic pressure, highlighting the importance of evaluating CD161 alongside PD-1 in HBV-associated hepatocellular carcinoma.
Scientific and public-health significance
These findings could inform the development of combined modulation strategies targeting both CD161 and PD-1 axes in patients with HBV-associated hepatocellular carcinoma.
Limitations and evidence strength
Key limitations include the need for further investigation into the cellular source of lectin-like transcript 1 and the influence of ongoing HBV virological activity on CD161+ T-cell subsets.
