定义噻唑化物 抗病毒药物 的结构-活性关系:平衡效力、代谢稳定性和水溶性。

Defining the Structure-Activity Relationships of Thiazolide Antivirals: Balancing Potency, Metabolic Stability, and Aqueous Solubility.
作者： Joshua Taujanskas, Sophie Pate, Andrew V Stachulski, Sara Piacentini, Simone La Frazia, M Gabriella Santoro, Paul M O'Neill
摘要中文翻译最近对噻唑化物 抗病毒药物 的研究已确定化合物 RM5038 和 RM5021 是 乙型肝炎病毒 (HBV)、丙型肝炎病毒 (HCV) 和甲型流感病毒的有效抑制剂,尽管它们的开发受到水溶性差和快速代谢清除的限制。在这项工作中,使用更新的聚合合成方法和灵活的保护基策略合成了一系列新型卤素取代和4'-烷基砜噻唑化物类似物。DMPK 评估表明,3-X 卤素取代显着提高了水溶性,同时增强了代谢稳定性。在氯噻唑系列中,化合物 10g 和 10l 显示出大大改善的代谢稳定性、优异的水溶性(比母体药物提高了 10 倍)、低个位数微摩尔 抗病毒药物(甲型流感)活性和可接受的体外安全指数。对 4'-烷基砜噻唑烷的评估还揭示了体外 DMPK 特性的改善,同时保留了微摩尔 抗病毒药物 效力。SAR 分析表明,含有 2 至 3 个碳的短烷基砜取代基对于 抗病毒药物 活性是最佳的,而支链烷基可能提供额外的代谢优势。总之,化合物 10g、10l 和 15d 代表了进行体内分析和其他临床前评估的有希望的早期先导化合物。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究旨在解决开发噻唑化物 抗病毒药物 的挑战,该噻唑化物 乙型肝炎病毒 (HBV) 感染的 治疗 具有更高的效力、代谢稳定性和水溶性。
设计与对象
该研究涉及新型噻唑烷类似物的合成及其 抗病毒药物 抗 HBV 活性的评估,重点是优化其理化性质。
核心方法
研究人员使用更新的聚合合成方法和灵活的保护基策略合成了一系列卤素取代的4'-烷基砜噻唑化物类似物。然后他们评估了药物的 DMPK 特征和 抗病毒药物 的体外效力。
主要结果
DMPK评价表明,3-X卤素取代显着提高了水溶性和代谢稳定性。化合物 10g 和 10l 表现出大大改善的代谢稳定性、优异的水溶性(比母体药物提高 10 倍)以及针对甲型流感病毒的低个位数微摩尔 抗病毒药物 活性。
解释与创新
研究结果表明,特定的结构修饰可以增强噻唑烷类化合物的理化性质,而不影响其 抗病毒药物 效力,为 HBV 药物设计提供了一种新方法。
科研与公卫意义
这些结果为开发更有效的抗 HBV 的 抗病毒药物 药物奠定了基础,可能改善 治疗 的选择并减少当前噻唑类药物的局限性。
局限与证据强度
该研究仅限于体外评估,证据仅基于摘要数据,可能无法完全反映体内疗效或安全性。这些发现的可信度是中等的。

Defining the Structure-Activity Relationships of Thiazolide Antivirals: Balancing Potency, Metabolic Stability, and Aqueous Solubility.

Authors: Joshua Taujanskas, Sophie Pate, Andrew V Stachulski, Sara Piacentini, Simone La Frazia, M Gabriella Santoro, Paul M O'Neill
Original abstract (English)
Recent studies on thiazolide antivirals have identified compounds RM5038 and RM5021 as potent inhibitors of hepatitis B virus (HBV), hepatitis C virus (HCV), and influenza A, although their development has been limited by poor aqueous solubility and rapid metabolic clearance. In this work, a series of novel halogen-substituted and 4'-alkylsulfone thiazolide analogs were synthesized using updated convergent synthetic approaches and flexible protecting group strategies. DMPK evaluation demonstrated that 3-X halogen substitution significantly improved aqueous solubility in parallel to enhancing metabolic stability. Within the chlorothiazolide series, compounds 10gand 10l displayed much improved metabolic stability profiles, excellent aqueous solubility representing a 10-fold improvement over the parent drug, together with low single-digit micromolar antiviral (influenza A) activity and acceptable in vitro safety indices. Evaluation of the 4'-alkylsulfone thiazolides also revealed improved in vitro DMPK profiles while retaining micromolar antiviral potency. SAR analysis indicated that short alkyl sulfone substituents containing two to three carbons are optimal for antiviral activity, while branched alkyl groups may provide additional metabolic advantages. In summary, compounds 10g, 10l, and 15d represent promising early leads for progression to in vivo profiling and additional preclinical evaluation.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to address the challenge of developing thiazolide antivirals with improved potency, metabolic stability, and aqueous solubility for the treatment of hepatitis B virus (HBV) infections.
Design and population
The study involved the synthesis of novel thiazolide analogs and their evaluation for antiviral activity against HBV, with a focus on optimizing their physicochemical properties.
Core methods
The researchers synthesized a series of halogen-substituted and 4'-alkylsulfone thiazolide analogs using updated convergent synthetic approaches and flexible protecting group strategies. They then evaluated the drugs' DMPK profiles and antiviral potency in vitro.
Main results
DMPK evaluation showed that 3-X halogen substitution significantly improved aqueous solubility and metabolic stability. Compounds 10g and 10l demonstrated much improved metabolic stability profiles, excellent aqueous solubility (10-fold improvement over the parent drug), and low single-digit micromolar antiviral activity against influenza A.
Interpretation and novelty
The findings indicate that specific structural modifications can enhance the physicochemical properties of thiazolides without compromising their antiviral potency, offering a novel approach to drug design for HBV.
Scientific and public-health significance
These results provide a foundation for the development of more effective antiviral agents against HBV, potentially improving treatment options and reducing the limitations of current thiazolide drugs.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited to in vitro evaluations, and the evidence is based on abstract-only data, which may not fully reflect in vivo efficacy or safety. The confidence in these findings is moderate.

工程外泌体传递 FOSB siRNA 减轻 乙型肝炎病毒 相关肾炎的足细胞损伤

Engineered Exosomes Delivering FOSB siRNA Alleviate Podocyte Injury in Hepatitis B Virus-Associated Nephritis
作者： Jie Li, Yongzheng Hu, Xinyi Hao, Yabin Wang, Wei Jiang
摘要中文翻译摘要 乙型肝炎病毒相关性肾小球肾炎(HBV-GN)是HBV感染常见的肝外表现,也是我国主要的继发性肾小球疾病。上调 FOSB 介导 HBx 诱导的足细胞损伤,而沉默 FOSB 可以逆转这种病理变化。骨髓间充质干细胞源性外泌体(BMSC-Exos)表现出优异的生物相容性,但其临床应用因循环半衰期短和载药量不足而受到限制。在此,我们构建了用 FOSB siRNA 封装的 Cyclo(RGDfC) 修饰的 BMSC-Exos,并研究了其针对 HBx 介导的足细胞损伤的治疗潜力。对 HBV-GN 患者和健康志愿者肾脏样本的转录组分析将 FOSB 筛选为关键致病基因。我们建立了体外 HBx 过表达足细胞模型和体内 HBx 转基因小鼠模型。通过 Cyclo(RGDfC) 和 siRNA 负载的共价偶联制备工程外泌体,然后进行综合表征。我们的数据证实 HBx 诱导 FOSB 过度表达。Cyclo(RGDfC) 修饰的工程外泌体可以被足细胞内化。相对于未修饰的外泌体,这些构建体显着下调 FOSB,减轻足细胞凋亡和炎症反应,并改善转基因小鼠的肾脏病变和功能障碍。总的来说,Cyclo(RGDfC) 修饰的加载 FOSB siRNA 的 BMSC-Exos 通过抑制 FOSB 依赖性足细胞损伤来阻碍 HBV-GN 进展,这代表了 HBV-GN 的前瞻性治疗策略。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
乙型肝炎病毒相关性肾小球肾炎(HBV-GN)是HBV感染的重要肝外表现,也是我国主要的继发性肾小球疾病。该研究旨在通过针对 FOSB 介导的足细胞损伤来探索 HBV-GN 的新治疗策略。
设计与对象
该研究涉及体外 HBx 过表达足细胞模型和体内 HBx 转基因小鼠模型。对 HBV-GN 患者和健康志愿者肾脏样本的转录组分析确定 FOSB 是关键致病基因。
核心方法
通过 Cyclo(RGDfC) 和 siRNA 负载的共价偶联制备工程外泌体,然后进行综合表征。在体外和体内模型中研究了这些外泌体的治疗潜力。
主要结果
我们的数据证实 HBx 诱导 FOSB 过度表达。Cyclo(RGDfC)修饰的工程外泌体被足细胞内化,并显着下调 FOSB,减轻足细胞凋亡和炎症反应,并改善转基因小鼠的肾脏病变和功能障碍。
解释与创新
研究结果表明,传递 FOSB siRNA 的工程外泌体可以有效抑制 FOSB 依赖性足细胞损伤,为 HBV-GN 提供一种有前景的治疗策略。
科研与公卫意义
这些结果为 HBV-GN 的 治疗 提供了一种潜在的新方法,可以为该疾病的未来研究和治疗开发提供信息。
局限与证据强度
骨髓间充质干细胞来源的外泌体由于其循环半衰期短和载药能力不足,在临床应用中受到限制。然而,该研究的结果得到了体外和体内模型的支持,为工程外泌体的治疗潜力提供了一定的信心。

Engineered Exosomes Delivering FOSB siRNA Alleviate Podocyte Injury in Hepatitis B Virus-Associated Nephritis

Authors: Jie Li, Yongzheng Hu, Xinyi Hao, Yabin Wang, Wei Jiang
Original abstract (English)
Abstract Hepatitis B virus-associated glomerulonephritis (HBV-GN) is the common extrahepatic manifestation of HBV infection and a major secondary glomerular disease in China. Upregulated FOSB mediates podocyte injury induced by HBx, whereas silencing FOSB can reverse this pathological change. Bone marrow mesenchymal stem cell-derived exosomes (BMSC-Exos) exhibit excellent biocompatibility, while their clinical application is limited by short circulating half-life and insufficient drug loading capacity. Herein, we constructed Cyclo(RGDfC)-modified BMSC-Exos encapsulated with FOSB siRNA and investigated its therapeutic potential against HBx-mediated podocyte damage. Transcriptomic analysis of renal samples from HBV-GN patients and healthy volunteers screened FOSB as a key pathogenic gene. We established an in vitro HBx-overexpressing podocyte model and an in vivo HBx transgenic mouse model. Engineered exosomes were prepared via covalent coupling of Cyclo(RGDfC) and siRNA loading, followed by comprehensive characterization. Our data verified that HBx induced FOSB overexpression. Cyclo(RGDfC)-modified engineered exosomes can be internalized by podocytes. Relative to unmodified exosomes, these constructs significantly downregulated FOSB, alleviated podocyte apoptosis and inflammatory response, and ameliorated renal lesions and dysfunction in transgenic mice. Collectively, Cyclo(RGDfC)-modified FOSB siRNA-loaded BMSC-Exos hinder HBV-GN progression through inhibiting FOSB-dependent podocyte injury, which represents a prospective therapeutic strategy for HBV-GN.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Hepatitis B virus-associated glomerulonephritis (HBV-GN) is a significant extrahepatic manifestation of HBV infection and a major secondary glomerular disease in China. The study aimed to explore a novel therapeutic strategy for HBV-GN by targeting FOSB-mediated podocyte injury.
Design and population
The study involved an in vitro HBx-overexpressing podocyte model and an in vivo HBx transgenic mouse model. Transcriptomic analysis of renal samples from HBV-GN patients and healthy volunteers identified FOSB as a key pathogenic gene.
Core methods
Engineered exosomes were prepared by covalent coupling of Cyclo(RGDfC) and siRNA loading, followed by comprehensive characterization. The therapeutic potential of these exosomes was investigated in both in vitro and in vivo models.
Main results
Our data verified that HBx induced FOSB overexpression. Cyclo(RGDfC)-modified engineered exosomes were internalized by podocytes and significantly downregulated FOSB, alleviated podocyte apoptosis and inflammatory response, and ameliorated renal lesions and dysfunction in transgenic mice.
Interpretation and novelty
The findings demonstrate that engineered exosomes delivering FOSB siRNA can effectively inhibit FOSB-dependent podocyte injury, offering a promising therapeutic strategy for HBV-GN.
Scientific and public-health significance
These results provide a potential new approach for the treatment of HBV-GN, which could inform future research and therapeutic development for this condition.
Limitations and evidence strength
Bone marrow mesenchymal stem cell-derived exosomes have limitations in clinical application due to their short circulating half-life and insufficient drug loading capacity. However, the study's results are supported by both in vitro and in vivo models, providing moderate confidence in the therapeutic potential of the engineered exosomes.

A 高优先级学术文献 · 研究A high priorityAcademic literature · Research

华支睾吸虫共感染增强 慢性乙型肝炎 患者多形核骨髓源性抑制细胞的扩增和代谢重编程

Clonorchis sinensis co-infection potentiates polymorphonuclear-myeloid-derived suppressor cells expansion and metabolic reprogramming in chronic hepatitis B patients
作者： Jie-Ru Qiu, Hong-Bin Zhang, Mei Shang, Jue Xu, Yue-Chun Fu, Yuan Liao, Bo Hu, Hui-Min Dong
摘要中文翻译背景已知华支睾吸虫(C. sinensis)合并感染会加速乙型肝炎病毒(HBV)患者的疾病进展并诱导免疫耐受,特别是在两种感染流行的地区。代谢重编程已成为免疫抑制的关键驱动因素。这些代谢变化通常是由骨髓源性抑制细胞(MDSC)精心策划的,这些细胞在慢性感染期间会扩张以促进免疫耐受。中华丝虫合并感染会重新编程代谢以有利于 MDSC 相关的抑制,而这一过程与正常肝酶水平的关系仍不清楚。目的 探讨中华支原体合并感染是否会诱导肝酶水平正常的 HBV 患者发生 MDSC 相关的代谢重编程。方法 在这项横断面研究中,总共招募了 117 名参与者,并分为四组:健康对照(n = 30)、单一感染中华支原体的参与者(n = 29)、单一感染 HBV 的参与者(n = 28)和混合感染的参与者(n = 30)。使用化学发光免疫测定法测量血清细胞因子谱,使用液相色谱-串联质谱法测量氨基酸代谢物,并使用流式细胞术测量MDSC子集。进行 Spearman 相关性和中介分析来探讨炎症、代谢和免疫之间的关系。结果 HBV单一感染和合并感染患者的肝酶和HBV DNA水平无统计学差异。然而,与HBV单一感染组相比,合并感染组的炎症细胞因子白细胞介素6(IL-6)和肿瘤坏死因子-α(TNF-α)水平较高,同时干扰素-γ(IFN-γ)水平明显较低。流式细胞术检测显示,多形核MDSC(PMN-MDSC)在共感染组中表现出最大的扩增,并且其比例与IL-6和TNF-α水平密切相关。在代谢方面,共同感染组的鸟氨酸与精氨酸的比率显着较高。中介分析表明,PMN-MDSC 约占 IL-6 对 IFN-γ 抑制总效应的 45%。中华线虫合并感染通过 IL-6/TNF-α 连接的 PMN-MDSC 扩增和鸟氨酸与精氨酸比率升高与免疫抑制相关,导致尽管肝酶水平正常,但 IFN-γ 的产生受到抑制。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究旨在探讨华支睾吸虫合并感染是否会在肝酶水平正常的 HBV 患者中诱导 MDSC 相关的代谢重编程。背景强调,已知中华支原体合并感染会加速乙型肝炎患者的疾病进展并诱导免疫耐受,特别是在流行地区。
设计与对象
这项横断面研究纳入了 117 名参与者,分为四组:健康对照(n = 30)、中华支原体单一感染参与者(n = 29)、HBV 单一感染参与者(n = 28)和混合感染参与者(n = 30)。
核心方法
使用化学发光免疫测定法测量血清细胞因子谱,使用液相色谱-串联质谱法测量氨基酸代谢物,并使用流式细胞术测量MDSC子集。流式细胞术检测显示,多形核MDSC(PMN-MDSC)在共感染组中表现出最大的扩增,并且其比例与IL-6和TNF-α水平密切相关。
主要结果
HBV单一感染和合并感染患者的肝酶和HBV DNA水平无统计学差异。然而,共同感染组的炎症细胞因子:白细胞介素-6 (IL-6) 和肿瘤坏死因子-α (TNF-α) 水平较高,同时干扰素-γ (IFN-γ) 水平明显较低。中介分析表明,PMN-MDSC 约占 IL-6 对 IFN-γ 抑制总效应的 45%。
解释与创新
作者解释说,中华线虫合并感染与通过 IL-6/TNF-α 连接的 PMN-MDSC 扩增和鸟氨酸与精氨酸比率升高而产生的免疫抑制有关,导致尽管肝酶水平正常,但 IFN-γ 的产生受到抑制。这一发现强调了共感染可能调节 HBV 患者 免疫应答 的新机制。
科研与公卫意义
这些发现可能有助于了解合并感染 HBV 患者的免疫调节,并可能指导未来基于免疫的治疗和针对合并感染情况的 监测 策略的研究。
局限与证据强度
该研究是横断面的,限制了其建立因果关系的能力。证据基于相关性和中介分析,可能无法完全捕捉感染与 免疫应答 之间复杂的相互作用。信心是中等的。

Clonorchis sinensis co-infection potentiates polymorphonuclear-myeloid-derived suppressor cells expansion and metabolic reprogramming in chronic hepatitis B patients

Authors: Jie-Ru Qiu, Hong-Bin Zhang, Mei Shang, Jue Xu, Yue-Chun Fu, Yuan Liao, Bo Hu, Hui-Min Dong
Original abstract (English)
BACKGROUND Clonorchis sinensis (C. sinensis ) co-infection is known to accelerate disease progression and induce immune tolerance in patients with hepatitis B virus (HBV), particularly in regions where both infections are endemic. Metabolic reprogramming has emerged as a key driver of immunosuppression. These metabolic shifts are often orchestrated by myeloid-derived suppressor cells (MDSCs), which expand during chronic infections to promote immune tolerance. sinensis co-infection reprograms metabolism to favor MDSC-linked suppression and how this process relates to normal liver enzyme levels remains unclear. AIM To investigate whether C. sinensis co-infection induces MDSC- linked metabolic reprogramming in patients with HBV and normal liver enzyme levels. METHODS In this cross-sectional study, a total of 117 participants were enrolled and assigned to four groups: Healthy controls (n = 30), C. sinensis mono-infected participants (n = 29), HBV mono-infected participants (n = 28), and co-infected participants (n = 30). Serum cytokine profiles were measured using chemiluminescence immunoassay, amino acid metabolites using liquid chromatography-tandem mass spectrometry, and MDSC subsets using flow cytometry. Spearman correlation and mediation analyses were performed to explore the relationships among inflammation, metabolism, and immunity. RESULTS HBV mono-infected and co-infected patients showed no statistically significant differences in liver enzyme and HBV DNA levels. However, compared with the HBV mono-infected group, the co-infected group had higher levels of the inflammatory cytokine: Interleukin-6 (IL-6) and tumor necrosis factor-α (TNF-α), accompanied by markedly lower interferon-γ (IFN-γ) levels. Flow cytometric assay revealed that polymorphonuclear MDSCs (PMN-MDSCs) showed the greatest expansion in the co-infected group, and their proportion correlated strongly with IL-6 and TNF-α levels. Metabolically, the co-infected group had a significantly higher ornithine-to-arginine ratio. Mediation analysis suggested that PMN-MDSCs accounted for approximately 45% of the total effect of IL-6 on IFN-γ suppression. sinensis co-infection was associated with immunosuppression through IL-6/TNF-α-linked PMN-MDSC expansion and elevated ornithine-to-arginine ratio, resulting in suppressed IFN-γ production despite normal liver enzyme levels.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to investigate whether Clonorchis sinensis co-infection induces MDSC-linked metabolic reprogramming in patients with HBV and normal liver enzyme levels. The background highlights that C. sinensis co-infection is known to accelerate disease progression and induce immune tolerance in HBV patients, particularly in endemic regions.
Design and population
This cross-sectional study enrolled 117 participants divided into four groups: Healthy controls (n = 30), C. sinensis mono-infected participants (n = 29), HBV mono-infected participants (n = 28), and co-infected participants (n = 30).
Core methods
Serum cytokine profiles were measured using chemiluminescence immunoassay, amino acid metabolites using liquid chromatography-tandem mass spectrometry, and MDSC subsets using flow cytometry. Flow cytometric assay revealed that polymorphonuclear MDSCs (PMN-MDSCs) showed the greatest expansion in the co-infected group, and their proportion correlated strongly with IL-6 and TNF-α levels.
Main results
HBV mono-infected and co-infected patients showed no statistically significant differences in liver enzyme and HBV DNA levels. However, the co-infected group had higher levels of the inflammatory cytokine: Interleukin-6 (IL-6) and tumor necrosis factor-α (TNF-α), accompanied by markedly lower interferon-γ (IFN-γ) levels. Mediation analysis suggested that PMN-MDSCs accounted for approximately 45% of the total effect of IL-6 on IFN-γ suppression.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpreted that C. sinensis co-infection was associated with immunosuppression through IL-6/TNF-α-linked PMN-MDSC expansion and elevated ornithine-to-arginine ratio, resulting in suppressed IFN-γ production despite normal liver enzyme levels. This finding highlights a novel mechanism by which co-infection may modulate immune responses in HBV patients.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings may inform the understanding of immune modulation in HBV patients with co-infections, potentially guiding future research on immune-based therapies and surveillance strategies for co-infection scenarios.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is cross-sectional, limiting its ability to establish causality. The evidence is based on correlations and mediation analyses, which may not fully capture the complex interactions between infections and immune responses. The confidence is moderate.

1990-2023 年,尽管中东和北非 21 个国家的乙型肝炎发病率有所下降,但乙型肝炎的残留负担:年龄模式和阿曼案例研究

Residual Hepatitis B Burden Despite Declining Rates Across 21 Middle East and North Africa Countries, 1990–2023: Age Patterns and a Case Study of Oman
作者： Khalid M. Al-Naamani, Sultan Albalushi, Mohamed El-Kassas, Hilal Alsabti, Siham AlSinani, Saleh A. Alqahtani, Khalid Alswat, Tarannum Behlim, Waleed Al-hamoudi, Faisal M. Sanai
摘要中文翻译在中东和北非 (MENA),乙型肝炎病毒 (HBV) 负担的年龄标准化率 (ASR) 可能正在下降,而中老年人的绝对负担却在上升。在这项研究中,我们旨在描述 21 个中东和北非国家的比率、计数和年龄分布的变化,并以阿曼为案例研究。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
尽管中东和北非 (MENA) 地区的感染率有所下降,但该研究旨在解决持续存在的 乙型肝炎病毒 (HBV) 负担,重点关注年龄模式并以阿曼为案例研究。
设计与对象
本研究分析了 21 个中东和北非国家的年龄标准化率 (ASR) 和 乙型肝炎病毒 (HBV) 感染的绝对负担,并选择阿曼作为案例研究来探讨具体趋势。
核心方法
该研究利用全球健康数据库的数据来描述特定国家和时间段内乙型肝炎病毒发病率、计数和年龄分布的变化。
主要结果
乙型肝炎病毒 (HBV) 负担的年龄标准化率 (ASR) 可能正在下降,但中东和北非 (MENA) 地区中老年人的绝对负担正在增加。
解释与创新
研究结果凸显了一个悖论,即乙型肝炎病毒负担按标准化计算下降,但老年人群中的绝对数量却上升,这表明需要有针对性的干预措施。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果强调了特定年龄 监测 的重要性以及解决中东和北非地区日益增长的乙型肝炎绝对负担的干预措施,从而为公共卫生战略提供了信息。
局限与证据强度
该研究依赖于汇总数据,不提供详细的统计分析,这可能会限制其结论的可信度。

Residual Hepatitis B Burden Despite Declining Rates Across 21 Middle East and North Africa Countries, 1990–2023: Age Patterns and a Case Study of Oman

Authors: Khalid M. Al-Naamani, Sultan Albalushi, Mohamed El-Kassas, Hilal Alsabti, Siham AlSinani, Saleh A. Alqahtani, Khalid Alswat, Tarannum Behlim, Waleed Al-hamoudi, Faisal M. Sanai
Original abstract (English)
Across the Middle East and North Africa (MENA), the age-standardized rates (ASRs) of hepatitis B virus (HBV) burden may be declining while the absolute burden rises among middle-aged and older adults. In this study, we aimed to characterize changes in rates, counts, and age distribution across 21 MENA countries, with Oman as a case study.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to address the persistent hepatitis B virus (HBV) burden despite declining rates in the Middle East and North Africa (MENA) region, focusing on age patterns and Oman as a case study.
Design and population
This study analyzed age-standardized rates (ASRs) and absolute burden of hepatitis B virus (HBV) infection across 21 MENA countries, with Oman selected as a case study to explore specific trends.
Core methods
The study utilized data from global health databases to characterize changes in HBV rates, counts, and age distribution across the specified countries and time period.
Main results
The age-standardized rates (ASRs) of hepatitis B virus (HBV) burden may be declining, but the absolute burden is increasing among middle-aged and older adults in the Middle East and North Africa (MENA) region.
Interpretation and novelty
The findings highlight a paradox where HBV burden declines in standardized terms but rises in absolute numbers among older populations, suggesting a need for targeted interventions.
Scientific and public-health significance
The results inform public health strategies by emphasizing the importance of age-specific surveillance and interventions to address the growing absolute burden of HBV in the MENA region.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study relies on aggregated data and does not provide detailed statistical analyses, which may limit the confidence in its conclusions.

干扰素治疗可降低 乙型肝炎病毒 相关肝细胞癌高风险患者的肝细胞癌 发病率

Interferon therapy reduces incidence of hepatocellular carcinoma in patients with high risk of hepatitis B virus-related hepatocellular carcinoma
作者： Miao Hao, Chen-Rui Liu, Meng Zhang, Xia-Qing Zhou, Zi-Cheng Jiang, Xiao-Hong Gao, Xin Zhang, Wen-Jun Wang, Yi-Kai Wang, Xiao-Li Jia
摘要中文翻译背景我国HCC患者中乙型肝炎病毒(HBV)相关肝细胞癌(HCC)患者比例高达85.69%。目前的抗HBV药物包括核苷(酸)类似物(NAs)和干扰素(IFN)。目的 比较 NAs 和 IFN 治疗对 HBV 相关 HCC 高危患者 HCC 发生的影响。方法 我们纳入了 368 例 HBV 相关 HCC 高危患者,其中 120 例接受皮下注射 IFN(联合或不联合 NA),构成 IFN 组。其余 248 名患者被分配到 NAs 组,并接受单独 NAs 的 治疗 治疗。结果 对于 HBV 相关 HCC 高风险患者,经过 5 年 发病率 期后,IFN 组的 HCC 发病率 为 2.54%,而 NAs 组的 发病率 显着较高,为 25.74%(P 结论 与 NAs 相比,基于 IFN 的治疗显着降低了高风险患者的 HCC 风险)乙型肝炎病毒相关的肝癌。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究旨在解决中国 HBV 相关肝细胞癌 (HCC) 的高 发病率 问题,并评估干扰素 (IFN) 治疗与核苷类似物 (NA) 对高危患者 HCC 发生的影响。
设计与对象
一项回顾性 队列研究 研究对 368 名 HBV 相关 HCC 高风险患者进行,其中 120 名患者接受 IFN 治疗(联合或不联合 NA),248 名患者仅接受 NA 治疗。
核心方法
患者入组并随访 5 年 治疗 期。使用统计分析对 IFN 组和 NAs 组之间的 HCC 发病率 进行监测和比较以确定显着性。
主要结果
治疗治疗5年后,IFN组的HCC 发病率为2.54%,而NAs组的发病率显着较高,为25.74%(P < 0.001)。
解释与创新
作者将这些发现解释为证据,表明在高危 HBV 患者中,与单独使用 NA 相比,基于 IFN 的治疗可显着降低 HCC 风险,强调了一种新的治疗方法。
科研与公卫意义
这些结果表明,基于 IFN 的治疗可能是减少 HBV 相关 HCC 高风险患者的 HCC 发病率 的更有效选择,可能为 治疗 指南和公共卫生策略提供信息。
局限与证据强度
该研究因其回顾性设计和潜在的混杂因素而受到限制,但由于 IFN 和 NA 组之间的清晰比较以及统计学上显着的结果,证据是强有力的。

Interferon therapy reduces incidence of hepatocellular carcinoma in patients with high risk of hepatitis B virus-related hepatocellular carcinoma

Authors: Miao Hao, Chen-Rui Liu, Meng Zhang, Xia-Qing Zhou, Zi-Cheng Jiang, Xiao-Hong Gao, Xin Zhang, Wen-Jun Wang, Yi-Kai Wang, Xiao-Li Jia
Original abstract (English)
BACKGROUND The proportion of patients with hepatitis B virus (HBV)-related hepatocellular carcinoma (HCC) is as high as 85.69% among HCC patients in China. Current anti-HBV drugs include nucleos(t)ide analogs (NAs) and interferon (IFN). AIM To compare the impact of NAs and IFN therapy on the occurrence of HCC in patients at high risk of HBV-related HCC. METHODS We enrolled 368 patients with high risk of HBV-related HCC, and 120 received subcutaneous injections of IFN, with or without NAs, constituting the IFN group. The remaining 248 patients were assigned to the NAs group, and received treatment with NAs alone. RESULTS For patients with a high risk of HBV-related HCC, after a 5-year treatment period, HCC incidence in the IFN group was 2.54%, while the NAs group showed a significantly higher incidence of 25.74% (P CONCLUSION IFN-based therapy, compared with NAs, significantly reduced the risk of HCC in patients with high risk of HBV-related HCC.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to address the high incidence of HBV-related hepatocellular carcinoma (HCC) in China and evaluate the impact of interferon (IFN) therapy versus nucleos(t)ide analogs (NAs) on HCC occurrence in high-risk patients.
Design and population
A retrospective cohort study was conducted on 368 patients with high risk of HBV-related HCC, with 120 patients receiving IFN therapy (with or without NAs) and 248 patients receiving NA therapy alone.
Core methods
Patients were enrolled and followed for a 5-year treatment period. HCC incidence was monitored and compared between the IFN group and the NAs group using statistical analysis to determine significance.
Main results
After 5 years of treatment, the HCC incidence in the IFN group was 2.54%, while the NAs group had a significantly higher incidence of 25.74% (P < 0.001).
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpreted these findings as evidence that IFN-based therapy significantly reduces HCC risk compared to NAs alone in high-risk HBV patients, highlighting a novel therapeutic approach.
Scientific and public-health significance
These results suggest that IFN-based therapy could be a more effective option for reducing HCC incidence in patients at high risk of HBV-related HCC, potentially informing treatment guidelines and public health strategies.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited by its retrospective design and potential for confounding factors, but the evidence is strong due to the clear comparison between IFN and NAs groups and the statistically significant results.

乙型和丁型肝炎:基于干扰素的治疗 - 用于临床试验或标准护理 - 回到未来?

Hepatitis B and D: Interferon-Based Therapy-For Clinical Trials or Standard of Care-Back to the Future?
作者： Zillah Cargill, Harry L A Janssen, Tarik Asselah, Kosh Agarwal
摘要中文翻译过去三十年来,干扰素 (IFN) 在慢性病毒性乙型肝炎和丁型肝炎的治疗中发挥了重要作用。核苷/核苷酸类似物(NUC)在抑制乙型肝炎病毒方面的广泛应用(其副作用较小),加上最近获得的用于丁型肝炎 治疗 的 bulevirtide 许可,意味着其临床使用进一步下降。聚乙二醇化干扰素α (peg-IFNα) 治疗 效果欠佳,耐受性差,仅在有限数量的患者中实现了乙型肝炎和丁型肝炎的持久清除。尽管如此,与单独使用 NUC 的患者相比,其在乙型肝炎中的使用显着增加了实现乙型肝炎表面抗原 (HBsAg) 消失的患者数量。有证据表明,peg-IFNα 还与慢性丁型肝炎的长期临床结果的整体改善相关,即使对于许多丁型肝炎病毒载量在停药后反弹的人来说也是如此。最近的临床试验重点关注 peg-IFNα 与新型 抗病毒药物 疗法的联合治疗,以实现乙型肝炎和丁型肝炎的有限治愈。本文重点介绍最新的研究进展,探讨 peg-IFNα 治疗 是否仍可能在乙型肝炎和丁型肝炎 治疗 中发挥重要作用。
查看综述五要素
范围与问题
过去三十年来,干扰素 (IFN) 在慢性病毒性乙型肝炎和丁型肝炎的治疗中发挥了重要作用。
证据基础与方法
核苷/核苷酸类似物(NUC)在抑制乙型肝炎病毒方面的广泛应用(其副作用较小),加上最近获得的用于丁型肝炎 治疗 的 bulevirtide 许可,意味着其临床使用进一步下降。
共识与结论
聚乙二醇化干扰素α (peg-IFNα) 治疗 效果欠佳,耐受性差,仅在有限数量的患者中实现了乙型肝炎和丁型肝炎的持久清除。
争议与缺口
尽管如此,与单独使用 NUC 的患者相比,其在乙型肝炎中的使用显着增加了实现乙型肝炎表面抗原 (HBsAg) 消失的患者数量。
科研与实践启示
有证据表明,peg-IFNα 还与慢性丁型肝炎的长期临床结果的整体改善相关,即使对于许多丁型肝炎病毒载量在停药后反弹的人来说也是如此。

Hepatitis B and D: Interferon-Based Therapy-For Clinical Trials or Standard of Care-Back to the Future?

Authors: Zillah Cargill, Harry L A Janssen, Tarik Asselah, Kosh Agarwal
Original abstract (English)
Interferon (IFN) has historically played a major role in chronic viral hepatitis B and D management over the last three decades. Widening utilisation of nucleoside/nucleotide analogues (NUCs) for hepatitis B viral suppression, which have a better side-effect profile, alongside the recent licensing of bulevirtide for treatment of hepatitis D, means that its clinical use has further declined. Pegylated-interferon alpha (peg-IFNα) treatment is suboptimal with poor tolerability and achieves durable clearance of hepatitis B and D in only a limited number of patients. Nevertheless, its use in hepatitis B has significantly increased the number of patients achieving hepatitis B surface antigen (HBsAg) loss compared to those on NUCs alone. Evidence has shown that peg-IFNα is also associated with overall improved long-term clinical outcomes in chronic hepatitis D, even for the many whose hepatitis D viral load rebounds after discontinuation. Clinical trials have recently focused on peg-IFNα in combination with novel antiviral therapies in the goal for finite cure in hepatitis B and D. This article focuses on recent research advances, examining if peg-IFNα treatment may still play an important role in hepatitis B and D treatment.
View review five-element analysis
Scope and question
Interferon (IFN) has historically played a major role in chronic viral hepatitis B and D management over the last three decades.
Evidence base and method
Widening utilisation of nucleoside/nucleotide analogues (NUCs) for hepatitis B viral suppression, which have a better side-effect profile, alongside the recent licensing of bulevirtide for treatment of hepatitis D, means that its clinical use has further declined.
Consensus and conclusions
Pegylated-interferon alpha (peg-IFNα) treatment is suboptimal with poor tolerability and achieves durable clearance of hepatitis B and D in only a limited number of patients.
Controversies and gaps
Nevertheless, its use in hepatitis B has significantly increased the number of patients achieving hepatitis B surface antigen (HBsAg) loss compared to those on NUCs alone.
Research and practice implications
Evidence has shown that peg-IFNα is also associated with overall improved long-term clinical outcomes in chronic hepatitis D, even for the many whose hepatitis D viral load rebounds after discontinuation.

A 高优先级学术文献 · 综述A high priorityAcademic literature · Review

美国的 慢性乙型肝炎:当前形势、未满足的需求和未来方向。

Chronic Hepatitis B in the United States: Current Landscape, Unmet Needs and Future Directions.
作者： Chari Cohen, James Philip Esteban, Shravani Bobde, Robert J Wong
摘要中文翻译慢性乙型肝炎 (CHB) 感染是美国的一项重大公共卫生挑战,与及时 诊断、治疗、监测和疾病管理相关的大量需求未得到满足。这篇叙述性综述探讨了美国慢性乙型肝炎的情况,包括患者护理方面的差距、不断发展的治疗前景、血清学生物标志物在改善乙型肝炎患者 (PLWHB) 患者的治疗结果和经历方面的临床效用。在美国,乙型肝炎的诊断和治疗仍然不足,目前的努力不足以实现世界卫生组织相关的 2030 年消除目标。美国肝病研究协会和美国传染病学会的最新指南代表了北美的观点,标志着向扩大 治疗 资格标准和共同决策的转变。核苷(酸)类似物 (NA) 是美国目前的护理标准,但单独使用 NA 很少能实现 功能性治愈。针对 功能性治愈 的新型有限疗法,例如反义寡核苷酸、小干扰 RNA、免疫调节剂以及组合或序贯疗法,正在研究中。由于慢性乙型肝炎的不可预测性和复杂性,定量乙型肝炎表面抗原等生物标志物可为 治疗 决策提供预后价值,并有助于指导个体化慢性乙型肝炎管理。PLWHB 经历心理影响和健康相关问题,包括对 传播 和 乙型肝炎病毒 相关并发症的恐惧。改善 CHB 管理的机会包括增加筛查和与护理的联系、开发更多针对 功能性治愈 的 治疗、采用血清学生物标志物来告知 治疗 决策以及优先考虑 PLWHB 的生活经历和偏好。
查看综述五要素
范围与问题
慢性乙型肝炎 (CHB) 感染是美国的一项重大公共卫生挑战,与及时 诊断、治疗、监测和疾病管理相关的大量需求未得到满足。
证据基础与方法
这篇叙述性综述探讨了美国慢性乙型肝炎的情况,包括患者护理方面的差距、不断发展的治疗前景、血清学生物标志物在改善乙型肝炎患者 (PLWHB) 患者的治疗结果和经历方面的临床效用。
共识与结论
在美国,乙型肝炎的诊断和治疗仍然不足,目前的努力不足以实现世界卫生组织相关的 2030 年消除目标。
争议与缺口
改善 CHB 管理的机会包括增加筛查和与护理的联系、开发更多针对 功能性治愈 的 治疗、采用血清学生物标志物来告知 治疗 决策以及优先考虑 PLWHB 的生活经历和偏好。
科研与实践启示
由于慢性乙型肝炎的不可预测性和复杂性,定量乙型肝炎表面抗原等生物标志物可为 治疗 决策提供预后价值,并有助于指导个体化慢性乙型肝炎管理。

Chronic Hepatitis B in the United States: Current Landscape, Unmet Needs and Future Directions.

Authors: Chari Cohen, James Philip Esteban, Shravani Bobde, Robert J Wong
Original abstract (English)
Chronic hepatitis B (CHB) infection represents a major public health challenge in the US, with substantial unmet needs related to timely diagnosis, treatment, monitoring and disease management. This narrative review explores the CHB landscape in the US, including gaps in patient care, the evolving therapeutic landscape, the clinical utility of serological biomarkers to improve outcomes and the experiences of people living with hepatitis B (PLWHB). Hepatitis B remains underdiagnosed and undertreated in the US, and current efforts are insufficient to meet the associated World Health Organization 2030 elimination targets. Latest guidelines from the American Association for the Study of Liver Diseases and the Infectious Diseases Society of America, representing a North American perspective, signify a shift towards expanded treatment eligibility criteria and shared decision-making. Nucleos(t)ide analogues (NAs) are the current standard of care in the US, but functional cure is rarely achieved with NAs alone. Novel finite therapies targeting functional cure, such as antisense oligonucleotides, small interfering RNAs, immune modulators and combination or sequential regimens, are under investigation. Because of the unpredictable, complex nature of CHB, biomarkers such as quantitative hepatitis B surface antigen offer prognostic value for treatment decisions and could help guide individualised CHB management. PLWHB experience psychological impacts and health-related concerns, including fear of transmission and hepatitis B virus-related complications. Opportunities for improvement of CHB management include increased screening and linkage to care, development of more treatments targeting functional cure, adoption of serological biomarkers to inform treatment decisions and prioritising the lived experiences and preferences of PLWHB.
View review five-element analysis
Scope and question
Chronic hepatitis B (CHB) infection represents a major public health challenge in the US, with substantial unmet needs related to timely diagnosis, treatment, monitoring and disease management.
Evidence base and method
This narrative review explores the CHB landscape in the US, including gaps in patient care, the evolving therapeutic landscape, the clinical utility of serological biomarkers to improve outcomes and the experiences of people living with hepatitis B (PLWHB).
Consensus and conclusions
Hepatitis B remains underdiagnosed and undertreated in the US, and current efforts are insufficient to meet the associated World Health Organization 2030 elimination targets.
Controversies and gaps
Opportunities for improvement of CHB management include increased screening and linkage to care, development of more treatments targeting functional cure, adoption of serological biomarkers to inform treatment decisions and prioritising the lived experiences and preferences of PLWHB.
Research and practice implications
Because of the unpredictable, complex nature of CHB, biomarkers such as quantitative hepatitis B surface antigen offer prognostic value for treatment decisions and could help guide individualised CHB management.

乙型肝炎病毒作为癌症一级预防策略:机制、结果与模型扩展

Hepatitis B Vaccine as a Primary Prevention Strategy for Cancer: Mechanisms, Outcomes and Model Extension
作者： Qingyang Xia
摘要中文翻译通过预防肝脏慢性感染,针对乙型肝炎病毒(HBV)的疫苗接种可以预防肝癌。本文综述了HBV在肝细胞癌中的作用、疫苗接种在预防HBV感染及相关癌症中的效果,以及中国经验对其他癌症预防计划的启示。HBV通过诱导肝脏慢性炎症、将病毒DNA整合到肝脏DNA中以及通过病毒蛋白改变细胞调控来促进癌症发生。中国的儿童疫苗接种计划显著降低了年轻儿童的感染率。来自启东试验的扩展数据也表明原发性肝癌有所减少;然而,由于癌症病例数量较少,确切的减少程度尚不清楚。与人乳头瘤病毒(HPV)疫苗接种的比较显示,HBV疫苗接种的生物学原理可以应用于其他感染,但疫苗接种的实施需与目标人群的年龄和具体情况相匹配。中国于2025年将HPV疫苗接种纳入国家计划是一项政策变化。通过及时的疫苗接种、可及的医疗服务以及对高危人群的持续照护,可以有效防止疾病传播。
查看综述五要素
范围与问题
综述聚焦于乙型肝炎病毒(HBV)在肝细胞癌中的作用,疫苗接种在预防HBV感染及其相关癌症中的有效性,以及中国儿童疫苗接种计划对癌症预防策略的启示。
证据基础与方法
本文是一篇综述,探讨了HBV促进癌症的生物学机制、疫苗接种计划的结果以及从中国经验中获得的启示。它并未明确报告所使用的数据库、检索日期或纳入研究的资格标准。
共识与结论
HBV通过慢性炎症、将病毒DNA整合到肝脏DNA中以及通过病毒蛋白改变细胞调控来促进癌症发生。中国的儿童疫苗接种计划显著降低了年轻儿童的HBV感染率,而启东试验的扩展数据表明原发性肝癌有所减少,尽管由于癌症病例数量较少,确切的减少程度尚不确定。
争议与缺口
由于癌症病例数量有限,启东试验中肝癌减少的确切程度尚未明确。在将HBV疫苗接种的生物学原理应用于其他感染(如人乳头瘤病毒(HPV))时,还需要考虑目标人群的年龄和具体情况。
科研与实践启示
综述建议将疫苗接种计划扩展至包括HPV,如中国于2025年所做的那样,这可能是一项增强癌症预防的政策变化。它还强调了及时的疫苗接种、可及的医疗服务以及对高危人群的持续照护对于有效防止疾病传播的重要性。

Hepatitis B Vaccine as a Primary Prevention Strategy for Cancer: Mechanisms, Outcomes and Model Extension

Authors: Qingyang Xia
Original abstract (English)
Vaccination against Hepatitis B can prevent liver cancer by preventing chronic infection of the liver. This paper reviews the role of hepatitis B virus (HBV) in hepatocellular carcinoma, cancer prevention via vaccination, and lessons learned from China's experience for other cancer-prevention programmes. HBV promotes cancer by inducing chronic inflammation in the host, integrating viral DNA into the host's DNA, and altering cell regulation through viral proteins. China's childhood vaccination programme has reduced infections among young children significantly. Extended data from the Qidong trial have also indicated a decrease in primary liver cancer; however, due to a small number of cancer cases, the exact extent remains unknown. Comparison with human papillomavirus (HPV) vaccination shows that the biological principle can be applied to infections, but delivery needs to match the age and circumstances of the target population. China's addition of HPV vaccination to the national programme in 2025 is a policy change. Disease spread can be effectively prevented through timely vaccination, accessible medical services, and ongoing care for high-risk groups.
View review five-element analysis
Scope and question
The review focuses on the role of Hepatitis B virus (HBV) in hepatocellular carcinoma, the effectiveness of vaccination in preventing HBV infection and associated cancer, and the implications of China's childhood vaccination programme for cancer prevention strategies.
Evidence base and method
The paper is a review that examines the biological mechanisms of HBV in promoting cancer, the outcomes of vaccination programmes, and the lessons learned from China's experience. It does not explicitly report on the databases used, search dates, or eligibility criteria for included studies.
Consensus and conclusions
HBV promotes cancer through chronic inflammation, integration of viral DNA into host DNA, and alteration of cell regulation via viral proteins. China's childhood vaccination programme has significantly reduced HBV infections among young children, and extended data from the Qidong trial suggest a decrease in primary liver cancer, although the exact extent remains uncertain due to the small number of cancer cases.
Controversies and gaps
The exact extent of liver cancer reduction from the Qidong trial is not well established due to the limited number of cancer cases. There is also a need to consider the age and circumstances of the target population when applying biological principles from HBV vaccination to other infections, such as human papillomavirus (HPV).
Research and practice implications
The review suggests that expanding vaccination programmes to include HPV, as done in China in 2025, could be a policy change to enhance cancer prevention. It also highlights the importance of timely vaccination, accessible medical services, and ongoing care for high-risk groups to effectively prevent disease spread.

不要放弃B:在无替诺福韦方案治疗HIV和预防中乙型肝炎病毒(HBV)感染及再激活的风险

Don't Drop the B: Risk of Hepatitis B Virus Infection and Reactivation in the Era of Tenofovir-Sparing Regimens for Treatment and Prevention of HIV.
作者： Tzu-Hao Lee, Debika Bhattacharya, H Nina Kim
摘要中文翻译无替诺福韦抗逆转录病毒治疗(ART)和用于人类免疫缺陷病毒(HIV)的暴露前预防(PrEP)正日益被使用。然而,这些方案可能因对乙型肝炎病毒(HBV)缺乏完全活性而增加新HBV感染或再激活的风险。风险的大小和机制可能因基线HBV血清学特征而异,因此在更换方案前需要与患者进行讨论。重要的是,这些风险可以通过HBV筛查、疫苗接种和换方案后的监测来缓解。本文综合不同临床情境的证据,提出一个实用的工作流程,以减少在转向无替诺福韦方案过程中潜在但可预防的HBV风险。最后,我们强调知识空白并概述未来研究的重点。在决定转向无替诺福韦方案的ART或PrEP时,不应仅从HIV疗效和便利性的角度出发,还必须纳入HBV预防和再激活风险的考量。

范围与问题
本综述聚焦于在无替诺福韦方案治疗HIV和预防中乙型肝炎病毒(HBV)感染及再激活的风险。它探讨了这些方案对HBV的影响,特别是与基线HBV血清学特征的关系,以及在方案变更前进行患者特定讨论的必要性。
证据基础与方法
本综述综合了不同临床情境的证据,强调了通过HBV筛查、疫苗接种和换方案后的监测来缓解风险的重要性。它并未明确报告数据库、检索日期或纳入标准,但提出了一个实用的工作流程,用于管理在转向无替诺福韦方案过程中HBV的风险。
共识与结论
综述得出结论,通过HBV筛查、疫苗接种和换方案后的监测可以降低HBV感染或再激活的风险。它强调了风险的变异性取决于基线HBV血清学特征,并指出在无替诺福韦方案决策中必须纳入HBV预防策略。

Don't Drop the B: Risk of Hepatitis B Virus Infection and Reactivation in the Era of Tenofovir-Sparing Regimens for Treatment and Prevention of HIV.

Authors: Tzu-Hao Lee, Debika Bhattacharya, H Nina Kim
Original abstract (English)
Tenofovir-sparing antiretroviral therapy (ART) and pre-exposure prophylaxis (PrEP) for human immunodeficiency virus (HIV) are increasingly used. However, these regimens may increase the risk of new hepatitis B virus (HBV) infection or reactivation due to lack of full activity against HBV. The magnitude and mechanism of risk can vary by baseline HBV serologic profile and need to be discussed with the patient before switching regimens. Importantly, these risks can be mitigated through HBV screening, vaccination, and post-switch monitoring. Here, we synthesize the evidence across different clinical scenarios and propose a practical workflow to reduce the potential but preventable risk during transitions to tenofovir-sparing regimens. Finally, we highlight knowledge gaps and outline future research priorities. The decision to switch to tenofovir-sparing ART or PrEP should not be made solely through the lens of HIV efficacy and convenience and must incorporate HBV prevention and reactivation risk.
View review five-element analysis
Scope and question
The review focuses on the risk of hepatitis B virus (HBV) infection and reactivation in the context of tenofovir-sparing regimens for HIV treatment and prevention. It addresses the impact of these regimens on HBV, particularly in relation to baseline HBV serologic profiles and the need for patient-specific discussions before regimen changes.
Evidence base and method
The review synthesizes evidence from various clinical scenarios, emphasizing the importance of HBV screening, vaccination, and post-switch monitoring to mitigate risks. It does not explicitly report on databases, search dates, or eligibility criteria, but it outlines a practical workflow for managing HBV risks during transitions to tenofovir-sparing regimens.
Consensus and conclusions
The review concludes that the risk of HBV infection or reactivation can be reduced through HBV screening, vaccination, and post-switch monitoring. It highlights the variability of risk based on baseline HBV serologic profiles and the necessity of incorporating HBV prevention strategies into decision-making for tenofovir-sparing regimens.
Controversies and gaps
The review identifies knowledge gaps and outlines future research priorities, particularly in understanding the mechanisms of HBV risk and the effectiveness of mitigation strategies. It also notes that the decision to switch to tenofovir-sparing regimens should not be based solely on HIV efficacy and convenience but must consider HBV prevention and reactivation risks.
Research and practice implications
The review emphasizes the need for HBV screening, vaccination, and post-switch monitoring as critical steps in reducing the risk of HBV infection or reactivation. It also suggests that future research should focus on clarifying the mechanisms of HBV risk and improving strategies for HBV prevention in the context of tenofovir-sparing regimens.

发病率和非洲人群中与新发乙型肝炎病毒感染相关的因素:系统综述

Incidence and Factors Associated With Incident Hepatitis B Virus Infection in African Populations: A Systematic Review
作者： Israa Mohammed Ali
摘要中文翻译摘要 背景 发病率(HBV)感染仍然是非洲的重要公共卫生问题,持续的传播导致慢性肝病及相关并发症的负担。尽管HBV在非洲人群中的患病率已被广泛研究,但关于新发HBV感染及其相关因素的证据则较为不一致。本系统综述旨在综合非洲人群中新发HBV感染的发病率及其相关因素的现有证据。方法 通过PubMed进行系统文献检索,使用以下检索策略:("hepatitis B" OR HBV) AND (incident OR 发病率 OR seroconversion OR "new infection") AND (Africa OR African)。纳入标准为:研究对象为非洲人群,且报告了基于纵向或重复检测的新发HBV感染、HBV血清转换或发病率。研究根据预定义的纳入标准进行筛选,并从符合标准的全文研究中提取相关数据。使用Newcastle–Ottawa量表(NOS)评估队列研究研究中的偏倚风险。由于研究人群、环境、研究设计、随访时间和结局定义存在显著异质性,因此采用叙述性综合而非元分析来汇总结果。结果 有五项研究符合纳入标准,分别来自刚果民主共和国、科特迪瓦、布基纳法索、博茨瓦纳和马拉维。这些研究主要针对献血者,其中一项研究评估了未接受治疗的HIV感染者。报告的HBV 发病率在不同环境中差异较大。刚果民主共和国的发病率为每1000人年25.4,博茨瓦纳为每100人年3.6,马拉维为每1000人年13.7。在科特迪瓦,HBV血清转换的发病率从1992–1994年的每100人年7.3下降到2010–2012年的每100人年0.2。布基纳法索的研究也报告了重复献血者中新发HBV感染的情况。与新发感染相关的因素在不同研究中存在差异,这些差异根据献血类型、年龄、地理环境和人群特征而有所不同。然而,结果并不足够一致,无法确定非洲人群中统一的风险因素集。结论 新发HBV感染在非洲的多个环境中仍持续发生,尽管发病率在不同人群和研究期间存在显著差异。现有证据有限且异质性较大,主要来自献血者人群,而一般成人人群的代表性相对不足。需要进一步进行纵向研究,采用标准化定义和更广泛的人群样本,以更好地描述非洲地区新发HBV感染的发病率及其决定因素。

在科特迪瓦,HBV血清转换的发病率从1992–1994年的每100人年7.3下降到2010–2012年的每100人年0.2。

Incidence and Factors Associated With Incident Hepatitis B Virus Infection in African Populations: A Systematic Review

Authors: Israa Mohammed Ali
Original abstract (English)
Abstract Background Hepatitis B virus (HBV) infection remains an important public health concern in Africa, where ongoing transmission contributes to the burden of chronic liver disease and related complications. Although HBV prevalence in African populations has been widely studied, evidence regarding incident HBV infection and factors associated with newly acquired infection is less consistently characterized. This systematic review aimed to synthesize the available evidence on the incidence of incident HBV infection and associated factors among populations in Africa. Methods A systematic literature search was conducted in PubMed using the search strategy: "("hepatitis B" OR HBV) AND (incident OR incidence OR seroconversion OR "new infection") AND (Africa OR African)". Studies were eligible if they were conducted in African populations and reported incident HBV infection, HBV seroconversion, or incidence based on longitudinal or repeated testing among adults. Studies were screened according to predefined eligibility criteria, and relevant data were extracted from eligible full-text studies. Risk of bias was assessed using the Newcastle–Ottawa Scale (NOS) for cohort studies.. Due to substantial heterogeneity In study populations, settings, study designs, follow-up periods, and outcome definitions, findings were synthesized narratively rather than through meta-analysis. Results Five studies conducted in the Democratic Republic of the Congo, Côte d’Ivoire, Burkina Faso, Botswana, and Malawi met the inclusion criteria. The studies included predominantly blood donors, while one study evaluated HIV-infected treatment-naïve adults. Reported HBV incidence varied considerably across settings. Incidence was 25.4 per 1000 person-years in the Democratic Republic of the Congo, 3.6 per 100 person-years in Botswana, and 13.7 per 1000 person-years in Malawi. In Côte d’Ivoire, HBV seroconversion incidence decreased from 7.3 per 100 person-years in 1992–1994 to 0.2 per 100 person-years in 2010–2012. The Burkina Faso study also reported incident HBV infection among repeat blood donors. Factors associated with incident infection varied between studies, with differences observed according to donor type, age, geographical setting, and population characteristics. However, findings were not sufficiently consistent to identify a single set of risk factors across African populations. Conclusion Incident HBV infection continues to occur among adults in several African settings, although incidence varies substantially across populations and study periods. The available evidence is limited and heterogeneous, with a predominance of blood-donor populations and relatively limited representation of the general adult population. Further longitudinal studies using standardized definitions and broader population-based samples are needed to better characterize the incidence and determinants of newly acquired HBV infection in Africa.
View review five-element analysis
Scope and question
This systematic review aimed to synthesize the available evidence on the incidence of incident HBV infection and associated factors among populations in Africa.
Evidence base and method
Risk of bias was assessed using the Newcastle–Ottawa Scale (NOS) for cohort studies..
Consensus and conclusions
In Côte d’Ivoire, HBV seroconversion incidence decreased from 7.3 per 100 person-years in 1992–1994 to 0.2 per 100 person-years in 2010–2012.
Controversies and gaps
The available evidence is limited and heterogeneous, with a predominance of blood-donor populations and relatively limited representation of the general adult population.
Research and practice implications
Conclusion Incident HBV infection continues to occur among adults in several African settings, although incidence varies substantially across populations and study periods.

乙型肝炎病毒和丁型肝炎病毒的实验室模型:寻找细胞允许性及限制因子

Laboratory Models of Hepatitis B and D Viruses: Search for Cell Permissiveness and Restriction Factors
作者： Artemiy P. Fedulov, Olga N. Ivanova, Vladimir P. Chulanov, Dmitry S. Kostyushev, Alexander V. Ivanov, Ольга Николаевна Иванова
摘要中文翻译
查看综述五要素
范围与问题
本综述聚焦于人类乙型肝炎病毒(HBV)感染、其基因型及相关疾病。核心问题是评估HBV及其卫星病毒丁型肝炎病毒(HDV)的实验室模型,特别是关于细胞允许性和限制因子。
证据基础与方法
本综述是对HBV和HDV实验室模型的系统评估。它讨论了使用人类原代肝细胞、肝祖细胞HepaRG、诱导多能干细胞(iPS)衍生的肝细胞样细胞以及过表达NTCP受体的肝癌细胞。综述未明确报告元分析或汇总估计,但总结了现有方法和关于限制因子的数据。
共识与结论
综述得出结论,新的治疗手段和对病毒限制因子的理解受到有效细胞和动物模型缺乏的限制。它指出当前模型中大多数细胞不具有允许性,并且感染率受到肝细胞极化、NTCP受体表达不足以及存在其他共受体和限制因子的限制,这些因子主要在病毒进入阶段起作用。
争议与缺口
关于限制HBV和HDV感染率的确切机制尚无共识。证据表明肝细胞极化和NTCP受体表达水平是关键因素,但其他共受体和限制因子的存在仍未解决。综述还指出,缺乏有效的模型阻碍了研究进展。
科研与实践启示
综述暗示需要改进细胞和动物模型,以更好地理解HBV和HDV的感染机制,并开发有效的治疗手段。它还建议应研究肝细胞极化、NTCP受体表达及其他限制因子,以增强研究和治疗策略。

Laboratory Models of Hepatitis B and D Viruses: Search for Cell Permissiveness and Restriction Factors

Authors: Artemiy P. Fedulov, Olga N. Ivanova, Vladimir P. Chulanov, Dmitry S. Kostyushev, Alexander V. Ivanov, Ольга Николаевна Иванова
Original abstract (English)
Hepatitis B virus (HBV) is an oncogenic human pathogen that accounts for 250 mln cases of chronic hepatitis worldwide. Hepatitis delta virus (HDV) is its satellite that substantially aggravates liver disease. The development of new antivirals and the investigation of viral pathogenesis are limited by the absence of effective cellular and animal models. Current cell models are based on human primary hepatocytes, liver progenitor HepaRG cells, iPS-derived hepatocyte-like cells or various hepatoma cells overexpressing the putative NTCP receptor. However, in most of these models, a majority of cells are non-permissive. In this review, we briefly assess these models, summarize the approaches to the increasing infection rates, and discuss the data on proviral and antiviral (restriction) host factors. The data discussed suggest that rates of HBV and HDV infections are limited by hepatocyte polarization and insufficient expression of the NTCP receptor, as well as the existence of additional co-receptors and restriction factors that can act mostly on the virus entry stage.
View review five-element analysis
Scope and question
The review focuses on human hepatitis B virus (HBV) infection, its genotypes, and associated diseases. The central question is to evaluate laboratory models for HBV and its satellite hepatitis delta virus (HDV), particularly regarding cell permissiveness and restriction factors.
Evidence base and method
The review is a systematic assessment of laboratory models for HBV and HDV. It discusses the use of human primary hepatocytes, liver progenitor HepaRG cells, iPS-derived hepatocyte-like cells, and hepatoma cells overexpressing the NTCP receptor. The review does not explicitly report on meta-analysis or pooled estimates but summarizes existing approaches and data on host factors.
Consensus and conclusions
The review concludes that the development of new antivirals and understanding of viral pathogenesis are limited by the lack of effective cellular and animal models. It highlights that current models have a majority of non-permissive cells and that infection rates are constrained by hepatocyte polarization, insufficient NTCP receptor expression, and the presence of additional co-receptors and restriction factors primarily acting during the virus entry stage.
Controversies and gaps
There is a lack of consensus on the exact mechanisms limiting HBV and HDV infection rates. The evidence suggests that hepatocyte polarization and NTCP receptor expression levels are key factors, but the existence of additional co-receptors and restriction factors remains unresolved. The review also notes that the absence of effective models hinders research progress.
Research and practice implications
The review implies the need for improved cellular and animal models to better understand HBV and HDV infection mechanisms and to develop effective antivirals. It also suggests the importance of investigating hepatocyte polarization, NTCP receptor expression, and other restriction factors to enhance research and therapeutic strategies.

乙型肝炎病毒和乙型肝炎病毒/丁型肝炎病毒检测:意大利当前状况与展望

Testing for hepatitis C virus and hepatitis B virus/hepatitis D virus: Current scenarios and perspectives in Italy
作者： Francesca Colapietro, Maurizia Rossana Brunetto, Nicola Coppola, Stefano Fagiuoli, Pietro Lampertico
摘要中文翻译
查看综述五要素
范围与问题
Liver disease accounts for approximately 4% of all deaths worldwide, and viral hepatitis affects more than 300 million people.
证据基础与方法
本专家意见审查总结了意大利三级中心积极从事 HCV、HBV 和 HDV 医院检测的经验,并确定了改进检测和与护理联系的实际途径。
共识与结论
在意大利,丙型肝炎病毒 (HCV) 筛查受益于专门的政府资助,而 乙型肝炎病毒 (HBV) 和丁型肝炎病毒 (HDV) 检测则得到科学协会建议的支持,但并未受益于专门的国家公共卫生计划。
争议与缺口

Testing for hepatitis C virus and hepatitis B virus/hepatitis D virus: Current scenarios and perspectives in Italy

Authors: Francesca Colapietro, Maurizia Rossana Brunetto, Nicola Coppola, Stefano Fagiuoli, Pietro Lampertico
Original abstract (English)
Liver disease accounts for approximately 4% of all deaths worldwide, and viral hepatitis affects more than 300 million people. The World Health Organization aims to reduce new hepatitis infections by 90% and deaths by 65% by 2030 through vaccination and screening programs. In Italy, screening for hepatitis C virus (HCV) benefits from dedicated government funding, whereas testing for hepatitis B virus (HBV) and hepatitis D virus (HDV) is supported by recommendations from scientific societies but does not benefit from dedicated national public health programmes. This expert-opinion review summarises the experience of tertiary-level Italian centres actively engaged in hospital-based testing for HCV, HBV, and HDV and identifies practical pathways to improve testing and linkage to care. This article presents proposals to improve the effectiveness of viral hepatitis management in Italy by: (1) Strengthening coordination between hospital units and integrating information systems between general practitioners and hospitals; (2) Implementing a centralised reporting system and a structured referral network to ensure that patients with positive test results receive appropriate therapies in a timely manner; (3) Providing the resources needed to increase testing in public addiction treatment services (Servizi per le Dipendenze) and prisons; (4) Promoting screening in high-risk populations, including vulnerable and older populations; and (5) Implementing a national awareness campaign on viral hepatitis. These measures could help Italy make concrete progress towards eliminating HCV infection and controlling HBV/HDV infection within the next decade.

Liver disease accounts for approximately 4% of all deaths worldwide, and viral hepatitis affects more than 300 million people.

Consensus and conclusions

Controversies and gaps
The World Health Organization aims to reduce new hepatitis infections by 90% and deaths by 65% by 2030 through vaccination and screening programs.
Research and practice implications
These measures could help Italy make concrete progress towards eliminating HCV infection and controlling HBV/HDV infection within the next decade.

慢性乙型肝炎 中代谢功能障碍相关的脂肪肝和 抗病毒药物 反应:系统评价和荟萃分析

Metabolic dysfunction-associated fatty liver and antiviral response in chronic hepatitis B: A systematic review and meta-analysis
作者： Jia Liu, Peng Zhang, Ke-Zhen Hu, Zhi-Hui Zhao, Yong-Ning Xin, Yan-Zhen Bi
摘要中文翻译
查看综述五要素
范围与问题
该综述旨在评估 MAFLD 对 抗病毒药物 治疗 反应和 慢性乙型肝炎 (CHB) 患者长期临床结果的影响。核心问题是 MAFLD 是否影响 CHB 患者对 抗病毒药物 治疗的病毒学、生化和临床反应。
证据基础与方法
本次系统评价和荟萃分析遵循 PRISMA 指南。证据基础包括来自 PubMed、Cochrane 图书馆、EMBASE 和 Web of Science 的截至 2025 年 11 月 30 日发表的研究。基于 抗病毒药物 方案和 MAFLD 诊断方法进行亚组分析,并进行留一敏感性分析以评估研究结果的稳健性。
共识与结论
MAFLD 与较低的病毒学和生化反应率、第 48 周时乙型肝炎表面抗原血清清除的几率较高(特别是在接受核苷(酸)类似物治疗的患者中)以及尽管接受 抗病毒药物 治疗但发生肝细胞癌(HCC)的几率增加有关。然而,在任何时间点均未观察到 MAFLD 与乙型肝炎 e 抗原血清清除之间存在显着关联。
争议与缺口
亚组分析表明,抗病毒药物 方案和 MAFLD 诊断方法部分解释了总体结果的异质性,特别是 乙型肝炎病毒 DNA 清除率和 HCC 方面。证据没有报告 MAFLD 与这些结果相关的具体机制,也没有报告 MAFLD 对其他临床结果(如肝纤维化进展)的影响。
科研与实践启示
研究结果支持将代谢风险评估纳入慢性乙型肝炎的管理,并强调需要对 MAFLD 和代谢功能障碍相关的脂肪性肝病进行标准化定义。

Metabolic dysfunction-associated fatty liver and antiviral response in chronic hepatitis B: A systematic review and meta-analysis

Authors: Jia Liu, Peng Zhang, Ke-Zhen Hu, Zhi-Hui Zhao, Yong-Ning Xin, Yan-Zhen Bi
Original abstract (English)
BACKGROUND Metabolic dysfunction-associated fatty liver disease (MAFLD) frequently coexists with chronic hepatitis B (CHB). However, its association with antiviral treatment responses and long-term clinical outcomes in patients with CHB remains unclear. AIM To systematically evaluate the impact of MAFLD on antiviral treatment responses and long-term clinical outcomes in patients with CHB. METHODS This systematic review and meta-analysis was conducted in accordance with the Preferred Reporting Items for Systematic Reviews and Meta-Analyses guidelines. PubMed, the Cochrane Library, EMBASE, and Web of Science were searched for studies published up to November 30, 2025. Meta-analyses were performed to assess associations between MAFLD and antiviral treatment responses or clinical outcomes. Subgroup analyses were conducted according to antiviral regimen and MAFLD diagnostic approach, and leave-one-out sensitivity analyses were performed to evaluate robustness. RESULTS Thirty-two studies were included, of which 20 evaluated antiviral treatment efficacy and 13 assessed clinical outcomes in patients with CHB and concomitant MAFLD (one study reported both treatment responses and clinical outcomes). MAFLD was associated with a higher rate of hepatitis B surface antigen seroclearance at 48 weeks [odds ratio (OR) = 1.41, 95%CI: 1.28-1.55], although this association was attenuated at 96 weeks (OR = 1.44, 95%CI: 0.96-2.16). No significant association was observed between MAFLD and hepatitis B e antigen seroclearance at any time-point. MAFLD was consistently associated with lower rates of alanine aminotransferase normalization and hepatitis B virus DNA clearance throughout the treatment duration. MAFLD was associated with a significantly increased risk of hepatocellular carcinoma (HCC) despite antiviral therapy (OR = 1.50, 95%CI: 1.01-2.24). Subgroup analyses according to the antiviral regimen revealed distinct patterns, partly explaining the heterogeneity in the overall analyses. Subgroup analyses suggested that the antiviral regimen and MAFLD diagnostic approach partly explained the heterogeneity, particularly for hepatitis B virus DNA clearance and HCC. CONCLUSION MAFLD was associated with lower virological and biochemical response rates, higher odds of hepatitis B surface antigen seroclearance at week 48, particularly among patients receiving nucleos(t)ide analogue therapy, and higher odds of HCC in patients with CHB, but not with hepatitis B e antigen seroclearance. These findings support metabolic risk assessment in CHB management and highlight the need for standardized MAFLD/metabolic dysfunction-associated steatotic liver disease definitions.
View review five-element analysis
Scope and question
The review aims to evaluate the impact of MAFLD on antiviral treatment responses and long-term clinical outcomes in patients with chronic hepatitis B (CHB). The central question is whether MAFLD influences virological, biochemical, and clinical responses to antiviral therapy in CHB.
Evidence base and method
This systematic review and meta-analysis followed the PRISMA guidelines. The evidence base included studies from PubMed, the Cochrane Library, EMBASE, and Web of Science, published up to November 30, 2025. Subgroup analyses were conducted based on antiviral regimen and MAFLD diagnostic approach, and leave-one-out sensitivity analyses were performed to assess the robustness of findings.
Consensus and conclusions
MAFLD was associated with lower virological and biochemical response rates, higher odds of hepatitis B surface antigen seroclearance at week 48, particularly among patients receiving nucleos(t)ide analogue therapy, and increased odds of hepatocellular carcinoma (HCC) despite antiviral therapy. However, no significant association was observed between MAFLD and hepatitis B e antigen seroclearance at any time-point.
Controversies and gaps
Subgroup analyses suggested that antiviral regimen and MAFLD diagnostic approach partly explained the heterogeneity in the overall findings, particularly for hepatitis B virus DNA clearance and HCC. The evidence does not report on the specific mechanisms linking MAFLD to these outcomes or the impact of MAFLD on other clinical outcomes such as liver fibrosis progression.
Research and practice implications
The findings support the integration of metabolic risk assessment in the management of CHB and highlight the need for standardized definitions of MAFLD and metabolic dysfunction-associated steatotic liver disease.

合成生物学在病毒样颗粒设计和开发中的应用 疫苗

Synthetic Biology in the Design and Development of Virus-Like Particle Vaccines
作者： Yong-Can Zhou
摘要中文翻译随着新发传染病的频繁出现和精准医疗需求的不断增长,传统疫苗的局限性日益显现。病毒样颗粒(VLP)由于结合了天然病毒的典型结构特征和良好的安全性,已成为开发下一代 疫苗 的关键平台。本文从合成生物学的角度,综述了VLP 疫苗的工作原理、生产工艺和关键构建技术,重点介绍了底盘蛋白设计、抗原模块工程化改造、连锁系统优化、结构稳定性增强等方面的研究进展。同时,对已上市的病毒颗粒疫苗(HBV)、戊型肝炎病毒(HEV)、人乳头瘤病毒(HPV)等的开发现状进行了总结,并分析了病毒颗粒疫苗目前在流感、COVID-19、HIV等临床研究中的应用情况。结果表明,合成生物学显着增强了VLP 疫苗的可编程性、免疫原性和开发效率,推动其进化走向多价、智能和个性化形式。然而,复杂抗原展示、大规模生产以及长期保护功效评估等问题仍有待解决。未来的研究可以进一步整合人工智能驱动的蛋白质设计、新型表达系统和精准递送技术,以推进VLP 疫苗的临床转化和工业应用。
查看综述五要素
范围与问题
该综述的重点是利用合成生物学设计和开发病毒样颗粒(VLP)疫苗,重点是乙型肝炎病毒(HBV)作为靶点。核心问题是合成生物学如何增强 HBV VLP 疫苗 的可编程性、免疫原性和开发效率。
证据基础与方法
该综述以HBV为重点,对VLP 疫苗的工作原理、生产工艺和关键施工技术进行了系统分析。总结了已上市的HBV VLP 疫苗的开发现状,并分析了其在临床研究中的应用,包括流感、COVID-19和HIV的临床研究。
共识与结论
综述得出的结论是,合成生物学显着增强了VLP 疫苗的可编程性、免疫原性和开发效率,推动其进化走向多价、智能和个性化形式。重点介绍了底盘蛋白设计、抗原模块工程改造、连接系统优化和结构稳定性增强等方面的进展。
争议与缺口
该审查将复杂抗原展示、大规模生产挑战以及长期保护功效评估等未解决的问题确定为需要进一步调查的关键证据差距。
科研与实践启示
未来的研究应整合人工智能驱动的蛋白质设计、新型表达系统和精准递送技术,以推进 VLP 疫苗 的临床转化和工业应用,特别是针对 HBV。

Synthetic Biology in the Design and Development of Virus-Like Particle Vaccines

Authors: Yong-Can Zhou
Original abstract (English)
With the frequent emergence of new infectious diseases and the growing demand for precision medicine, the limitations of traditional vaccines are becoming increasingly apparent. Virus-like particles (VLPs) have become a key platform for the development of next-generation vaccines due to their combination of structural characteristics typical of natural viruses and a favorable safety profile. From the perspective of synthetic biology, this article reviews the working principles, production processes, and key construction technologies of VLP vaccines, with a focus on research progress regarding chassis protein design, engineering modification of antigen modules, optimization of linkage systems, and enhancement of structural stability. Meanwhile, the development status of marketed VLP vaccines, such as those for hepatitis B virus (HBV), hepatitis E virus (HEV), and human papillomavirus (HPV) is summarized, and the application of VLP vaccines currently in clinical research, including those for influenza, COVID-19, and HIV is analyzed. The results demonstrate that synthetic biology has significantly enhanced the programmability, immunogenicity, and development efficiency of VLP vaccines, driving their evolution toward multivalent, intelligent, and personalized forms. However, issues such as complex antigen display, large-scale production, and the evaluation of long-term protective efficacy remain to be addressed. Future research could further integrate AI-driven protein design, novel expression systems, and precision delivery technologies to advance the clinical translation and industrial application of VLP vaccines.
View review five-element analysis
Scope and question
The review focuses on the design and development of virus-like particle (VLP) vaccines using synthetic biology, with an emphasis on hepatitis B virus (HBV) as the target. The central question is how synthetic biology can enhance the programmability, immunogenicity, and development efficiency of VLP vaccines for HBV.
Evidence base and method
The review is a systematic analysis of the working principles, production processes, and key construction technologies of VLP vaccines, with a focus on HBV. It summarizes the development status of marketed VLP vaccines for HBV and analyzes their application in clinical research, including those for influenza, COVID-19, and HIV.
Consensus and conclusions
The review concludes that synthetic biology has significantly enhanced the programmability, immunogenicity, and development efficiency of VLP vaccines, driving their evolution toward multivalent, intelligent, and personalized forms. It highlights progress in chassis protein design, engineering modification of antigen modules, optimization of linkage systems, and enhancement of structural stability.
Controversies and gaps
The review identifies unresolved issues such as complex antigen display, large-scale production challenges, and the evaluation of long-term protective efficacy as critical evidence gaps that require further investigation.
Research and practice implications
Future research should integrate AI-driven protein design, novel expression systems, and precision delivery technologies to advance the clinical translation and industrial application of VLP vaccines, particularly for HBV.

慢性乙型肝炎的功能性治愈中的过继免疫转移:来自造血干细胞移植的见解

Adoptive Immune Transfer in the Functional Cure of Chronic Hepatitis B: Insights from Hematopoietic Stem Cell Transplantation
作者： He Ma, Jia-Rui Zheng, Xin-kun Liu, Lin-Xiang Huang, Yan-di Xie, Bo Feng
摘要中文翻译实现 慢性乙型肝炎 的 功能性治愈 仍然具有挑战性,主要是因为无法重组有效的 宿主 免疫应答 来对抗病毒。同种异体造血干细胞移植后 慢性乙型肝炎 的意外 功能性治愈 为免疫介导的 HBsAg 清除提供了独特的原理验证模型。本文旨在阐明造血干细胞移植介导的HBsAg清除的核心机制在于“过继性免疫转移”,并探讨基于该机制的现代细胞免疫疗法的设计原则和未来方向。我们回顾了现有的 病例报告 和荟萃分析证据,承认其局限性,并剖析了供体来源的 乙型肝炎病毒 特异性 T 和 B 细胞可能共同重建适应性免疫并打破 宿主 免疫耐受的免疫机制。然后我们比较评估了嵌合抗原受体T细胞疗法和T细胞受体工程化T细胞疗法的技术特点和局限性。当前的工程化 T 细胞疗法虽然转化了过继转移的核心原则,但表现出基本的局限性,包括单克隆特异性、无法重塑 宿主 微环境以及缺乏持久的体液重建。更广泛的功能治疗策略可能需要采用以造血干细胞移植为例的协同作用和全身免疫重塑的范式。未来的策略应该开发暂时优化的多层联合疗法,为能够系统性重建 抗病毒药物 免疫力的下一代疗法提供可能的途径。

Adoptive Immune Transfer in the Functional Cure of Chronic Hepatitis B: Insights from Hematopoietic Stem Cell Transplantation

Authors: He Ma, Jia-Rui Zheng, Xin-kun Liu, Lin-Xiang Huang, Yan-di Xie, Bo Feng
Original abstract (English)
Achieving a functional cure for chronic hepatitis B remains challenging, primarily due to the inability to reconstitute effective host immune responses against the virus. The unexpected functional cure of chronic hepatitis B following allogeneic hematopoietic stem cell transplantation provides a unique proof-of-principle model for immune-mediated HBsAg clearance. This review aims to elucidate that the core mechanism underlying hematopoietic stem cell transplantation-mediated HBsAg clearance lies in “adoptive immune transfer”, and to explore design principles and future directions for modern cell-based immunotherapies based on this mechanism. We reviewed available case reports and meta-analytic evidence, acknowledging their limitations, and dissected the immunological mechanisms by which donor-derived hepatitis B virus-specific T and B cells may jointly reconstitute adaptive immunity and break host immune tolerance. We then comparatively evaluated the technical characteristics and limitations of chimeric antigen receptor T cell therapy and T-cell receptor-engineered T-cell therapy. Current engineered T-cell therapies, while translating the core principle of adoptive transfer, exhibit fundamental limitations including monoclonal specificity, inability to remodel the host microenvironment, and absence of durable humoral reconstitution. A broader functional-cure strategy may require adopting the paradigm of synergy and systemic immune reshaping exemplified by hematopoietic stem cell transplantation. Future strategies should develop temporally optimized, multilayered combination therapies, offering a possible pathway for next-generation therapeutics capable of systemically rebuilding antiviral immunity.
View review five-element analysis
Scope and question
This review aims to elucidate that the core mechanism underlying hematopoietic stem cell transplantation-mediated HBsAg clearance lies in “adoptive immune transfer”, and to explore design principles and future directions for modern cell-based immunotherapies based on this mechanism.
Evidence base and method
We reviewed available case reports and meta-analytic evidence, acknowledging their limitations, and dissected the immunological mechanisms by which donor-derived hepatitis B virus-specific T and B cells may jointly reconstitute adaptive immunity and break host immune tolerance.
Consensus and conclusions
We then comparatively evaluated the technical characteristics and limitations of chimeric antigen receptor T cell therapy and T-cell receptor-engineered T-cell therapy.
Controversies and gaps
A broader functional-cure strategy may require adopting the paradigm of synergy and systemic immune reshaping exemplified by hematopoietic stem cell transplantation.
Research and practice implications
Future strategies should develop temporally optimized, multilayered combination therapies, offering a possible pathway for next-generation therapeutics capable of systemically rebuilding antiviral immunity.

争议与缺口

Antiviral Treatment Utilisation and Long-Term Outcomes in HBV-Related Hepatocellular Carcinoma: A Systematic Review and Meta-Analysis

Authors: weihan yang
Original abstract (English)
This systematic review and meta-analysis examines nucleos(t)ide analogue treatment utilisation and long-term outcomes in adults with hepatitis B virus-related hepatocellular carcinoma, including treatment timing, survival and recurrence.
View review five-element analysis
Scope and question
The review targets hepatitis B virus (HBV) infection leading to hepatocellular carcinoma (HCC) in adult patients, evaluating the utilisation of nucleos(t)ide analogue antiviral therapy and its impact on long‐term outcomes such as survival and tumour recurrence. The central question is how treatment timing and use of nucleos(t)ide analogues affect these outcomes in HBV‐related HCC.
Evidence base and method
The supplied evidence does not report the specific review type, searched databases, search dates, eligibility criteria, study selection process, number of included studies, or whether a meta‐analysis or consensus methodology was employed.
Consensus and conclusions
The supplied evidence does not report any pooled estimates, areas of agreement, or definitive conclusions regarding nucleos(t)ide analogue utilisation and outcomes in HBV‐related HCC.
Controversies and gaps
The supplied evidence does not report heterogeneity, conflicting findings, methodological weaknesses, missing populations, or other concrete evidence gaps related to the review topic.
Research and practice implications
The supplied evidence does not report specific research priorities, surveillance needs, diagnostic or therapeutic recommendations, or policy implications for HBV‐related HCC management.

B 常规重要公共卫生新闻

改用注射剂 治疗 后乙型肝炎重新激活:罕见,但谨慎行事 - Aidsmap

Hepatitis B reactivation after switch to injectable treatment: rare, but proceed with caution - Aidsmap
新闻简报本新闻报道强调了艾滋病毒感染者改用含有卡博特韦/利匹韦林的 治疗 注射疗法后乙型肝炎重新激活的风险。在国际艾滋病毒感染药物治疗大会(HIV 格拉斯哥 2026 年)上发表的研究发现,在西班牙和意大利进行的 队列研究 研究中,重新激活率很低或不存在。在西班牙,RELATIVITY 队列研究 对 2024 年底前改用注射剂 治疗 的 3349 人进行了跟踪,中位随访时间为 25 个月。没有观察到临床上怀疑的肝酶升高导致的再激活,但 115 名参与者检测到了分离的抗 HBc。在意大利,SCohoLART 研究对 627 人进行了跟踪调查,其中 30% 的人曾接触过乙肝病毒。报告了四起 HBV 相关事件,其中包括一例需要住院治疗的症状再激活病例。报告指出,多替拉韦/拉米夫定的再激活风险似乎较低,但证据不足以证实保护作用。由于重新激活的不可预测性,建议定期监测。没有常规进行 HBV DNA 检测,这可能低估了病毒再激活病例。
查看新闻五要素
时间
发布日期:2026年11月19日。事件日期:提供的证据中未报告。时间范围:西班牙的随访期为 25 个月,意大利的随访期为 2.7 年。
地点与对象
受影响人群:艾滋病毒感染者,特别是既往接触过乙型肝炎或潜在隐性低水平感染者。
事件
在改用含有卡博特韦/利匹韦林的 治疗 注射方案后,在一些个体中观察到乙型肝炎重新激活。国际艾滋病毒感染药物治疗大会(HIV 格拉斯哥 2026)上发表的研究报告了这一点。
规模、影响与风险
在西班牙,115 人在随访过程中检测到了分离的抗 HBc,但没有观察到临床上怀疑的肝酶升高导致的再激活。在意大利,四人经历了乙型肝炎相关事件,其中一例症状重新激活,需要住院治疗。再激活风险较低,但不可忽略,并且证据不足以证实拉米夫定的保护作用。
应对、状态与不确定性
研究表明重新激活的情况很少见,但建议定期监测。证据不足以证实拉米夫定可以防止再激活。并未对所有分离出抗 HBc 的患者进行 HBV DNA 检测,这可能低估了病毒再激活病例。
B routine priorityPublic-health news

Hepatitis B reactivation after switch to injectable treatment: rare, but proceed with caution - Aidsmap

News Brief
This news report highlights the risk of hepatitis B reactivation in people living with HIV after switching to injectable treatment regimens containing cabotegravir / rilpivirine. Studies presented at the International Congress on Drug Therapy in HIV Infection (HIV Glasgow 2026) found that reactivation rates were low or non-existent in cohort studies conducted in Spain and Italy. In Spain, the RELATIVITY cohort followed 3349 individuals who switched to injectable treatment before the end of 2024, with a median follow-up of 25 months. No clinically suspected reactivation with liver enzyme elevations was observed, but 115 participants had isolated anti-HBc detected. In Italy, the SCohoLART study followed 627 individuals, with 30% having previous HBV exposure. Four HBV-related events were reported, including one case of symptomatic reactivation requiring hospitalisation. The report notes that the risk of reactivation appears low with dolutegravir / lamivudine, but the evidence is not sufficient to confirm protection. Regular monitoring is recommended due to the unpredictability of reactivation. HBV DNA testing was not routinely performed, which may have underestimated reactivation cases.
Original source text (English)
Hepatitis B reactivation after switch to injectable treatment: rare, but proceed with caution By Keith Alcorn Spain 19 November 2026 7 mins Although hepatitis B reactivation is rare after a switch to injectable cabotegravir / rilpivirine, people with HIV require careful monitoring before and after a switch to assess protection against hepatitis B and reactivation risks, studies presented at last week’s International Congress on Drug Therapy in HIV Infection (HIV Glasgow 2026) suggest. Cohort studies carried out in Spain and Italy found that rates of hepatitis B reactivation after switching to injectable cabotegravir / rilpivirine were low or non-existent. But the Italian study highlighted the risk of poor or non-existent immunity to hepatitis B despite vaccination. Three vaccinated people acquired hepatitis B during the study, although their infections were asymptomatic and detected only through tests for hepatitis B core antibody (anti-HBc) and surface antibody (anti-HBs). People with active chronic hepatitis B are not recommended to switch away from HIV treatment regimens containing tenofovir, which also suppresses hepatitis B. But for a much larger group of people – those with past exposure to hepatitis B or potential hidden, low-level infection – the risks and benefits of switching away from tenofovir have been less clear. Cohort studies in Europe report that approximately one-third of people with HIV have antibodies to hepatitis B core antigen (anti-HBc) but have no detectable hepatitis B surface antigen, showing that they have cleared hepatitis B infection or have a very low-level (occult) infection. Reactivation of hepatitis B due to an occult infection after the withdrawal of tenofovir is rare; a meta-analysis of studies that carried out regular HBV testing after tenofovir withdrawal found that nearly four cases occurred in every 100 person-years of follow-up. In most cases they are detected only by testing, but a US Veterans study found that one in four reactivations were accompanied by a flare-up of hepatitis that may lead to hospitalisation and liver damage. There seems to be no way to predict who will experience reactivation or how soon reactivation might occur after tenofovir withdrawal, emphasising the importance of regular monitoring for reactivation. Although the risk of reactivation appears low when taking dolutegravir / lamivudine, the currently available evidence is not sufficient to confirm that lamivudine protects against reactivation, Professor Anna Maria Geretti of the Royal Free Hospital, London, told the conference. More data is also needed on the risk of reactivation after switching to cabotegravir / rilpivirine. Two studies presented at HIV Glasgow provided further information. Glossary antibody A protein substance (immunoglobulin) produced by the immune system in response to a foreign organism. Many diagnostic tests for HIV detect the presence of antibodies to HIV in blood. hepatitis Inflammation of the liver, which can be caused by hepatitis viruses (A, B, C etc), alcohol or some drugs. If not treated, hepatitis can lead to hepatic fibrosis and/or cirrhosis. HIV Human immunodeficiency virus, the virus which causes AIDS. There are two variants: HIV-1, and HIV-2. HIV-1 is by far the most common worldwide. See ‘subtype’ for more information. on Abbreviation of a Latin term meaning every night. therapy Any form of treatment. Drugs, radiation, and psychiatric counselling are forms of therapy. In Spain, the RELATIVITY cohort followed 3349 people who switched to cabotegravir / rilpivirine before the end of 2024. Participants were followed for a median of 25 months after switching. The cohort was predominantly male (84%) and had been taking antiretroviral therapy for a median of nine years before switching. Half of all participants had been vaccinated against hepatitis B and had immunity, defined as testing positive for hepatitis B surface antibody and negative for hepatitis B core antibody. A further 12% had been vaccinated but did not have immunity. Seven percent had not been vaccinated and did not have evidence of exposure (no antibodies to HBc or HBs). Twelve per cent of participants had evidence of past HBV infection (anti-HBc and anti-HBs) while 3% had isolated anti-HBc without surface antibody, indicating either waning infection or extremely low-level HBV infection. Five study participants had active HBV (detectable hepatitis B surface antigen) at study entry. HBV serology was unavailable for 13% of participants. HBV serology was carried out during follow-up if acute infection was suspected but it was not carried out routinely for all participants. Isolated anti-HBc was detected in 115 people during a median 25 months of follow-up. No cases of clinically suspected reactivation with liver enzyme elevations were observed. Thirteen people had ALT increases but HBsAg was negative. HBV DNA testing was not done in all people with isolated anti-HBc so reactivation may be underestimated, Dr Luz Martin Carbonero of University Hospital La Paz, Madrid, noted. One person who had been vaccinated twice without developing antibody responses to hepatitis B acquired hepatitis B during the follow-up period. In Italy, the SCohoLART study followed 627 people who switched to injectable cabotegravir / rilpivirine, for a median of 2.7 years. Of these, 30% had previous HBV exposure, including 3% with isolated anti-HBc. Of the remainder, 50% had vaccine-induced immunity and 19% had no previous exposure or immunity. During the follow-up period, four people experienced HBV-related events. One participant with anti-HBc at the time of switching developed symptomatic reactivation of hepatitis B within three months of switching, leading to hospitalisation and resumption of tenofovir-containing treatment. Three vaccinated participants who were anti-HBc negative at the time of switching were found to have acquired HBV during follow-up. Two had no immunity despite vaccination; one had been vaccinated but had low levels of anti-HBs. HBV acquisition was detected by routine HBV serology. The findings underline the importance of checking immunisation status and antibody levels before switching to cabotegravir / rilpivirine or another regimen that doesn’t contain an HBV-active antiretroviral. Professor Anna Maria Geretti said that forthcoming British HIV Association guidelines will recommend the following management protocol for people with isolated anti-HBc measurements before switching: If anti-HBc positive but HBsAg-negative and HBV-DNA negative, give one dose of HBV vaccine and test for anti-HBs two weeks later. If anti-HBs levels are above 10 IU/ml, evaluate other risk factors for reactivation before switching. If anti-HBs levels are below 10 IU/ml, complete the HBV vaccine series before evaluating risk factors and before switching. Professor Geretti emphasised that although immunisation is advisable, inducing or boosting anti-HBs has not been shown to protect against reactivation, so it is important to assess the reactivation risk before switching. Re-vaccination is also advisable for people without anti-HBs, especially in circumstances where vaccination history is missing. A cross-sectional study of 679 people receiving HIV care in Buenos Aires, Argentina, found that information on HBV status was missing in over 40% of participants and 30% lacked protective immunity to HBV, either due to absence of vaccination or waning protection. References Geretti AM. Hepatitis B 2-drug regimen and re-activation. International Congress on Drug Therapy in HIV Infection, Glasgow, 2026. Rueda MMA et al. HBV outcomes after switching to long-acting cabotegravir plus rilpivirine in the Spanish RELATIVITY cohort. International Congress on Drug Therapy in HIV Infection, Glasgow, abstract O35, 2026. Raccagni AR et al. Hepatitis B after switch to long-acting cabotegravir / rilpivirine: data from the SCohoLART study. International Congress on Drug Therapy in HIV Infection, Glasgow, abstract O36, 2026. Laufer N et al. Incomplete HBV serological documentation could mask unrecognized immunity gaps in people living with HIV receiving tenofovir-sparing antiretroviral therapy. International Congress on Drug Therapy in HIV Infection, Glasgow, abstract P505, 2026. Keith Alcorn Editorial Consultant Keith joined the editorial team of the National AIDS Manual (NAM) in 1991, launched the aidsmap website in 1998 and was the Senior Editor of NAM and aidsmap until 2018. He now contributes news and conference reports as a freelancer
