Publication date: 2026-11-19. Event date: Not reported in the supplied evidence. Time range: Follow-up periods of 25 months in Spain and 2.7 years in Italy.
Location and population
Affected population: People living with HIV, particularly those with past exposure to hepatitis B or potential hidden, low-level infection.
Event
Hepatitis B reactivation was observed in some individuals after switching to injectable treatment regimens containing cabotegravir / rilpivirine. This was reported by studies presented at the International Congress on Drug Therapy in HIV Infection (HIV Glasgow 2026).
Scale, impact and risk
In Spain, 115 people had isolated anti-HBc detected during follow-up, but no clinically suspected reactivation with liver enzyme elevations was observed. In Italy, four people experienced HBV-related events, including one case of symptomatic reactivation requiring hospitalisation. Reactivation risk is low but not negligible, and the evidence is insufficient to confirm protection with lamivudine.
Response, status and uncertainty
Studies suggest that reactivation is rare, but regular monitoring is recommended. The evidence is not sufficient to confirm that lamivudine protects against reactivation. HBV DNA testing was not done in all people with isolated anti-HBc, potentially underestimating reactivation cases.

The report notes that the market grew from $3.93 billion in 2025 to $4.1 billion in 2026, with a CAGR of 4.2%, and is projected to reach $4.74 billion by 2030 at a CAGR of 3.7%. In 2022, 1.2 million new HBV infections were reported, underscoring the disease's ongoing public health significance. The report emphasizes future trends such as next-generation therapies, shorter treatment regimens, and therapeutic vaccines, but does not provide specific details on current public health responses or investigations. The analysis is based on market data and does not include detailed epidemiological or clinical findings.

November 18, 2026, 17:15 GMT Share This Article Chronic Hepatitis B Market Report 2026 The Business Research Company The Business Research Company’s Chronic Hepatitis B Global Market Report 2026 – Market Size, Trends, And Forecast 2026-2035 LONDON, GREATER LONDON, UNITED KINGDOM, November 18, 2026 /EINPresswire.com/ -- The market for chronic hepatitis B has been expanding steadily, reflecting advancements in healthcare and increasing awareness about the disease. As efforts to improve diagnosis and treatment continue, this sector is expected to witness consistent growth, driven by both medical innovation and public health initiatives. Let’s explore the market size, key growth factors, dominant regional players, and emerging trends shaping the future of chronic hepatitis B treatment. Understanding the Market Size and Growth Projections for Chronic Hepatitis B The chronic hepatitis B market has shown a steady upward trend in recent years. It is projected to grow from $3.93 billion in 2025 to $4.1 billion in 2026, representing a compound annual growth rate (CAGR) of 4.2%. This increase during the historical period has been largely propelled by enhanced hepatitis B screening efforts, broader access to antiviral medications, heightened awareness of liver disease complications, wider usage of nucleos(t)ide analogs, and the establishment of long-term disease monitoring protocols. Download a free sample of the chronic hepatitis b market report: https://thebusinessresearchcompany.com/sample_request?id=62495943&type=smp&name=Chronic%20Hepatitis%20B%20Market%20Report%202026&utm_source=EINPresswire&utm_medium=Paid&utm_campaign=Nov_PR Looking ahead, the market is expected to maintain steady expansion, reaching $4.74 billion by 2030, with a CAGR of 3.7%. This forecasted growth is supported by rising investments in next-generation HBV therapies, a growing preference for treatment regimens that require shorter durations, ongoing development of therapeutic vaccines, and intensified efforts to eliminate viral reservoirs. Additional trends expected to influence the market include increased adoption of long-term antiviral treatments, advancements in functional cure strategies, heightened emphasis on early detection and disease monitoring, expansion of immune-modulating therapies, and stronger patient adherence initiatives. What Chronic Hepatitis B Means and Its Medical Impact Chronic hepatitis B is a persistent infection caused by the hepatitis B virus (HBV) lasting longer than six months. It can lead to serious liver complications, including liver damage, failure, or cancer. The condition often remains symptomless for many years but can be diagnosed through blood testing and managed effectively using antiviral drugs combined with regular health monitoring. View the full chronic hepatitis b market report: https://thebusinessresearchcompany.com/report/chronic-hepatitis-b-global-market-report?utm_source=EINPresswire&utm_medium=Paid&utm_campaign=Nov_PR Key Factors Driving Growth in the Chronic Hepatitis B Market One of the primary growth drivers for the chronic hepatitis B market is the high prevalence of HBV infection worldwide. Hepatitis B is a liver infection caused by the virus, which can be prevented through vaccination programs. However, low vaccination coverage in many low- and middle-income countries results in higher transmission rates, as large populations remain vulnerable. This increase in infection rates boosts the demand for diagnostic testing and treatment solutions. For instance, a report released by the Centers for Disease Control and Prevention (CDC) in June 2024 highlighted that raising global hepatitis B vaccination coverage to 90% and improving treatment availability to 80% could prevent 26 million new infections and save 9 million lives between 2022 and 2050. In 2022 alone, there were 1.2 million new HBV infections reported. This significant disease burden continues to fuel the chronic hepatitis B market’s expansion. 研究提供了在没有乙型肝炎的人群中存在丁型肝炎病毒 患病率 的额外证据 - STT Info

Study provides additional evidence on hepatitis D virus prevalence in people without hepatitis B - STT Info
新闻简报HUS 和赫尔辛基大学进行的一项研究挑战了人们的既定认识,即如果没有同时感染乙型肝炎 (HBV),就不可能发生丁型肝炎病毒 (HDV) 感染。该研究分析了 1,996 名患者的 2,121 份血清和血浆样本,结果显示,20 名患者 HDV 抗体呈阳性,其中 17 名患者未感染 HBV。一名患者被诊断为活动性 HDV 感染,但没有 HBV,分析材料中 HDV 的 患病率 约为 1%。研究结果表明丁型肝炎病毒可能利用其他辅助病毒,例如丙型肝炎病毒(HCV),这可能具有病毒学意义。然而,该研究并未证实丁型肝炎病毒可以在没有乙型肝炎病毒的情况下发生,需要进一步的研究来确定其临床意义。该研究发表在《临床病毒学杂志》上,并提供了芬兰关于 HDV 患病率 的第一份数据。

B routine priorityPublic-health news

Study provides additional evidence on hepatitis D virus prevalence in people without hepatitis B - STT Info

News Brief
A study conducted by HUS and the University of Helsinki challenges the established understanding that hepatitis D virus (HDV) infection cannot occur without a simultaneous hepatitis B (HBV) infection. The research analyzed 2,121 blood serum and plasma samples from 1,996 patients, revealing that 20 patients were positive for HDV antibodies, with 17 not having HBV infection. One patient was diagnosed with an active HDV infection without HBV, and the prevalence of HDV in the analyzed material was about one percent. The findings suggest that HDV may use other helper viruses, such as hepatitis C virus (HCV), which could have virological significance. However, the study does not confirm that HDV can occur without HBV, and further research is needed to determine the clinical implications. The study was published in the Journal of Clinical Virology and provides the first data on HDV prevalence in Finland.
Original source text (English)
Sinä päätät henkilötiedoistasi Sivustomme STT Info käyttää evästeitä. Klikkaa "Hyväksy kaikki" nähdäksesi kaiken sisällön sivustolla. Valitse "Hylkää kaikki", jos haluat rajoittaa evästeiden käytön vain välttämättömiin. Jos haluat tehdä tarkempia valintoja, klikkaa "Näytä tiedot". Lue lisää evästeistä Hylkää kaikki Hyväksy kaikki Tiedotteet Tilaa tiedotteita Julkaisijat Tietoa palvelusta Osta kertatiedote Kirjaudu Study provides additional evidence on hepatitis D virus prevalence in people without hepatitis B 18.11.2026 10:59:27 EET | HUS | Press release Share The established medical understanding has been that hepatitis D virus infection cannot occur without a simultaneous hepatitis B infection. The results from a study conducted by HUS and the University of Helsinki suggest that hepatitis D virus infection can occur without a simultaneous hepatitis B infection. Researcher Jussi Hepojoki, specialist in clinical microbiology Hanna Jarva and specialist in clinical microbiology Satu Kurkela Hanna Raijas-Turva HUS Hepatitis D (HDV) is a satellite virus, which means that it needs a helper virus for transmission. The current understanding is that HDV cannot be transmitted without a simultaneous hepatitis B (HBV) infection. However, this view has been challenged in some recent studies. A study conducted by HUS and the University of Helsinki provided additional evidence on the prevalence of HDV without an HBV infection. “Based on the results, we cannot conclude that HDV can occur without HBV, but the evidence we gained is very interesting. If it can be demonstrated that HDV can occur without HBV, further research will be needed to determine the clinical significance of HDV without HBV,” explains Satu Kurkela, head of division and a specialist in clinical microbiology at HUS. Hepatitis B, C and D are blood-borne infections. Hepatitis B and C can cause severe liver diseases if the infections become chronic. The current understanding is that an HDV infection predisposes an HBV carrier to severe symptoms of the disease. More research needed on other possible helper viruses The study analyzed 2,121 samples of blood serum and plasma from 1,996 patients. The samples analyzed were residual specimens from samples sent to the HUS Virology and Immunology laboratory for other diagnostic purposes in 2021 and 2022. In the analyzed samples, 20 patients were positive for HDV antibodies, and 17 of them did not have a simultaneous HBV infection. One patient was diagnosed with an active HDV infection without an HBV infection. This patient was diagnosed with hepatitis C (HCV). “This finding was especially interesting, because there is previous evidence suggesting that HCV may be a possible HDV helper virus. Our finding does not provide certainty on the matter, and more research is needed,” says Jussi Hepojoki, a researcher at the University of Helsinki. The helper virus defines which cells HDV can penetrate in the body. “Information on what other helper viruses HDV can use would be virologically significant. Especially, as we know that HDV may significantly aggravate HBV infection.” First data on HDV prevalence in Finland The study provided information on the prevalence of HDV in Finland for the first time. In the analyzed material, the prevalence was about one percent. “This does not mean that one percent of Finns carry HDV, but it provides initial information on the occurrence of the virus in Finland,” says Hanna Jarva, chief physician and specialist in clinical microbiology at HUS. Currently, HDV antibodies are only examined from people who have been diagnosed with HBV. If it is true that HDV can also occur without HBV, some HDV infections may remain undetected. “Hopefully, future research will show if this really is the case, and, if so, what its clinical significance is.” The study was published in the Journal of Clinical Virology. Research article: Further evidence of hepatitis D occurring in the absence of hepatitis B In university hospitals, research is part of the treatment: new findings make treatment practises more efficient and help target treatments correctly. We work in close collaboration with the University of Helsinki and other universities, and we publish about 3000 peer-reviewed research articles annually. Today’s research provides better care tomorrow. 在一项针对 23,098 名患者的研究中,简化的 GADA 模型改善了 HBV 相关肝细胞癌的检测 - EurekAlert!

A simplified GADA model improves detection of HBV-related hepatocellular carcinoma in a study of 23,098 patients - EurekAlert!
新闻简报EurekAlert! 于 2026 年 11 月 19 日发表的一项研究!强调了中国用于检测 HBV 相关肝细胞癌 (HCC) 的简化 GADA 模型的开发。该模型源自已建立的 GALAD 框架,结合了性别、年龄、脱γ-羧基凝血酶原 (DCP) 和甲胎蛋白 (AFP),以提高诊断准确性。它在三个 队列研究 的 23,098 名参与者身上进行了测试:建模 (1,533)、内部验证 (657) 和外部验证 (20,908)。GADA模型在外部验证中表现出强大的性能,AUC范围为0.858至0.977,敏感性为86.00%,特异性为81.88%。该模型旨在区分 HBV 相关 HCC、慢性乙型肝炎 和 HBV 相关肝硬化,降低检测复杂性并提高资源有限环境中的可及性。然而,建议将其作为现有诊断方法的辅助手段,而不是替代方法。该研究即将发表在《中华医学杂志》上,并强调在广泛使用之前需要进一步评估和校准。
查看新闻五要素
时间
该研究于2026年8月17日完成并在线发布,新闻稿于2026年11月19日发布。外部验证在中国10家医院进行。
地点与对象
该研究在中国进行,参与者来自全国十家医院,包括 905 名新诊断的肝细胞癌 (HCC) 患者和 20,003 名非 HCC 患者(17,802 名 慢性乙型肝炎 患者和 2,201 名乙肝相关肝硬化患者)。
事件
北京大学和中国合作医院的研究人员开发并验证了一种简化的 GADA 模型,用于检测 HBV 相关肝细胞癌 (HCC)。该模型是为患有 HBV 相关慢性肝病的患者设计的,并在三个 队列研究 中进行了测试:建模、内部验证和外部验证。
规模、影响与风险
GADA 模型在 23,098 名参与者中进行了测试,显示出强大的诊断性能,AUC 范围为 0.858 至 0.977。在外部验证 队列研究 中表现出高敏感性 (86.00%) 和特异性 (81.88%)。该模型旨在提高资源有限的临床环境中的可及性并降低测试复杂性。
应对、状态与不确定性
该研究即将发表在《中华医学杂志》上。该模型的性能在多个 队列研究 中得到了验证,建议将其作为现有诊断途径的辅助手段,而不是替代成像或临床评估。在广泛实施之前,需要跨实验室进行进一步的前瞻性评估和校准。
B routine priorityPublic-health news

A simplified GADA model improves detection of HBV-related hepatocellular carcinoma in a study of 23,098 patients - EurekAlert!

News Brief
A study published on November 19, 2026, by EurekAlert! highlights the development of a simplified GADA model for detecting HBV-related hepatocellular carcinoma (HCC) in China. The model, derived from the established GALAD framework, combines gender, age, des-gamma-carboxy prothrombin (DCP), and alpha-fetoprotein (AFP) to improve diagnostic accuracy. It was tested on 23,098 participants across three cohorts: modeling (1,533), internal validation (657), and external validation (20,908). The GADA model showed strong performance, with AUCs ranging from 0.858 to 0.977 and sensitivity of 86.00% and specificity of 81.88% in the external validation. The model is designed to distinguish HBV-related HCC from chronic hepatitis B and HBV-related cirrhosis, reducing testing complexity and improving accessibility in resource-limited settings. However, it is recommended as an adjunct to existing diagnostic methods rather than a replacement. The study is forthcoming in the Chinese Medical Journal and emphasizes the need for further evaluation and calibration before widespread use.
Original source text (English)
Advanced Search Home News Releases Multimedia Meetings Login Register News Release 19-Nov-2026 A simplified GADA model improves detection of HBV-related hepatocellular carcinoma in a study of 23,098 patients The four-variable model, tailored to patients with HBV-related chronic liver disease, retained strong performance across ten external validation centers Peer-Reviewed Publication Chinese Medical Journals Publishing House Co., Ltd. Facebook X LinkedIn WeChat Bluesky Message WhatsApp Email image: (A) Modeling cohort. (B) Internal validation cohort. (C) External validation cohort. AUC, area under the receiver operating characteristic curve; AFP, alpha-fetoprotein; AFP-L3%, lens culinaris agglutinin-reactive AFP; DCP, des-gamma-carboxy prothrombin. Credit: Chinese Medical Journal. view more Credit: Prof. Fengmin Lu from Peking University Health Science Center, China; Dr. Lijuan Liu from Fujian Medical University, China; and Dr. Mei Zhang from Medical College of Shihezi University, China Image source link: https://www.ovid.com/jnls/cmj/fulltext/10.1097/cm9.0000000000004244~development-and-validation-of-gada-model-for-diagnosing BEIJING, CHINA—Researchers from Peking University and collaborating hospitals across China have developed and validated GADA, a simplified model for detecting hepatitis B virus (HBV)-related hepatocellular carcinoma (HCC). Derived from the established GALAD framework, GADA combines gender, age, des-gamma-carboxy prothrombin (DCP; also known as PIVKA-II), and alpha-fetoprotein (AFP). In more than 23,000 participants, it showed strong diagnostic performance for both overall and early-stage HCC. The study is forthcoming in the Chinese Medical Journal. Why a China-specific model was needed Chronic HBV infection causes more than 40% of HCC cases worldwide and is associated with approximately 86% of HCC cases in China. The epidemiology of HCC in China therefore differs substantially from that in many Western cohorts, where hepatitis C, alcohol-related liver disease, and metabolic liver disease contribute more prominently. The GALAD model integrates gender, age, AFP, AFP-L3%, and DCP and has performed well in several Western populations. However, its performance required reassessment in HBV-associated chronic liver disease. AFP-L3% also adds measurement complexity, cost, and sample-volume requirements, and is not readily available in all clinical settings. The study, led by Professor Fengmin Lu was published online on August 17, 2026, in the Chinese Medical Journal. How GADA was developed and validated The investigators analyzed 2,190 adults with HBV-related chronic liver disease at Mengchao Hepatobiliary Hospital of Fujian Medical University. Participants were randomly assigned to a modeling cohort (70%; n = 1,533) and an internal validation cohort (30%; n = 657). A separate prospective external validation cohort enrolled 20,908 participants at ten hospitals across China, including 905 patients with newly diagnosed HCC and 20,003 patients without HCC (17,802 with chronic hepatitis B and 2,201 with HBV-related cirrhosis). Altogether, 23,098 participants were included. AFP and DCP provided complementary diagnostic information, whereas AFP-L3% contributed limited additional utility in this HBV-focused population. The researchers therefore developed the four-variable GADA score: Z = –11.111 + 1.122 x gender + 0.073 x age + 2.660 x log10(DCP) + 0.578 x log10(AFP) Gender is coded as 1 for male and 0 for female. Strong and reproducible diagnostic performance Cohort AUC (95% CI) Sensitivity Specificity Modeling 0.940 (0.927–0.952) 82.57% 91.53% Internal validation 0.935 (0.914–0.953) 82.04% 92.65% External validation 0.919 (0.916–0.923) 86.00% 81.88% In the modeling cohort, GADA outperformed the original GALAD model (AUC 0.940 vs. 0.907). In the external validation cohort, it also outperformed AFP alone (AUC 0.748) and DCP alone (AUC 0.841). Center-specific AUCs ranged from 0.895 to 0.977, supporting robustness across geographically and clinically diverse settings. For early-stage HCC meeting the Milan criteria, GADA achieved AUCs of 0.858 in the modeling cohort, 0.868 in the internal validation cohort, and 0.903 in the external validation cohort. In the external cohort, sensitivity was 88.16% and specificity was 74.50%. Clinical significance GADA was designed for the setting in which the clinical need is greatest in China: distinguishing HBV-related HCC from chronic hepatitis B and HBV-related cirrhosis. By removing AFP-L3% while preserving strong diagnostic accuracy, the model may reduce testing complexity and improve accessibility, including in hospitals with more limited laboratory resources. The model should be viewed as an adjunct to established HCC surveillance and diagnostic pathways, not as a replacement for imaging or clinical assessment. Prospective evaluation in routine surveillance populations and calibration of operational thresholds across laboratories will be important before broad implementation. Reference DOI: 10.1097/CM9.0000000000004244 About Professor Fengmin Lu from Peking University Health Science Center, China Prof. Fengmin Lu is Director of the Peking University Infectious Disease Research Center, Professor in the Department of Microbiology at Peking University Health Science Center, and a jointly appointed Professor at the Center for Infectious Diseases, Peking University Third Hospital. His research focuses on the molecular mechanisms of HBV-related hepatocarcinogenesis, occult HBV infection, and biomarkers. He led the development and multicenter validation of the GADA model. He has led more than 20 national research projects, received two Second-Class National Science and Technology Progress Awards, published nearly 200 papers, and holds 25 authorized invention patents, seven of which have been commercialized. Funding This work was supported by the National Natural Science Foundation of China (No. 82272433) and the Fujian Provincial Key Laboratory of Hepatic Drug Research (No. 2022-YF-0050). Event

B 常规重要公共卫生新闻

慢性乙型肝炎 肝纤维化中的地区劣势超过了个人社会经济地位 - Bioengineer.org

Area Disadvantage Outweighs Personal Socioeconomic Status in Chronic Hepatitis B Liver Fibrosis - Bioengineer.org
新闻简报土耳其的一项全国性研究调查了 慢性乙型肝炎 成人患者的社会经济因素与肝纤维化严重程度之间的关系。该研究于 2026 年 11 月 20 日发表,发现地区社会经济劣势与纤维化阶段的相关性比个人社会经济地位或出生地背景更强。该研究分析了 1,259 名参与者,其中 956 名被纳入最终的 队列研究 中。最发达省份(第 1 级)的 Ishak 纤维化评分中位数为 1.0,最贫困省份(第 6 级)为 3.0,第 6 级显着纤维化 患病率 为 66.1%,第 1 级为 7.1%。这些发现强调了地理和结构性社会经济因素在疾病进展和检测中的作用,强调在管理中需要基于地方的社会流行病学考虑慢性乙型肝炎。该研究使用了严格的统计方法,包括倾向评分匹配,以将地理差异与私人经济环境分开。慢性乙型肝炎 仍然是全球主要的健康问题,导致肝硬化和肝细胞癌,社会经济不平等可能会扰乱 诊断 和临床管理之间的轨迹。
查看新闻五要素
时间
该研究于2019年1月1日至2024年12月31日期间进行,发布日期为2026年11月20日星期五。
地点与对象
该研究的重点是土耳其患有 慢性乙型肝炎 的成年人,特别是 81 个行政省的 24 个临床中心。
事件
一项全国性多中心调查发现,慢性乙型肝炎 患者肝纤维化的严重程度与地区社会经济劣势的相关性比与个人社会经济状况或出生地背景的相关性更强。
规模、影响与风险
本研究纳入了1,259名成年受试者,其中956人构成了分析队列。第1级省份的中位Ishak纤维化评分为1.0,而第6级省份的中位评分则为3.0。第6级省份的显著纤维化发生率为66.1%,相比之下第1级省份仅为7.1%。慢性乙型肝炎是重要的公共卫生优先事项,与肝硬化和肝细胞癌的发生密切相关。
应对、状态与不确定性
该研究使用统计调整、倾向得分匹配和重叠加权来解释混杂因素。它强调了社会经济条件对疾病进展和检测的影响,但没有提供具体的干预措施或结果。
B routine priorityPublic-health news

Area Disadvantage Outweighs Personal Socioeconomic Status in Chronic Hepatitis B Liver Fibrosis - Bioengineer.org

News Brief
A nationwide study in Türkiye examined the relationship between socioeconomic factors and liver fibrosis severity in adults with chronic hepatitis B. The research, published on November 20, 2026, found that area-level socioeconomic disadvantage had a stronger correlation with fibrosis stages than individual socioeconomic status or birthplace background. The study analyzed 1,259 participants, with 956 included in the final cohort. Median Ishak fibrosis scores were 1.0 in the most developed provinces (tier 1) and 3.0 in the most deprived (tier 6), with significant fibrosis prevalence at 66.1% in tier 6 versus 7.1% in tier 1. These findings emphasize the role of geographic and structural socioeconomic factors in disease progression and detection, underscoring the need for place-based social epidemiological considerations in managing chronic hepatitis B. The study used rigorous statistical methods, including propensity score matching, to isolate geographic disparities from private economic circumstances. Chronic hepatitis B remains a major global health concern, contributing to liver cirrhosis and hepatocellular carcinoma, and socioeconomic inequalities can disrupt the trajectory between diagnosis and clinical management.
Original source text (English)
HOME NEWS EXPLORE CAREER Companies Jobs EVENTS iGEM News Team PHOTOS VIDEO WIKI BLOG COMMUNITY FACEBOOK INSTAGRAM TWITTER Friday, November 20, 2026 No Result View All Result Login HOME NEWS EXPLORE CAREER Companies Jobs Lecturer PhD Studentship Postdoc Research Assistant EVENTS iGEM News Team PHOTOS VIDEO WIKI BLOG COMMUNITY FACEBOOK INSTAGRAM TWITTER HOME NEWS EXPLORE CAREER Companies Jobs Lecturer PhD Studentship Postdoc Research Assistant EVENTS iGEM News Team PHOTOS VIDEO WIKI BLOG COMMUNITY FACEBOOK INSTAGRAM TWITTER No Result View All Result No Result View All Result Home NEWS Science News Health Area Disadvantage Outweighs Personal Socioeconomic Status in Chronic Hepatitis B Liver Fibrosis by November 18, 2026 in Health Reading Time: 6 mins read 0 Area Disadvantage Outweighs Personal Socioeconomic Status in Chronic Hepatitis B Liver Fibrosis Share on FacebookShare on TwitterShare on LinkedinShare on RedditShare on Telegram In a nationwide multicentre investigation examining adults living with chronic hepatitis B across twenty-four clinical centres in Türkiye, the severity of histologically staged liver fibrosis was found to correlate far more consistently with area-level socioeconomic disadvantage in a patient’s current place of residence than with individual socioeconomic measures or birthplace background. Chronic viral hepatitis remains a substantial driver of progressive hepatic injury, yet the extent to which broader geographical deprivation shapes the clinical severity at which liver disease is diagnosed has remained insufficiently characterised. By evaluating biopsy-confirmed liver tissue alongside detailed socioeconomic data, investigators identified a pronounced spatial gradient in disease stage that persisted even after rigorous statistical adjustments for demographic profiles, personal income, and healthcare engagement markers. These findings suggest that broader living environments and structural barriers exert a substantial influence on disease detection and progression, underscoring the vital importance of place-based social epidemiological factors in explaining disparities across the chronic hepatitis B continuum of care. Chronic hepatitis B continues to represent a formidable worldwide public health priority, serving as a primary underlying cause of irreversible liver cirrhosis and hepatocellular carcinoma. Global epidemiological assessments from 2022 estimated that 254 million people were living with chronic hepatitis B virus infection, resulting in approximately 1.2 million incident infections and 1.1 million deaths within that year alone. Within this challenging international context, Türkiye represents a clinically relevant setting, where national seroprevalence studies indicate that hepatitis B surface antigen positivity spans between 4.0% and 4.6% of the general population, accompanied by notable geographic and demographic variation. Although clinical protocols emphasise regular surveillance to avert end-stage hepatic sequelae, socioeconomic inequalities can severely disrupt the trajectory between diagnosis and clinical management. Fibrosis severity at presentation effectively functions as an objective biological correlate of delayed care, highlighting that patients who face pronounced social vulnerabilities frequently enter specialized hospital infrastructure only after significant and potentially irreversible structural liver damage has already become established. To investigate these dynamics systematically, the researchers screened 1,259 adult candidates aged 18 years or older who had undergone diagnostic liver biopsy during routine clinical care between 1 January 2019 and 31 December 2024 across 24 participating centres throughout Türkiye. All included individuals presented with chronic infection confirmed by the persistence of hepatitis B surface antigen for at least six months. From this initial group, 147 patients declined participation in structured face-to-face interviews, 134 lacked definitive histopathological biopsy reports, and 22 were excluded because of comorbid conditions known to independently exacerbate liver fibrosis, such as coinfection with hepatitis C virus, hepatitis D virus, or human immunodeficiency virus, chronic alcohol consumption, active malignancy, or hereditary conditions such as Wilson disease or haemochromatosis. The resulting analytic cohort comprised 956 adults with complete biopsy and socioeconomic assessments. Within this cohort, 53.7% were male, the median age was 46.0 years, the median age at diagnosis was 27.0 years, and the median age at biopsy was 41.0 years. Discover more Check Symptoms Study Biology Find Treatments socioeconomic status Socioeconomic Status Assessment of area-level socioeconomic context was grounded in the official İl SEGE-2025 framework, operationalised as the provincial socioeconomic development index across all eighty-one administrative provinces of Türkiye. This framework synthesises 52 objective indicators across eight core dimensions, including demography, employment, education, healthcare infrastructure, innovation, fiscal structure, territorial accessibility, and overall living quality, classifying provinces into six development tiers where level 1 denotes the highest socioeconomic development and level 6 denotes the most severe deprivation. Concurrently, comprehensive personal data were gathered through post-biopsy face-to-face interviews, capturing formal educational attainment, employment status, household occupancy, monthly income, receipt of governmental social assistance, health insurance coverage, and residential stability. In addition, healthcare accessibility was measured directly through reported travel times to secondary or tertiary centres equipped for chronic viral hepatitis management, alongside documented durations of clinical follow-up prior to tissue sampling, providing a robust empirical framework capable of isolating structural geographic disparities from private economic circumstances. Histological evaluation served as the primary clinical outcome, with hepatic fibrosis graded according to the standardized Ishak scoring system spanning from stage 0 through stage 6. Within the full analytic cohort of 956 patients, mild fibrosis corresponding to Ishak stages 0 through 2 was identified in 73.1% of participants, moderate fibrosis covering stages 3 and 4 was documented in 21.7%, and advanced fibrosis or cirrhosis encompassing stages 5 and 6 was found in 5.2%. In primary multivariable ordinal logistic regression across the entire range of fibrosis stages, a one-level decline in provincial socioeconomic development was independently associated with an adjusted odds ratio of 1.97 for reaching higher fibrosis stages (95% confidence interval: 1.82 to 2.15; P < 0.001). Concurrently, older age at biopsy (adjusted odds ratio: 1.12 per 10-year increment; P = 0.019), longer travel time to a specialized care centre (adjusted odds ratio: 1.53 per category increase; P < 0.001), and longer duration of residence in the current locality (adjusted odds ratio: 1.21 per 5-year increase; P = 0.023) independently predicted greater fibrosis severity. To rigorously interrogate potential confounding in comparisons between regions of extreme development, the research team implemented propensity score matching and overlap weighting focused strictly on participants living in tier 1 and tier 6 provinces. In the 1:1 nearest-neighbour matched cohort balanced for age at biopsy and exact sex across 127 paired individuals, participants residing in the most disadvantaged socioeconomic environment exhibited markedly higher fibrosis burdens. Specifically, median Ishak fibrosis scores were 1.0 in tier 1 compared with 3.0 in tier 6, and the prevalence of significant fibrosis, defined as Ishak stage 3 or higher, reached 66.1% in tier 6 compared with merely 7.1% in tier 1. Stratified conditional logistic regression revealed that residence in tier 6 conferred an odds ratio of 26.00 for significant liver fibrosis relative to tier 1 (95% confidence interval: 8.21 to 82.37; P < 0.001). Overlap weighting demonstrated matching stability, producing a weighted significant fibrosis prevalence of 63.6% in tier 6 versus 6.6% in tier 1, corresponding to a weighted odds ratio of 24.75 (95% confidence interval: 8.96 to 68.36; P < 0.001). Sequential sensitivity regression models further reinforced the robustness of these observed associations when introducing additional layers of statistical control. Among participants residing in the extreme provincial tiers, the unadjusted contrast in significant fibrosis yielded an odds ratio of 28.83 after controlling for age and sex, which remained largely unchanged at an odds ratio of 28.17 following the inclusion of individual-level socioeconomic indicators such as household income and schooling. When access-related factors, including travel duration and pre-biopsy surveillance continuity, were incorporated into the model, the odds ratio remained pronounced at 18.98 (95% confidence interval: 8.43 to 42.72; P < 0.001). Crucially, formal interaction tests demonstrated no evidence of effect modification across age (P = 0.425), sex (P = 0.110), or equivalised household income (P = 0.695). Moreover, the relative index of inequality calculated across the ridit-ranked personal income distribution was not statistically associated with significant fibrosis (odds ratio: 0.63, 95% confidence interval: 0.21 to 1.87; P = 0.401), showing that personal financial resources alone did not explain the substantial regional disparity. Model-derived predicted probabilities clearly illustrated the clinical gap separating populations situated at opposite ends of the geographic socioeconomic spectrum. For a 40-year-old male with an average equivalised household income, the model-estimated probability of presenting with significant liver fibrosis was 69.3% (95% confidence interval: 59.8% to 77.4%) in tier 6 provinces, compared with only 7.4% (95% confidence interval: 3.3% to 15.6%) in tier 1 environments. Similarly, when assessing individuals earning a standardized household income of 30,000 Turkish liras monthly at the median cohort age of 41 years, the predicted probability of significant fibrosis stood at 70.3% (95% confidence interval: 61.0% to 78.2%) in the most disadvantaged provinces, while remaining at 8.7% (95% confidence interval: 4.4% to 16.4%) in the most developed provinces. Across both geographical groupings, estimated risks rose steadily with advancing age, yet the trajectories across income brackets ran strictly parallel, demonstrating that elevated income provided little apparent mitigation against the adverse structural conditions present in underdeveloped provinces. Further insight into the dominance of contemporary living conditions over historical origin emerged from reciprocal subgroup evaluations categorising patients by birthplace and migration destination. Among 370 individuals born in the most disadvantaged tier 6 provinces, the prevalence of significant liver fibrosis varied dramatically according to their current place of residence (chi-square P < 0.001). Those who remained in tier 6 exhibited the highest prevalence of significant fibrosis at 72.1%, whereas individuals who had migrated to moderately developed tier 4 or tier 5 provinces showed lower rates of 31.2% and 23.1%, respectively, and those residing in highly developed tier 1, 2, or 3 settings had rates of 8.3%, 7.0%, and 7.9%. Conversely, among participants born in affluent tier 1 provinces, significant fibrosis occurred in only 3.7% of those who remained in tier 1, compared with 55.0% among those who relocated to tier 6. This reciprocal symmetry firmly indicates that cumulative liver injury correlates with current residential conditions rather than early-life regional origin. To synthesize these interconnected dynamics, the researchers developed a conceptual directed acyclic graph illustrating how regional socioeconomic deprivation exerts downstream pressures through extended travel burdens and fragmented clinical surveillance, culminating in advanced histological fibrosis at biopsy. Nevertheless, the authors emphasised important methodological caveats, noting that the cohort was observational, hospital-based, and reliant on routine clinical biopsies rather than a population-wide screening protocol. Central pathology re-review was unavailable, and interviews were conducted after biopsy with a median interval of 60 months, meaning personal variables captured circumstances at interview. Detailed viral loads, antiviral treatment regimens, and metabolic comorbidities were also unmeasured in these models. Despite these limitations, the nationwide evidence demonstrates that structural geographic disadvantage, rather than personal socioeconomic status alone, remains intimately tied to advanced liver fibrosis in chronic hepatitis B, highlighting the imperative for targeted public health investments that expand specialist clinical access and follow-up continuity in underserved regions. 研究表明东亚人乙型肝炎感染与肺癌风险升高有关 - Bioengineer.org

Study Links Hepatitis B Infection to Elevated Lung Cancer Risk in East Asians - Bioengineer.org
新闻简报A study published on November 19, 2026, by Bioengineer.org found a significant association between hepatitis B virus infection and an increased risk of lung cancer, particularly in East Asian populations. The research integrated epidemiological data, genetic analysis, and transcriptomics to explore potential causal mechanisms and shared molecular pathways. The pooled relative effect of hepatitis B infection on lung cancer risk was 1.18 (95% CI 1.07 to 1.30, p-value 0.003), with a stronger correlation observed in East Asian cohorts (1.19, 95% CI 1.10 to 1.29, p-value 0.001). Mendelian randomization analyses further supported this link, showing an odds ratio of 1.226 (95% CI 1.152 to 1.305, p-value < 0.0001) for East Asian populations. The association remained significant even after adjusting for tobacco smoking and other respiratory risk factors. However, no such association was found in European or U.S. populations, indicating geographic variation. The study identified three genes—PTTG1, DTL, and TPX2—as potential cross-disease expression signatures, involved in cell-cycle regulation and proliferative processes. These findings contribute to understanding the epidemiological and genetic links between hepatitis B infection and lung cancer, though they do not establish causality or suggest immediate public health interventions.
查看新闻五要素
时间
发布日期:2026年11月19日。事件日期:提供的证据中未报告。时间范围:提供的证据中未报告。
地点与对象
东亚人口。提供的证据中没有报告具体国家或地区。
事件
一项研究发现 乙型肝炎病毒 感染与肺癌风险增加之间存在显着关联,其中在东亚人群中观察到的相关性最强。该研究由 Bioengineer.org 报道。
规模、影响与风险
乙型肝炎病毒 感染对肺癌风险的汇总相对效应为 1.18(95% CI 1.07 至 1.30,p 值 0.003)。在东亚人群中,汇总相对效应为 1.19(95% CI 1.10 至 1.29,p 值 0.001)。调整混杂因素后,东亚人群肺癌的优势比为 1.226(95% CI 1.152 至 1.305,p 值 < 0.0001)。在多变量孟德尔随机化分析中,优势比仍保持在 1.131 的较高水平(95% CI 1.077 至 1.188,p 值 < 0.001)。
应对、状态与不确定性
该研究进行了荟萃分析、孟德尔随机化和转录组分析来评估这种关联。多种方法的研究结果都很可靠,但该研究没有提供有关具体公共卫生应对措施或干预措施的信息。

Study Links Hepatitis B Infection to Elevated Lung Cancer Risk in East Asians - Bioengineer.org

News Brief
A study published on November 19, 2026, by Bioengineer.org found a significant association between hepatitis B virus infection and an increased risk of lung cancer, particularly in East Asian populations. The research integrated epidemiological data, genetic analysis, and transcriptomics to explore potential causal mechanisms and shared molecular pathways. The pooled relative effect of hepatitis B infection on lung cancer risk was 1.18 (95% CI 1.07 to 1.30, p-value 0.003), with a stronger correlation observed in East Asian cohorts (1.19, 95% CI 1.10 to 1.29, p-value 0.001). Mendelian randomization analyses further supported this link, showing an odds ratio of 1.226 (95% CI 1.152 to 1.305, p-value < 0.0001) for East Asian populations. The association remained significant even after adjusting for tobacco smoking and other respiratory risk factors. However, no such association was found in European or U.S. populations, indicating geographic variation. The study identified three genes—PTTG1, DTL, and TPX2—as potential cross-disease expression signatures, involved in cell-cycle regulation and proliferative processes. HOME NEWS EXPLORE CAREER Companies Jobs EVENTS iGEM News Team PHOTOS VIDEO WIKI BLOG COMMUNITY FACEBOOK INSTAGRAM TWITTER Friday, November 20, 2026 No Result View All Result Login HOME NEWS EXPLORE CAREER Companies Jobs Lecturer PhD Studentship Postdoc Research Assistant EVENTS iGEM News Team PHOTOS VIDEO WIKI BLOG COMMUNITY FACEBOOK INSTAGRAM TWITTER HOME NEWS EXPLORE CAREER Companies Jobs Lecturer PhD Studentship Postdoc Research Assistant EVENTS iGEM News Team PHOTOS VIDEO WIKI BLOG COMMUNITY FACEBOOK INSTAGRAM TWITTER No Result View All Result No Result View All Result Home NEWS Science News Cancer Study Links Hepatitis B Infection to Elevated Lung Cancer Risk in East Asians by November 19, 2026 in Cancer Reading Time: 3 mins read 0 Study Links Hepatitis B Infection to Elevated Lung Cancer Risk in East Asians Share on FacebookShare on TwitterShare on LinkedinShare on RedditShare on Telegram A comprehensive study integrating epidemiological data, genetic analysis, and transcriptomics has identified a significant association between hepatitis B virus infection and an increased risk of lung cancer, with the strongest correlation observed among East Asian populations. The research, which brought together multiple lines of evidence, sought to clarify potential causal mechanisms and common molecular pathways linking the viral infection with pulmonary malignancy. To investigate the epidemiological relationship between the conditions, investigators conducted a systematic review and meta-analysis pooling eleven independent effect estimates drawn from ten published studies. Across the combined cohort, individuals with hepatitis B virus infection exhibited a modest positive association with lung cancer, corresponding to a pooled relative effect of 1.18 with a 95 percent confidence interval spanning 1.07 to 1.30 and a p-value of 0.003. Geographic subgroup analyses revealed notable regional variation in this epidemiological pattern. In cohorts evaluating East Asian populations, the pooled relative effect stood at 1.19 with a 95 percent confidence interval ranging from 1.10 to 1.29 and a p-value of 0.001. Conversely, the meta-analysis found no statistically significant epidemiological association between viral infection and lung cancer within the European and United States subgroup. Discover more Research assistant services Postdoc opportunities Research report templates Learn Engineering Track Biotech To assess potential causality and determine the directional nature of the connection, the researchers performed two-sample Mendelian randomization analyses using genome-wide association study datasets from both East Asian and European populations. The investigative protocol incorporated reverse and multivariable models to examine whether genetically predicted susceptibility to infection independently influenced cancer risk while accounting for major confounding factors. In the primary inverse-variance weighted Mendelian randomization analysis of East Asian datasets, genetically predicted susceptibility to hepatitis B virus infection was linked to a higher risk of developing lung cancer, yielding an odds ratio of 1.226 with a 95 percent confidence interval of 1.152 to 1.305 and a p-value below 0.0001. No corresponding genetic association emerged when evaluating the European datasets. The observed association in East Asian populations remained robust even after statistical adjustment for established respiratory risk factors. In multivariable Mendelian randomization accounting for tobacco smoking and major pulmonary comorbidities, the odds ratio for lung cancer remained elevated at 1.131, supported by a 95 percent confidence interval of 1.077 to 1.188 and a p-value below 0.001. To explore biological features that might be shared between hepatitis B infection and pulmonary tumorigenesis, researchers evaluated transcriptomic expression profiles using differential expression analysis, weighted gene co-expression network analysis, functional enrichment methods, and machine-learning algorithms. These computational workflows identified three specific genes—PTTG1, DTL, and TPX2—as candidate cross-disease expression signatures. Discover more Tour Colleges Cancer research funding Donate to Research Find Conferences Health Experimental transcriptomic data demonstrated that PTTG1, DTL, and TPX2 were consistently upregulated in datasets representing both hepatitis B infection and lung cancer. Functional profiling indicated that these three candidate signature genes are primarily involved in cell-cycle regulation and proliferative biological processes, offering preliminary molecular targets for future research into how viral infection and lung cancer pathogenesis interact. Taken together, the researchers concluded that the study provides epidemiological and complementary genetic evidence supporting an association between hepatitis B virus infection susceptibility and lung cancer risk, with the clearest evidence observed in East Asian populations. This distinction between geographic cohorts was evident across both investigative methodologies. While East Asian datasets yielded statistically significant positive associations in observational pooling and two-sample Mendelian randomization, no statistically significant association was observed in the European and United States meta-analysis subgroup, and no corresponding genetic association was observed in European datasets. These integrated findings help address key questions in cancer epidemiology, where prior studies had suggested a link between hepatitis B virus infection and lung cancer but left the potential causal relationship and shared molecular features unclear. By conducting reverse and multivariable analyses alongside primary Mendelian randomization models, the investigators assessed both the direction and robustness of the connection. Furthermore, the authors noted that the exploratory transcriptomic and machine-learning analyses identified overlapping molecular signatures predominantly tied to cell-cycle regulation and proliferative processes, establishing candidate directions for further investigation into the biological relationship between hepatitis B virus infection and lung cancer. 简化的 GADA 模型可准确检测乙型肝炎相关肝癌 - Bioengineer.org

Simplified GADA Model Accurately Detects Hepatitis B Related Liver Cancer - Bioengineer.org
新闻简报Bioengineer.org 于 2026 年 11 月 19 日发表的一项研究强调了 GADA 模型的开发,这是一种简化的四变量诊断工具,用于检测 慢性乙型肝炎 病毒感染患者的肝细胞癌。该模型由北京大学和合作临床中心的研究人员创建,整合了患者性别、年龄、脱γ-羧基凝血酶原和甲胎蛋白。它经过多个阶段的验证,包括 1,533 名参与者的建模 队列研究 和 657 名参与者的内部验证组,总体研究人群为 23,098 人。GADA 模型表现出很强的诊断准确性,建模阶段的曲线下面积 (AUC) 为 0.940,内部验证为 0.935,外部验证为 0.919。它对检测肝癌表现出较高的敏感性(82.57%至86.00%)和特异性(91.53%至81.88%)。研究指出,肝细胞癌是全球一项重大健康挑战,其中 慢性乙型肝炎 病例占全球病例的 40% 以上,在中国占 86%。GADA 模型旨在作为支持现有 监测 途径的临床辅助手段,而不是成像或多学科评估的替代品,有待进一步的试验和校准。

时间
该研究发表于 2026 年 11 月 19 日,证据中没有明确提及事件日期。该模型在多个阶段进行了验证,包括建模 队列研究 和内部/外部验证组。
地点与对象
该研究在中国各地进行,涉及 23,098 名个体的集体研究人群,其中包括 2,190 名患有 慢性乙型肝炎 相关肝病的成年患者和 20,908 名外部验证研究参与者。该人群主要由感染 慢性乙型肝炎 的个体组成。
事件
一项多中心研究验证了 GADA 模型,这是一种简化的四变量统计工具,旨在检测 慢性乙型肝炎 病毒感染患者的肝细胞癌。该模型由北京大学和合作临床中心的研究人员开发。
规模、影响与风险
GADA 模型表现出很强的判别性能,建模 队列研究 中的曲线下面积 (AUC) 为 0.940,内部验证数据集中为 0.935,外部验证 队列研究 中为 0.919。它对检测肝癌表现出较高的敏感性(82.57%至86.00%)和特异性(91.53%至81.88%)。该研究强调了肝细胞癌对全球健康带来的重大挑战,特别是在乙型肝炎高负担人群中。
应对、状态与不确定性
GADA 模型是作为乙型肝炎相关肝癌的诊断工具而开发和验证的。它作为临床辅助手段而不是影像学或多学科评估的替代品。该研究强调需要进行前瞻性实施试验和跨实验室校准以进行全面验证。
B routine priorityPublic-health news

Simplified GADA Model Accurately Detects Hepatitis B Related Liver Cancer - Bioengineer.org

News Brief
A study published on November 19, 2026, by Bioengineer.org highlights the development of the GADA model, a simplified four-variable diagnostic tool for detecting hepatocellular carcinoma in patients with chronic hepatitis B virus infection. The model, created by researchers from Peking University and collaborating clinical centers, integrates patient sex, age, des-gamma-carboxy prothrombin, and alpha-fetoprotein. It was validated across multiple phases, including a modeling cohort of 1,533 participants and an internal validation group of 657 individuals, with an overall study population of 23,098. The GADA model demonstrated strong diagnostic accuracy, achieving an area under the curve (AUC) of 0.940 in the modeling phase, 0.935 in internal validation, and 0.919 in external validation. It showed high sensitivity (82.57% to 86.00%) and specificity (91.53% to 81.88%) for detecting liver cancer. The study notes that hepatocellular carcinoma is a major global health challenge, with chronic hepatitis B responsible for over 40% of cases worldwide and 86% in China. The GADA model is intended as a clinical adjunct to support existing surveillance pathways, rather than a substitute for imaging or multidisciplinary evaluation, pending further trials and calibration.
Original source text (English)
HOME NEWS EXPLORE CAREER Companies Jobs EVENTS iGEM News Team PHOTOS VIDEO WIKI BLOG COMMUNITY FACEBOOK INSTAGRAM TWITTER Friday, November 20, 2026 No Result View All Result Login HOME NEWS EXPLORE CAREER Companies Jobs Lecturer PhD Studentship Postdoc Research Assistant EVENTS iGEM News Team PHOTOS VIDEO WIKI BLOG COMMUNITY FACEBOOK INSTAGRAM TWITTER HOME NEWS EXPLORE CAREER Companies Jobs Lecturer PhD Studentship Postdoc Research Assistant EVENTS iGEM News Team PHOTOS VIDEO WIKI BLOG COMMUNITY FACEBOOK INSTAGRAM TWITTER No Result View All Result No Result View All Result Home NEWS Science News Cancer Simplified GADA Model Accurately Detects Hepatitis B Related Liver Cancer by November 19, 2026 in Cancer, Health Reading Time: 3 mins read 0 Simplified GADA Model Accurately Detects Hepatitis B Related Liver Cancer Share on FacebookShare on TwitterShare on LinkedinShare on RedditShare on Telegram A multicenter investigation across China has validated GADA, a simplified four-variable statistical model designed to identify hepatocellular carcinoma in patients with chronic hepatitis B virus infection. Developed by researchers from Peking University and collaborating clinical centers, the diagnostic tool integrates patient sex, age, des-gamma-carboxy prothrombin, and alpha-fetoprotein. Across a collective study population exceeding 23,000 individuals, the scoring system demonstrated strong discriminative performance for both overall liver cancer and early-stage disease, offering an alternative surveillance framework tailored specifically to populations with high burdens of viral hepatitis. Hepatocellular carcinoma represents a major global health challenge, with chronic hepatitis B infection responsible for more than 40 percent of cases worldwide and approximately 86 percent of cases diagnosed in China. This epidemiological pattern contrasts sharply with many Western clinical populations, where liver cancer arises more frequently from chronic hepatitis C infection, alcohol-related liver damage, and metabolic dysfunction. While the previously established GALAD algorithm integrates sex, age, alpha-fetoprotein, lens culinaris agglutinin-reactive alpha-fetoprotein, and des-gamma-carboxy prothrombin to detect liver tumors, its accuracy required dedicated re-evaluation in cohorts dominated by chronic viral hepatitis. The inclusion of lens culinaris agglutinin-reactive alpha-fetoprotein, known as AFP-L3%, introduces practical hurdles in routine healthcare environments. Measuring AFP-L3% requires specialized laboratory platforms, increases operational testing expenses, necessitates larger biological sample volumes, and remains unavailable across many secondary medical facilities. During the initial development phase, the investigative team determined that alpha-fetoprotein and des-gamma-carboxy prothrombin contributed robust and complementary diagnostic signals, whereas measuring AFP-L3% yielded minimal incremental clinical benefit among individuals with hepatitis B-related liver pathology. Discover more Research assistant services Postdoc opportunities Research report templates Learn Engineering Track Biotech To construct the streamlined diagnostic tool, investigators analyzed 2,190 adult patients presenting with chronic hepatitis B-related liver disease at Mengchao Hepatobiliary Hospital of Fujian Medical University. This cohort was randomly divided into a modeling dataset comprising 1,533 participants and an internal validation group containing 657 individuals. The resulting GADA mathematical formula calculates a predictive risk score by combining numerical coefficients: Z equals negative 11.111, plus 1.122 multiplied by sex coded as one for male or zero for female, plus 0.073 multiplied by age, plus 2.660 multiplied by the common logarithm of des-gamma-carboxy prothrombin, plus 0.578 multiplied by the common logarithm of alpha-fetoprotein. To rigorously verify model generalizability, researchers conducted a prospective external validation study enrolling 20,908 participants across ten medical centers throughout China. This external evaluation group included 905 patients diagnosed with new primary liver cancer alongside 20,003 control participants without malignancies, consisting of 17,802 individuals diagnosed with chronic hepatitis B and 2,201 patients with confirmed hepatitis B-related cirrhosis. Combining the initial developmental cohorts and the multi-hospital prospective trial yielded an overall evaluated study cohort of 23,098 individuals. Statistical evaluation demonstrated consistent diagnostic accuracy across all study phases. In the modeling cohort, the GADA tool achieved an area under the receiver operating characteristic curve of 0.940 with a 95 percent confidence interval spanning 0.927 to 0.952, yielding 82.57 percent sensitivity and 91.53 percent specificity. Within the internal validation dataset, the model produced an area under the curve of 0.935, demonstrating 82.04 percent sensitivity and 92.65 percent specificity. In the large prospective external validation cohort, GADA maintained an area under the curve of 0.919, achieving 86.00 percent sensitivity and 81.88 percent specificity. Comparative analyses revealed that the streamlined four-variable equation consistently outperformed individual standard blood tests and older algorithms. In the developmental cohort, GADA surpassed the five-variable GALAD model, which registered an area under the curve of 0.907. Across the external validation network, GADA demonstrated superior diagnostic capacity compared to alpha-fetoprotein alone, which achieved an area under the curve of 0.748, as well as des-gamma-carboxy prothrombin alone, which registered an area under the curve of 0.841. Across individual participating hospitals, center-specific performance metrics remained stable, with areas under the curve ranging from 0.895 to 0.977 across diverse clinical settings. Discover more Tour Colleges Cancer research funding Donate to Research Find Conferences Get Health Tips The scoring system also maintained discriminative power when evaluating early-stage malignancy restricted to the Milan criteria. For these early-stage lesions, GADA yielded areas under the curve of 0.858 in the derivation cohort, 0.868 in the internal validation cohort, and 0.903 in the prospective multicenter group. Among early-stage cases in the external validation cohort, the formula identified 88.16 percent of tumors while maintaining a specificity of 74.50 percent, indicating dependable capacity to detect nascent neoplasms before advanced progression. Investigators noted that GADA was engineered specifically to address clinical demands in settings where differentiating hepatocellular carcinoma from chronic hepatitis and cirrhosis presents substantial diagnostic difficulty. Omitting the complex AFP-L3% biomarker enables broader deployment in regional clinical laboratories with limited analytical infrastructure. However, the study authors emphasize that the algorithm is intended as a clinical adjunct to support established surveillance pathways, rather than an outright substitute for abdominal imaging or multidisciplinary medical evaluation, pending prospective implementation trials and cross-laboratory assay calibration. 短期类固醇与改善早期乙型肝炎肝衰竭结果有关 - Bioengineer.org

Short-Course Steroids Linked to Improved Early Hepatitis B Liver Failure Outcomes - Bioengineer.org
新闻简报中国南充川北医学院附属医院进行的一项为期十年的回顾性 队列研究 研究发现,与单独接受标准治疗的患者相比,早期 乙型肝炎病毒 相关肝衰竭住院患者接受短程静脉注射甲基强的松龙联合常规支持治疗后,4 周临床康复率显着更高,并发症发生率更低。该研究评估了 143 名患者,其中 43 名患者接受辅助糖皮质激素 治疗,100 名患者接受常规护理。所有参与者均接受基础 抗病毒药物 治疗和肝脏保护药物。研究表明,及时的免疫抑制可能会阻止肝细胞的快速破坏并支持组织再生。然而,该研究还指出了对继发感染和病毒再激活的担忧。研究结果基于医疗记录,并根据年龄、性别、基线凝血、胆红素和病毒载量进行了调整。尽管结果令人鼓舞,但该研究承认由于十年入组期间临床指南和医生偏好的变化而导致时间混杂等局限性。
查看新闻五要素
时间
该研究从2015年1月到2025年12月进行了十年,发布日期为2026年11月17日。
地点与对象
该研究在中国南充川北医学院附属医院进行,涉及 143 名 乙型肝炎病毒 相关肝衰竭患者。
事件
一项回顾性 队列研究 研究评估了短程静脉注射甲泼尼龙对早期 乙型肝炎病毒 相关肝衰竭结局的影响,并将其与传统支持治疗进行比较。研究发现,糖皮质激素组的四周临床康复率明显更高。
规模、影响与风险
该研究涉及 143 名患者,其中 43 名患者接受辅助糖皮质激素 治疗,100 名患者接受常规治疗。该研究强调了短程类固醇在提高康复率和减少早期乙型肝炎肝衰竭并发症方面的潜力,尽管它承认对继发感染和病毒再激活的担忧。
应对、状态与不确定性
该研究是一项回顾性 队列研究 分析,其结果基于医疗记录。它应用了中国国家指南定义的诊断标准,并针对多个变量进行了调整。然而,该研究承认由于不断发展的临床实践而存在诸如时间混杂等局限性。
B routine priorityPublic-health news

Short-Course Steroids Linked to Improved Early Hepatitis B Liver Failure Outcomes - Bioengineer.org

News Brief
A ten-year retrospective cohort study conducted at the Affiliated Hospital of North Sichuan Medical College in Nanchong, China, found that hospitalized patients with early hepatitis B virus-related liver failure who received short-course intravenous methylprednisolone alongside conventional supportive care had significantly higher rates of four-week clinical recovery and lower complication rates compared to those treated with standard therapy alone. The study evaluated 143 patients, with 43 receiving adjunctive glucocorticoid treatment and 100 receiving conventional care. All participants received foundational antiviral therapy and liver-protective medications. The research suggests that timely immunosuppression may halt rapid hepatocyte destruction and support tissue regeneration. However, the study also notes concerns about secondary infections and viral reactivation. The findings were based on medical records and adjusted for age, sex, baseline coagulation, bilirubin, and viral load. Despite the promising results, the study acknowledges limitations such as temporal confounding due to changing clinical guidelines and physician preferences over the ten-year enrollment period.
Original source text (English)
HOME NEWS EXPLORE CAREER Companies Jobs EVENTS iGEM News Team PHOTOS VIDEO WIKI BLOG COMMUNITY FACEBOOK INSTAGRAM TWITTER Friday, November 20, 2026 No Result View All Result Login HOME NEWS EXPLORE CAREER Companies Jobs Lecturer PhD Studentship Postdoc Research Assistant EVENTS iGEM News Team PHOTOS VIDEO WIKI BLOG COMMUNITY FACEBOOK INSTAGRAM TWITTER HOME NEWS EXPLORE CAREER Companies Jobs Lecturer PhD Studentship Postdoc Research Assistant EVENTS iGEM News Team PHOTOS VIDEO WIKI BLOG COMMUNITY FACEBOOK INSTAGRAM TWITTER No Result View All Result No Result View All Result Home NEWS Science News Health Short-Course Steroids Linked to Improved Early Hepatitis B Liver Failure Outcomes by November 17, 2026 in Health Reading Time: 7 mins read 0 Short-Course Steroids Linked to Improved Early Hepatitis B Liver Failure Outcomes Share on FacebookShare on TwitterShare on LinkedinShare on RedditShare on Telegram Hospitalized patients presenting with early- or pre-stage hepatitis B virus-related liver failure who received short-course intravenous methylprednisolone alongside conventional supportive care experienced significantly higher rates of four-week clinical recovery and lower overall complication rates than those treated with standard therapy alone, according to a ten-year retrospective cohort study conducted at the Affiliated Hospital of North Sichuan Medical College in Nanchong, China. The investigation evaluated medical records from 143 qualifying patients treated between January 2015 and December 2025, comparing 43 individuals who received adjunctive glucocorticoid treatment against a control cohort of 100 individuals managed exclusively with conventional medical regimens. Across the entire cohort, all participants received foundational antiviral coverage alongside liver-protective medications and symptom-directed inpatient management, providing a standardized baseline from which researchers could assess the physiological and prognostic associations of temporary corticosteroid administration during the critical initial phases of hepatic decompensation. The clinical rationale driving the study centers on the complex immunopathogenesis of hepatitis B virus-induced liver failure, which remains the leading etiology of acute hepatic collapse across China and continues to carry severe short-term mortality risks. In the earliest phases of acute deterioration, excessive immune activation and severe inflammatory cascades trigger widespread hepatocyte necrosis even while patients retain a degree of baseline synthetic functional reserve. Because this early window represents a potentially reversible interval, clinicians have long theorized that timely immunosuppression could halt rapid hepatocyte destruction and create permissive conditions for tissue regeneration. Glucocorticoids have been utilized in liver failure management for more than five decades due to their potent anti-inflammatory properties; however, their clinical adoption has remained fiercely debated because of persistent concerns regarding secondary bacterial and fungal infections, gastrointestinal bleeding, and the potential reactivation of viral replication. To rigorously examine these dynamics, the investigators applied diagnostic criteria defined by the Chinese national guidelines for the diagnosis and treatment of liver failure. Eligible individuals met specific pre-stage or early-stage diagnostic benchmarks, which required severe systemic fatigue accompanied by marked gastrointestinal manifestations, significantly elevated transaminase levels, and progressive jaundice characterized by a daily total bilirubin increase of at least 17.1 micromoles per liter or absolute concentrations between 85.5 and 171 micromoles per liter. Coagulation parameters formed a critical threshold, requiring prothrombin time activity between 40 percent and 50 percent for pre-stage disease, or between 30 percent and 40 percent for early-stage disease, in the complete absence of preexisting extrahepatic organ failure. Patients presenting with confounding hepatic insults, including viral co-infections, alcoholic liver injury, drug-induced toxicity, or autoimmune disease, were systematically excluded, as were those with significant cardiopulmonary or renal comorbidities, malignancies, or previous exposure to artificial liver support and immunosuppressants. Discover more Study Biology Get Fit Guides Steroids steroids steroid Patients assigned to the conventional control arm received nucleos(t)ide analogue antiviral therapy consisting of daily entecavir at 0.5 milligrams, tenofovir disoproxil fumarate at 300 milligrams, or tenofovir alafenamide at 25 milligrams, paired with hepatoprotective agents, albumin and fresh frozen plasma infusions, and targeted complication prophylaxis. Patients in the glucocorticoid arm received identical antiviral and supportive regimens with the addition of intravenous methylprednisolone at doses ranging between 40 and 80 milligrams per day for three to seven days, followed by individual tapering or discontinuation based on clinical response, with total exposure restricted to two weeks or less. Attending clinicians selected initial methylprednisolone dosing based on baseline inflammatory activity and disease severity rather than body weight, administering 40 milligrams daily for milder inflammation and 80 milligrams daily for pronounced inflammation, while co-administering proton pump inhibitors to mitigate upper gastrointestinal ulceration and bleeding risks. The primary short-term endpoint was four-week clinical improvement, evaluated as a prespecified composite measure that required the concurrent fulfillment of three strict clinical and biochemical criteria: marked symptom alleviation with complete resolution of hepatic encephalopathy based on the West Haven grading system, clinically documented resolution of ascites and jaundice, and central laboratory verification of total bilirubin falling to five times the upper limit of normal or lower alongside prothrombin time activity reaching at least 40 percent in early-stage patients or 50 percent in pre-stage patients. At the end of four weeks, 81.4 percent of patients in the glucocorticoid group achieved this composite threshold compared to 54.0 percent in the conventional group, representing a statistically significant difference. In multivariable logistic regression adjusted for age, sex, baseline coagulation, bilirubin, and viral load, glucocorticoid therapy remained an independent predictor of recovery with an odds ratio of 4.21, while baseline prothrombin time activity was also independently protective with an odds ratio of 1.04 per one percent increase. Discover more Check Symptoms Soothe Digestion Save on Rx To capture continuous physiological trajectories during the first month of hospitalization, the researchers utilized linear mixed-effects models evaluating longitudinal measurements of total bilirubin, prothrombin time activity, and serum albumin adjusted for age, sex, and baseline values. For total bilirubin, downward trajectories were observed across both groups, and while the interaction between treatment group and time did not reach statistical significance, adjusted total bilirubin concentrations remained lower overall in the methylprednisolone cohort across the monitoring period, displaying the widest between-group margin at week one before gradually converging by week four. Prothrombin time activity demonstrated steady upward recovery in both study cohorts, but after adjusting for baseline values, the longitudinal trajectories showed no statistically significant differences between treatments, indicating that apparent unadjusted differences in coagulation recovery were primarily attributable to baseline imbalances existing prior to treatment initiation. Longitudinal serum albumin measurements displayed a distinct pattern, generating a statistically significant treatment-by-time interaction that remained robust after Benjamini-Hochberg multiplicity correction. Whereas albumin concentrations increased steadily over time in the conventional group at an estimated rate of 0.91 grams per liter per week, levels in the glucocorticoid group remained essentially unchanged, registering a minor slope of negative 0.14 grams per liter per week. Adjusted marginal means indicated that the methylprednisolone group maintained significantly higher circulating albumin concentrations during the first two weeks of treatment, but this gap closed entirely by weeks three and four. The researchers noted that these albumin trends reflected complex interplays involving exogenous plasma and albumin infusions, baseline nutritional reserves, systemic inflammation, and albumin’s prolonged biological half-life, cautioning against viewing early albumin dynamics as direct reflections of intrinsic liver synthetic recovery. Analysis of covariance models applied to week-four outcomes satisfied statistical assumptions for prothrombin time activity and albumin, showing no statistically significant differences in adjusted estimated marginal means between the two treatment groups. However, total bilirubin failed residual normality and parallel slope assumptions even following logarithmic transformation, prompting researchers to deploy the Johnson-Neyman technique to identify baseline-dependent response thresholds. This advanced analysis revealed that the therapeutic association of methylprednisolone was highly dependent on initial bilirubin severity: among patients entering the hospital with baseline total bilirubin below 258 micromoles per liter, methylprednisolone was associated with significantly lower week-four bilirubin levels, with greater relative benefits observed at lower baseline values. In contrast, between 258 and 396 micromoles per liter, between-group differences ceased to be statistically significant, and above 396 micromoles per liter, glucocorticoid administration was associated with significantly higher week-four bilirubin concentrations, a baseline-dependent interaction that remained robust in Cook’s distance sensitivity testing. Twelve-week survival metrics showed that 90.7 percent of glucocorticoid-treated patients achieved sustained clinical improvement compared to 66.0 percent in the conventional cohort, with loss to follow-up rates matching exactly at 7.0 percent across both arms. Unadjusted mortality was substantially lower in the glucocorticoid arm at 2.3 percent, representing a single fatal outcome, versus 27.0 percent, or 27 deaths, among conventional therapy recipients. Because the sparse number of deaths in the steroid cohort introduced significant risks of model separation and small-sample distortion, investigators utilized Firth’s penalized maximum likelihood estimation. In multinomial regression models setting clinical improvement as the reference category, higher baseline prothrombin time activity and serum albumin were significantly associated with decreased mortality risk, whereas glucocorticoid therapy did not reach statistical significance; however, binary sensitivity analyses modeling death versus improvement showed glucocorticoids were associated with a significantly reduced risk of death, yielding an odds ratio of 0.074. Safety evaluations demonstrated that short-course methylprednisolone did not increase the incidence of adverse clinical events, as overall complications occurred in 37.2 percent of the steroid cohort compared to 62.0 percent of the conventional group. Infectious complications developed in 25.6 percent of patients receiving glucocorticoids and 40.0 percent of conventional controls, a difference that was not statistically significant. Specifically, documented infections in the steroid group included six cases of spontaneous bacterial peritonitis, two intestinal infections, two urinary tract infections, and one case of oral candidiasis, while the conventional arm recorded twenty-one cases of peritonitis, six cases of pneumonia, five bronchitis cases, five intestinal infections, and three urinary tract infections. Rates of secondary upper gastrointestinal bleeding, hepatic encephalopathy, and hepatorenal syndrome were similarly comparable between the two arms, suggesting that early anti-inflammatory therapy may potentially prevent clinical deterioration toward complication-prone end-stage disease. Virological monitoring confirmed that concurrent nucleos(t)ide analogue administration effectively prevented glucocorticoid-induced viral rebound during the observation window. Real-time fluorescence quantitative polymerase chain reaction assays revealed no statistically significant differences between the two treatment arms in baseline viral loads, fourth-week viral concentrations, or overall logarithmic reductions from baseline. At week four, serum HBV-DNA negative conversion rates, defined as viral loads dropping below the assay’s lower limit of detection of 500 copies per milliliter, stood at 69.8 percent in the glucocorticoid group and 76.0 percent in the conventional group, demonstrating that short-term immunosuppression did not compromise the efficacy of antiviral replication inhibition. However, the study authors emphasized that virological follow-up was restricted to four weeks, leaving the potential risk of delayed viral reactivation following steroid withdrawal unmeasured over longer intervals. The authors underscored several methodological constraints inherent to the study, foremost being its retrospective, single-center design and non-randomized allocation, which introduces potential confounding by indication because clinicians may have selectively offered steroids to patients deemed more likely to benefit. Furthermore, the glucocorticoid cohort was relatively modest in size, and baseline prothrombin time activity was significantly higher among steroid recipients, indicating an underlying baseline imbalance that statistical adjustments cannot completely eliminate. The ten-year enrollment window also introduced temporal confounding stemming from evolving clinical guidelines, changing physician preferences, and shifting supportive care technologies. Consequently, the authors concluded that these observational associations do not establish a definitive causal survival benefit, highlighting an urgent need for prospective, multicenter randomized controlled trials to confirm efficacy and validate the baseline bilirubin thresholds that govern patient selection. Time
The study was conducted over a ten-year period from January 2015 to December 2025, with the publication date being November 17, 2026.
Location and population
The study was conducted at the Affiliated Hospital of North Sichuan Medical College in Nanchong, China, involving 143 patients with hepatitis B virus-related liver failure.
Event
A retrospective cohort study evaluated the impact of short-course intravenous methylprednisolone on early hepatitis B virus-related liver failure outcomes, comparing it with conventional supportive care. The study found significantly higher rates of four-week clinical recovery in the glucocorticoid group.
Scale, impact and risk
The study involved 143 patients, with 43 receiving adjunctive glucocorticoid treatment and 100 receiving conventional therapy. The research highlights the potential of short-course steroids to improve recovery rates and reduce complications in early hepatitis B liver failure, though it acknowledges concerns about secondary infections and viral reactivation.
Response, status and uncertainty
The study was a retrospective cohort analysis, with findings based on medical records. It applied diagnostic criteria defined by Chinese national guidelines and adjusted for multiple variables. However, the study acknowledges limitations such as temporal confounding due to evolving clinical practices.

中国乙肝新药市场大,实际销售前景较小_城市新闻网

China Market for New Hepatitis B Drugs Is Large, Actual Sales Prospects Smaller - www.citynewsservice.cn
新闻简报文章于2026年11月16日发表,讨论了将葛兰素史克公司的乙肝药物贝匹妥单抗(Hibsago)引入中国市场所面临的挑战。尽管中国有大量乙肝患者,约75 million人患有慢性乙肝,约六百万人在接受治疗,但由于严格的适应症要求,该药物的实际市场可能较小。在美国,该药物于2026年10月获批,允许部分患者在病毒水平被抑制且表面抗原水平低于3000国际单位每毫升时停药。然而,在试验中仅有24%的患者达到这些标准,19%的患者在24周后实现\"功能性治愈\"。该药物带有严重的肝反应和血小板减少的黑框警告。葛兰素史克已与智飞生物合作在中国分销该药物,但文章指出,该药物的经济因素,包括高昂的成本和报销困难,可能会限制其推广。中国监管决策预计将在2027年上半年做出,文章强调在该药物广泛使用之前需要进一步研究和成本考量。
